<!DOCTYPE html>  EXTREMO DE DETALLHE CHROMATICO VIVO CENTILANTE
<html lang="es" data-theme="dark">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>BELENTANI — Galería Oficial de Activos · Instituto Universal</title>
<meta name="description" content="BELENTANI Design System v1.0 — tokens, glass, iconos, logos, fondos, hero y certificado. AGPLv3. 2026-09-03.">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-600-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-700-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-600-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<style>
/* ============================================================
   BELENTANI THEME — belentani-theme.css (v1.0.0 · AGPLv3 · 2026-09-03)
   Teoría: 60-30-10 · WCAG 2.1 AA (≥4.5:1) · glassmorfismo
   ============================================================ */
:root{
  /* 60% superficie */
  --bel-bg:#0b1322; --bel-paper:#f4f7fb; --bel-ink:#0d1626;
  --bel-text:#e8f1ff; --bel-muted:#9fb3c8;
  /* 30% tecnología */
  --bel-cyan:#38e1ff; --bel-mint:#4ef0b0;
  /* 10% acción */
  --bel-lime:#b6ff2e; --bel-lime-ink:#0d1626;
  /* apoyo */
  --bel-coral:#ff6b5e; --bel-saffron:#ffb52e;
  /* glass */
  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(13,22,38,.55);
  --bel-glass-border:rgba(56,225,255,.18);
  --bel-glass-blur:18px;
  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
  /* forma */
  --radius-sm:10px; --radius:18px; --radius-lg:28px; --radius-pill:99px;
  /* tipografía */
  --font-display:"Sora",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --font-sans:"Inter",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --font-mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace;
  /* acentos legibles por tema */
  --acc-cyan:#7de9ff; --acc-lime:#c8ff5e; --acc-coral:#ff8a80; --acc-saffron:#ffc95e; --acc-mint:#7df5c6;
  --circuit:rgba(56,225,255,.08); --circuit-node:rgba(56,225,255,.16);
  --panel:#0a1120; --line:rgba(56,225,255,.14);
  --liquid-op:.55;
}
[data-theme="light"]{
  --bel-bg:#f4f7fb; --bel-text:#0d1626; --bel-muted:#41566d;
  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(255,255,255,.66); --bel-glass-border:rgba(13,22,38,.14);
  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(13,22,38,.10);
  --acc-cyan:#067a94; --acc-lime:#4d7a00; --acc-coral:#b3372c; --acc-saffron:#8a5a00; --acc-mint:#0a7a52;
  --circuit:rgba(13,22,38,.06); --circuit-node:rgba(13,22,38,.10);
  --panel:#ffffff; --line:rgba(13,22,38,.12);
  --liquid-op:.28;
}
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
html{scroll-behavior:smooth}
body{
  background:var(--bel-bg); color:var(--bel-text);
  font-family:var(--font-sans); font-size:15.5px; line-height:1.65;
  overflow-x:hidden; transition:background .4s ease,color .4s ease;
}
::selection{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink)}
:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--bel-cyan);outline-offset:2px;border-radius:6px}
h1,h2,h3,h4{font-family:var(--font-display);line-height:1.15}
.mono{font-family:var(--font-mono)}
img{max-width:100%;display:block}
button{font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer}

/* ---------- capas de fondo: circuitos + luz líquida ---------- */
.bg-circuit{position:fixed;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:0;pointer-events:none}
.cl{fill:none;stroke:var(--circuit);stroke-width:1.5}
.cn{fill:var(--circuit-node)}
.liquid{position:fixed;inset:-25%;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;filter:blur(90px) saturate(130%);opacity:var(--liquid-op)}
.blob{position:absolute;border-radius:50%;mix-blend-mode:screen;will-change:transform}
[data-theme="light"] .blob{mix-blend-mode:multiply}
.b1{width:52vmax;height:52vmax;left:-12vmax;top:-14vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 40% 40%,rgba(56,225,255,.55),transparent 62%);animation:drift1 46s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b2{width:44vmax;height:44vmax;right:-10vmax;top:22vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 55% 45%,rgba(182,255,46,.42),transparent 60%);animation:drift2 58s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b3{width:40vmax;height:40vmax;left:18vmax;bottom:-16vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%,rgba(78,240,176,.38),transparent 60%);animation:drift3 52s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
@keyframes drift1{to{transform:translate(9vmax,7vmax) scale(1.15)}}
@keyframes drift2{to{transform:translate(-8vmax,-6vmax) scale(.92)}}
@keyframes drift3{to{transform:translate(7vmax,-8vmax) scale(1.1)}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
  .blob,.floaty,.rv{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}
  html{scroll-behavior:auto}
}

/* ---------- glass ---------- */
.glass{
  background:var(--bel-glass-bg);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(140%);
  backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(140%);
  border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);
  box-shadow:var(--bel-shadow);
  border-radius:var(--radius);
}

/* ---------- nav ---------- */
.nav{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:60;padding:14px 0;transition:all .35s ease}
.nav.scrolled{
  background:var(--bel-glass-bg);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(140%);
  backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(140%);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);
  padding:9px 0;
}
.nav-in{max-width:1220px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px}
.brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--bel-text)}
.brand b{font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.14em;font-size:15px}
.brand small{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--acc-cyan);letter-spacing:.18em}
.nav-links{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none}
.nav-links::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.nav-links a{color:var(--bel-muted);text-decoration:none;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;padding:7px 11px;border-radius:var(--radius-pill);white-space:nowrap;transition:.25s}
.nav-links a:hover{color:var(--acc-cyan);background:rgba(56,225,255,.08)}
.theme-btn{width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);background:var(--bel-glass-bg);color:var(--bel-text);display:grid;place-items:center;transition:.25s}
.theme-btn:hover{border-color:var(--bel-cyan);color:var(--acc-cyan)}

/* ---------- layout ---------- */
.wrap{max-width:1220px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 22px;position:relative;z-index:1}
section{padding:86px 0 10px;scroll-margin-top:92px}
.kick{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.24em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.kick::before{content:"";width:26px;height:1px;background:var(--bel-cyan)}
h2{font-size:clamp(26px,3.4vw,40px);font-weight:700;margin:10px 0 8px;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.lead{color:var(--bel-muted);max-width:760px}
.meta-chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-top:14px}

/* ---------- botones / chips ---------- */
.btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;border-radius:var(--radius-pill);font-weight:600;font-size:14px;padding:13px 24px;text-decoration:none;border:1px solid transparent;transition:.25s;font-family:var(--font-sans)}
.btn-primary{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(182,255,46,.28)}
.btn-primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08);transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(182,255,46,.4)}
.btn-primary:active{transform:translateY(0);filter:brightness(.96)}
.btn-primary:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;transform:none;box-shadow:none}
.btn-ghost{border-color:rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:var(--acc-cyan);background:transparent}
.btn-ghost:hover{background:rgba(56,225,255,.1);transform:translateY(-2px)}
.btn-ghost:active{transform:translateY(0)}
.btn-ghost:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}
.btn-sm{padding:9px 16px;font-size:12.5px}
.chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:var(--acc-cyan);border-radius:var(--radius-pill);padding:6px 14px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase}
.chip.lime{border-color:rgba(182,255,46,.5);color:var(--acc-lime)}
.chip.mint{border-color:rgba(78,240,176,.5);color:var(--acc-mint)}
.chip.coral{border-color:rgba(255,107,94,.5);color:var(--acc-coral)}
.chip.saff{border-color:rgba(255,181,46,.5);color:var(--acc-saffron)}

/* ---------- hero ---------- */
.hero{padding:150px 0 40px}
.hero-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.05fr .95fr;gap:44px;align-items:center}
.hero h1{font-size:clamp(34px,5vw,60px);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:16px 0 14px}
.hero h1 .hl{color:var(--acc-lime)}
.hero h1 .hc{color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.hero p{color:var(--bel-muted);max-width:560px}
.hero-cta{display:flex;gap:14px;margin:26px 0 18px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.hero-stats{display:flex;gap:22px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px}
.hstat{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;color:var(--bel-muted);letter-spacing:.08em}
.hstat b{display:block;font-size:19px;color:var(--bel-text);font-family:var(--font-display)}
.hero-visual{position:relative}
.glow-lime{position:absolute;inset:-12%;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 55%,rgba(182,255,46,.22),transparent 62%);pointer-events:none;z-index:0}
.hero-frame{position:relative;z-index:1;border-radius:var(--radius-lg);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);box-shadow:var(--bel-shadow)}
.floaty{animation:float 7s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes float{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0)}50%{transform:translateY(-12px)}}
.hero-tag{position:absolute;z-index:2;left:18px;bottom:18px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}

/* ---------- tarjetas / grids ---------- */
.grid{display:grid;gap:18px}
.g2{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
.g3{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}
.g4{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}
.card{padding:22px;border-radius:var(--radius);border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel);transition:.3s}
.card h4{font-size:15px;margin-bottom:8px}
.card p{font-size:13px;color:var(--bel-muted)}
.card .tag{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase}

/* ---------- swatches ---------- */
.sw{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);cursor:pointer;background:var(--panel);transition:.25s;text-align:left;padding:0}
.sw:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);border-color:var(--bel-cyan)}
.sw .blk{height:74px}
.sw .inf{padding:12px 14px}
.sw .inf b{font-size:13px;display:block}
.sw .inf span{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--bel-muted);display:block;margin-top:2px}
.sw .inf em{font-style:normal;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:var(--acc-lime)}
.ratio-bar{display:flex;height:56px;border-radius:var(--radius-sm);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);margin:16px 0 6px}
.ratio-bar div{display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em}
.r60{width:60%;background:#0b1322;color:#9fb3c8}
.r30{width:30%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(56,225,255,.85),rgba(56,225,255,.55));color:#0d1626}
.r10{width:10%;background:#b6ff2e;color:#0d1626}

/* ---------- código ---------- */
.codebox{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;background:var(--panel)}
.codebox header{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);background:rgba(56,225,255,.05)}
.codebox header .fn{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.codebox header button{margin-left:auto}
.codebox pre{padding:16px;overflow:auto;max-height:420px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--bel-text)}

/* ---------- demo glass sobre circuitos ---------- */
.demo-stage{position:relative;border-radius:var(--radius-lg);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);padding:34px;background:#0b1322}
[data-theme="light"] .demo-stage{background:#eef3f9}
.demo-stage .abs-circ{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
.demo-inner{position:relative;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr;gap:22px}
.gcard{padding:24px}
.gcard .row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:14px}
.gcard .icbox{width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:12px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:rgba(56,225,255,.12);color:var(--acc-cyan);border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.25)}
.gcard h4{color:#e8f1ff}
.gcard .sub{font-size:12px;color:#9fb3c8}
.prog{height:8px;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(56,225,255,.15);overflow:hidden;margin:12px 0 6px}
.prog i{display:block;height:100%;width:72%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--bel-cyan),var(--bel-lime));border-radius:99px}
.btnrow{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}
.state-note{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;color:#9fb3c8;margin-top:12px;letter-spacing:.06em}

/* ---------- iconos ---------- */
.seg{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius-pill);overflow:hidden}
.seg button{background:transparent;border:0;color:var(--bel-muted);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;padding:8px 16px;letter-spacing:.08em}
.seg button.on{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);font-weight:600}
.icgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(120px,1fr));gap:14px;margin-top:20px}
.iccard{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);background:var(--panel);padding:18px 10px 12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:10px;transition:.25s;color:var(--bel-text)}
.iccard:hover{border-color:var(--bel-cyan);transform:translateY(-3px);color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.ic{width:var(--icon,24px);height:var(--icon,24px);transition:width .25s,height .25s}
.iccard span{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--bel-muted);letter-spacing:.08em}

/* ---------- logos ---------- */
.logo-stage{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:18px}
.logo-cell{border-radius:var(--radius);border:1px solid var(--line);padding:30px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:170px;position:relative}
.logo-cell .lbl{position:absolute;top:10px;left:14px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;color:var(--bel-muted)}
.cell-dark{background:#0b1322}
.cell-light{background:#f4f7fb}
.lockup{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px}
.lockup .wm b{font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.16em;font-size:24px}
.lockup .wm small{display:block;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.3em;color:var(--acc-cyan);margin-top:3px}
.lockup-v{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:10px;text-align:center}
.favrow{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:26px}
.favrow figure{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.favrow figcaption{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--bel-muted)}

/* ---------- fondos ---------- */
.fondos-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:18px}
.fondo-cell{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line)}
.fondo-prev{height:170px;position:relative}
.fondo-prev svg{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
.fondo-meta{padding:14px 16px;background:var(--panel);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.fondo-meta b{font-size:12.5px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-weight:500}
.fondo-meta button{margin-left:auto}

/* ---------- certificado ---------- */
.cert-wrap{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff}
.cert-wrap.bn svg{filter:grayscale(1) contrast(1.05)}
.cert-bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px}

/* ---------- vida útil ---------- */
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px}
th,td{padding:12px 14px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);vertical-align:top}
th{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.18em;color:var(--bel-muted);text-transform:uppercase}
td .mono{font-size:11.5px}
.st{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid}
.st-stable{color:var(--acc-lime);border-color:rgba(182,255,46,.5)}
.st-beta{color:var(--acc-saffron);border-color:rgba(255,181,46,.5)}
.st-draft{color:var(--acc-cyan);border-color:rgba(56,225,255,.5)}
.tree{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--bel-muted);background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:20px;overflow:auto}
.tree b{color:var(--acc-cyan);font-weight:500}
.tree i{color:var(--acc-lime);font-style:normal}
.rule{border-left:3px solid var(--bel-lime);padding:14px 18px;border-radius:0 var(--radius-sm) var(--radius-sm) 0;background:rgba(182,255,46,.06);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12.5px}

/* ---------- reveal ---------- */
.rv{opacity:0;transform:translateY(26px);transition:opacity .7s ease,transform .7s ease}
.rv.in{opacity:1;transform:none}

/* ---------- toast ---------- */
.toast{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:26px;transform:translate(-50%,80px);z-index:99;background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.06em;padding:11px 22px;border-radius:99px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(182,255,46,.35);transition:transform .35s ease;pointer-events:none}
.toast.show{transform:translate(-50%,0)}

/* ---------- footer ---------- */
footer{margin-top:90px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding:44px 0 34px;position:relative;z-index:1}
.foot{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr 1fr 1fr;gap:34px}
.foot h5{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.2em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:12px}
.foot a{color:var(--bel-muted);text-decoration:none;display:block;font-size:12.5px;padding:3px 0}
.foot a:hover{color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.foot p{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--bel-muted)}

@media (max-width:980px){
  .hero-grid,.demo-inner,.g3,.g4,.fondos-grid,.logo-stage{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .g2{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .foot{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .nav-links a{font-size:11.5px;padding:6px 8px}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<!-- defs globales: gradiente + escudo T -->
<svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute" aria-hidden="true">
  <defs>
    <linearGradient id="gradCL" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0" stop-color="#38e1ff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b6ff2e"/>
    </linearGradient>
    <symbol id="shield" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
      <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </symbol>
    <symbol id="shieldGrad" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
      <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="url(#gradCL)" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="url(#gradCL)" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </symbol>
    <pattern id="circuitPat" width="512" height="512" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
      <path class="cl" d="M0 64h118l34 34h118v94h66"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M64 512V396l40-40h120"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M512 128H400l-36 36v88"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M256 0v72l40 40h84"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M448 512v-84l-44-44H300"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M0 320h72l44 44v84"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="336" cy="192" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="224" cy="356" r="4"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="404" cy="384" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="296" cy="112" r="4"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="116" cy="364" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="400" cy="128" r="4"/>
    </pattern>
  </defs>
</svg>

<!-- fondo vivo -->
<svg class="bg-circuit" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#circuitPat)"/></svg>
<div class="liquid" aria-hidden="true"><div class="blob b1"></div><div class="blob b2"></div><div class="blob b3"></div></div>

<!-- ============ NAV (04b glass-nav) ============ -->
<nav class="nav" id="nav" aria-label="Navegación principal">
  <div class="nav-in">
    <a class="brand" href="#top">
      <svg class="ic" style="width:26px;height:26px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg>
      <span><b>BELENTANI</b><br><small>DESIGN SYSTEM v1.0</small></span>
    </a>
    <div class="nav-links">
      <a href="#tokens">02 Tokens</a>
      <a href="#componentes">04 Glass</a>
      <a href="#iconos">05 Iconos</a>
      <a href="#logos">01 Logos</a>
      <a href="#fondos">03 Fondos</a>
      <a href="#heroasset">06 Hero</a>
      <a href="#certificado">07 Cert.</a>
      <a href="#vida">Vida útil</a>
    </div>
    <button class="theme-btn" id="themeBtn" aria-label="Cambiar tema claro/oscuro">
      <svg class="ic" style="width:18px;height:18px" id="icMoon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M20 13.5A8 8 0 0 1 10.5 4 8 8 0 1 0 20 13.5Z"/></svg>
      <svg class="ic" style="width:18px;height:18px;display:none" id="icSun" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2.5V5M12 19v2.5M2.5 12H5M19 12h2.5M4.9 4.9 6.7 6.7M17.3 17.3l1.8 1.8M19.1 4.9l-1.8 1.8M6.7 17.3l-1.8 1.8"/></svg>
    </button>
  </div>
</nav>

<main id="top">

<!-- ============ HERO ============ -->
<header class="hero wrap">
  <div class="hero-grid">
    <div class="rv">
      <div class="meta-chips">
        <span class="chip">2026-09-03</span>
        <span class="chip lime">AGPLv3</span>
        <span class="chip mint">Fuente verdad · BELENTANI-DESIGN-SYSTEM.md</span>
      </div>
      <h1>Galería viva de activos.<br><span class="hc">Luz líquida</span>, glass y <span class="hl">código primero</span>.</h1>
      <p>Definición oficial de los 7 tipos de activos del Instituto Universal: tokens, componentes glass, iconos, logos, fondos, hero media y certificado. Multinacional, accesible WCAG 2.1 AA y versionada para los 5 repos.</p>
      <div class="hero-cta">
        <a class="btn btn-primary" href="#tokens">Explorar tokens</a>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" data-dl="theme">Descargar belentani-theme.css</button>
      </div>
      <div class="meta-chips">
        <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span>
        <span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
        <span class="chip mint">LINGUA · ES EN FR PT AR</span>
      </div>
      <div class="hero-stats">
        <div class="hstat"><b>7</b>tipos de activo</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>14</b>iconos stroke 1.8</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>≥4.5:1</b>contraste AA</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>60·30·10</b>regla de color</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="hero-visual rv">
      <div class="glow-lime" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="hero-frame floaty">
        <img src="https://image.qwenlm.ai/public_source/deb80e45-8c6d-443b-ad6d-55222331e587/18706d73c-2c4c-414b-b379-8944274ebad3.png" alt="Escudo T tridimensional sobre circuitos con glow lima y cyan — hero-secure-dark 1600×900" width="1600" height="900">
      </div>
      <div class="hero-tag">
        <span class="chip">06a · hero-secure-dark</span>
        <span class="chip lime">1600×900</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<!-- ============ 02 TOKENS ============ -->
<section id="tokens" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 02 · Paleta + Tokens</div>
    <h2>Tokens de diseño — código fuente, no imagen</h2>
    <p class="lead">Psicología del color aplicada: <b style="color:var(--acc-cyan)">navy = confianza</b>, <b style="color:var(--acc-lime)">lime = acción</b>, <b style="color:var(--acc-cyan)">cyan = tecnología</b>. Glassmorfismo: profundidad sin peso. Click en cualquier muestra copia su token.</p>
  </div>

  <div class="ratio-bar rv" role="img" aria-label="Regla 60-30-10: 60% navy superficie, 30% cyan tecnología, 10% lime acción">
    <div class="r60">60 · NAVY</div><div class="r30">30 · CYAN</div><div class="r10">10</div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid g4 rv" id="swatches" style="margin-top:18px"></div>

  <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:26px">
    <div class="codebox rv">
      <header><span class="fn">tokens/belentani-theme.css</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="theme">Copiar / bajar</button></header>
      <pre><code id="codeTheme"></code></pre>
    </div>
    <div class="codebox rv">
      <header><span class="fn">tokens/tailwind.preset.js</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="preset">Copiar / bajar</button></header>
      <pre><code id="codePreset"></code></pre>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid g4 rv" style="margin-top:22px">
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">60-30-10</span><h4>Superficie dominante</h4><p>Navy 60% como base; cyan/glass 30% estructura; lime 10% solo para CTA y logro.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">WCAG 2.1 AA</span><h4>Contraste ≥ 4.5:1</h4><p>Texto sobre navy y sobre paper verificado. Lime solo con tinta navy encima.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">Psicología</span><h4>Confianza · Acción · Tec</h4><p>Navy institucional, lime dopamina de logro, cyan feedback de sistema/IA.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">Glass</span><h4>Profundidad sin peso</h4><p>blur(18px) saturate(140%) + borde cyan 18%. Nunca sobre texto sin capa sólida.</p></div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 04 COMPONENTES GLASS ============ -->
<section id="componentes" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 04 · Componentes Glass</div>
    <h2>glass-card · glass-nav · btn-* · badge-chip</h2>
    <p class="lead">Demo real: blur auténtico sobre el fondo de circuitos. Estados hover / active / disabled / focus-visible (anillo cyan 2px — pulsa <span class="mono">Tab</span>).</p>
  </div>

  <div class="demo-stage rv" style="margin-top:26px">
    <svg class="abs-circ" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#circuitPat)"/></svg>
    <div class="demo-inner">
      <!-- 04a glass-card -->
      <div class="glass gcard">
        <div class="row">
          <div class="icbox"><svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10.2 3.5v5.6L5 17.9a2 2 0 0 0 1.8 3h10.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.8-3L13.8 9.1V3.5"/><path d="M9.8 3.5h4.4"/><path d="M7.3 14.5h9.4"/></svg></div>
          <div><h4>Lab · Ciberseguridad básica</h4><div class="sub">Ruta SECURE-T · Módulo 03 · 25 min</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="prog" role="progressbar" aria-valuenow="72" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><i></i></div>
        <div class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:#9fb3c8">72% · 3 insignias · racha 6 días</div>
        <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:16px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">Continuar</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Ver ruta</button>
        </div>
        <div class="meta-chips" style="margin-top:14px">
          <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span><span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">04a · rgba(13,22,38,.55) · blur 18px · borde cyan .18 · sombra 0 8 32</div>
      </div>
      <!-- 04c botones + 04d chips -->
      <div class="glass gcard">
        <h4 style="color:#e8f1ff;margin-bottom:14px">Estados de botón</h4>
        <div class="btnrow">
          <button class="btn btn-primary">btn-primary</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost">btn-ghost</button>
        </div>
        <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" disabled>disabled</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" disabled>disabled</button>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">hover ↑ brillo · active ↓ · focus-visible: anillo cyan 2px (Tab)</div>
        <h4 style="color:#e8f1ff;margin:18px 0 12px">badge-chip · radius 99px</h4>
        <div class="meta-chips">
          <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span>
          <span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
          <span class="chip mint">WCAG AA</span>
          <span class="chip saff">XP ×120</span>
          <span class="chip coral">RACHA 6</span>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">04d · outline cyan · texto cyan legible · nunca fondo sólido</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="codebox rv" style="margin-top:22px">
    <header><span class="fn">components/glass.css (extracto oficial)</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="glasscss">Copiar</button></header>
    <pre><code id="codeGlass"></code></pre>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 05 ICONOS ============ -->
<section id="iconos" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv" style="display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:18px;flex-wrap:wrap">
    <div style="flex:1;min-width:260px">
      <div class="kick">Tipo 05 · Iconos SVG stroke</div>
      <h2>Set único · 24px · trazo 1.8 · round caps</h2>
      <p class="lead">Mismos vectores a 16 / 24 / 32 sin redibujar. Click copia el SVG completo.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Tamaño de iconos">
      <button data-size="16">16</button><button data-size="24" class="on">24</button><button data-size="32">32</button>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="icgrid rv" id="iconGrid"></div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 01 LOGOS ============ -->
<section id="logos" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 01 · Logos SVG</div>
    <h2>logo-madre · escudo T + palabra</h2>
    <p class="lead">Legible a 16px favicon y a 2m de banner. Trazo mínimo 1.5px. Versiones horizontal, stacked, mono blanco y mono negro incluidas.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="logo-stage rv" style="margin-top:24px">
    <div class="logo-cell cell-dark"><span class="lbl">01a · horizontal (color)</span>
      <div class="lockup">
        <svg class="ic" style="width:56px;height:56px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg>
        <div class="wm"><b style="color:#e8f1ff">BELENTANI</b><small>SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</small></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="logo-cell cell-dark"><span class="lbl">01a · stacked</span>
      <div class="lockup-v">
        <svg class="ic" style="width:64px;height:64px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg>
        <div class="wm" style="text-align:center"><b style="color:#e8f1ff;letter-spacing:.2em">BELENTANI</b><small>SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</small></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="logo-cell cell-dark"><span class="lbl">01c · mono blanco (merch/PDF)</span>
      <div class="lockup">
        <svg class="ic" style="width:52px;height:52px;color:#ffffff" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shield"/></svg>
        <div class="wm"><b style="color:#fff">BELENTANI</b><small style="color:#fff;opacity:.75">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</small></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="logo-cell cell-light"><span class="lbl" style="color:#41566d">01c · mono negro</span>
      <div class="lockup">
        <svg class="ic" style="width:52px;height:52px;color:#0d1626" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shield"/></svg>
        <div class="wm"><b style="color:#0d1626">BELENTANI</b><small style="color:#0d1626;opacity:.7">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</small></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="logo-cell cell-dark" style="grid-column:1/-1"><span class="lbl">01b · favicon — prueba de escala 16 / 32 / 64</span>
      <div class="favrow">
        <figure><svg class="ic" style="width:16px;height:16px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg><figcaption>16px</figcaption></figure>
        <figure><svg class="ic" style="width:32px;height:32px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg><figcaption>32px</figcaption></figure>
        <figure><svg class="ic" style="width:64px;height:64px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg><figcaption>64px</figcaption></figure>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="btnrow rv" style="margin-top:18px">
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="logoh">logo-madre.svg</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="logov">logo-stacked.svg</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="monow">logo-mono-blanco.svg</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="monok">logo-mono-negro.svg</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="favicon">favicon.svg</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="faviconpng">favicon-32.png</button>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 03 FONDOS ============ -->
<section id="fondos" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 03 · Fondos ligeros</div>
    <h2>Texturas que no matan el contraste</h2>
    <p class="lead">bg-circuit-dark tileable 512px al 8% sobre navy · bg-grid-light rejilla papel · glow-lime radial reutilizable.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="fondos-grid rv" style="margin-top:24px">
    <div class="fondo-cell">
      <div class="fondo-prev" style="background:#0b1322"><svg aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#circuitPat)"/></svg></div>
      <div class="fondo-meta"><b>03a · bg-circuit-dark.svg</b><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="circuit">SVG</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="fondo-cell">
      <div class="fondo-prev" style="background:#f4f7fb"><svg id="gridPrev" aria-hidden="true"></svg></div>
      <div class="fondo-meta"><b>03b · bg-grid-light.svg</b><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="grid">SVG</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="fondo-cell">
      <div class="fondo-prev" style="background:#0b1322"><div class="glow-lime" style="inset:0"></div></div>
      <div class="fondo-meta"><b>03c · glow-lime (radial 20%)</b><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="glowcss">CSS</button></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 06 HERO MEDIA ============ -->
<section id="heroasset" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 06 · Hero media</div>
    <h2>hero-secure-dark.png · 1600×900</h2>
    <p class="lead">Escudo T 3D sobre circuitos + glow líquido. Exportable a WebP. Regenerado propio (no reference externo).</p>
  </div>
  <div class="glass rv" style="margin-top:22px;padding:14px;position:relative;overflow:hidden">
    <img src="https://image.qwenlm.ai/public_source/deb80e45-8c6d-443b-ad6d-55222331e587/18706d73c-2c4c-414b-b379-8944274ebad3.png" alt="hero-secure-dark: escudo T 3D sobre placa de circuitos con energía lima y ondas cyan líquidas" style="border-radius:var(--radius)">
    <div class="meta-chips" style="margin-top:12px">
      <span class="chip">PNG → WebP</span><span class="chip lime">1600×900</span><span class="chip mint">glow reutilizable</span>
      <span class="chip saff">06b hoodie-mockup · NO PRIORITARIO</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ============ 07 CERTIFICADO ============ -->
<section id="certificado" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 07 · Documentos</div>
    <h2>certificado-base.svg · marco + claim + QR verificable</h2>
    <p class="lead">Imprimible B/N legible. Alterna color / blanco-negro para validar contraste de impresión.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="cert-bar rv">
    <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Modo de vista del certificado">
      <button id="certColor" class="on">COLOR</button><button id="certBN">B/N IMPRIMIBLE</button>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="cert">Descargar certificado-base.svg</button>
  </div>
  <div class="cert-wrap rv" id="certWrap">
    <svg id="certSvg" viewBox="0 0 1200 849" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" role="img" aria-label="Certificado base BELENTANI con marco, claim y QR verificable">
      <rect width="1200" height="849" fill="#ffffff"/>
      <rect x="22" y="22" width="1156" height="805" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="3"/>
      <rect x="36" y="36" width="1128" height="777" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1"/>
      <rect x="22" y="22" width="14" height="14" fill="#b6ff2e"/><rect x="1164" y="22" width="14" height="14" fill="#38e1ff"/>
      <rect x="22" y="813" width="14" height="14" fill="#38e1ff"/><rect x="1164" y="813" width="14" height="14" fill="#b6ff2e"/>
      <g transform="translate(540,64) scale(5)" opacity="0.07">
        <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.8"/>
        <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>
      </g>
      <text x="600" y="120" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="15" letter-spacing="6" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".72">BELENTANI · INSTITUTO UNIVERSAL GRATUITO</text>
      <text x="600" y="192" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="58" fill="#0d1626" letter-spacing="6">CERTIFICADO</text>
      <text x="600" y="228" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="16" letter-spacing="8" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">DE COMPETENCIA DIGITAL</text>
      <text x="600" y="300" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="16" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".7">Se certifica que</text>
      <text x="600" y="356" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="600" font-size="40" fill="#0d1626">Nombre Apellido Estudiante</text>
      <line x1="330" y1="378" x2="870" y2="378" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1"/>
      <text x="600" y="430" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="16" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".85">ha completado con aprovechamiento la ruta de aprendizaje,</text>
      <text x="600" y="456" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="16" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".85">cumpliendo los estándares de calidad BELENTANI y accesibilidad WCAG 2.1 AA.</text>
      <text x="600" y="510" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="14" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".75">20 horas · 14 competencias · verificación por código QR</text>
      <line x1="110" y1="700" x2="430" y2="700" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.2"/>
      <text x="270" y="724" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="13" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">Dirección Académica</text>
      <line x1="500" y1="700" x2="820" y2="700" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.2"/>
      <text x="660" y="724" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="13" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">Mentoría Técnica</text>
      <text x="110" y="788" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="13" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">ID: BEL-2026-000123 · belentani.org/verify</text>

      <text x="1045" y="792" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="12" letter-spacing="3" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">VERIFICAR</text>
    </svg>
  </div>
</section>

        <tr><td class="mono">07</td><td>certificado-base + QR</td><td class="mono">svg</td><td class="mono">FASE CERTS</td><td><span class="st st-draft">DRAFT v0.9</span></td><td class="mono">assets/docs/</td></tr>

      <div class="card"><span class="tag">Vida útil</span><h4>Mantenimiento multinacional</h4><p>Los tokens se replican a los 5 repos vía preset; los iconos y logos son fuente única; el hero se regenera con IA local por región sin romper el claim; el certificado versiona con la fase de certs.</p></div>
