<div class="toast" id="toast" role="status" aria-live="polite">Copiado ✓</div>

<script>
/* ================= utilidades ================= */
var $=function(s){return document.querySelector(s)};
var toastT;
function toast(msg){var t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg||'Copiado ✓';t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastT);toastT=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('show')},1600)}
function copyText(txt,msg){ if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(function(){toast(msg)}).catch(function(){fallbackCopy(txt,msg)})}else{fallbackCopy(txt,msg)} }
function fallbackCopy(txt,msg){var ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=txt;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();try{document.execCommand('copy');toast(msg)}catch(e){toast('No se pudo copiar')}document.body.removeChild(ta)}
function dl(name,text,mime){var b=new Blob([text],{type:mime||'image/svg+xml'});var u=URL.createObjectURL(b);var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(u)},1200);toast('Descargando '+name)}

/* ================= tema ================= */
var root=document.documentElement;
function setTheme(th){root.setAttribute('data-theme',th);$('#icMoon').style.display=th==='dark'?'':'none';$('#icSun').style.display=th==='light'?'':'none';try{localStorage.setItem('bel-theme',th)}catch(e){}}
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){setTheme(root.getAttribute('data-theme')==='dark'?'light':'dark')});
try{var st=localStorage.getItem('bel-theme');if(st)setTheme(st)}catch(e){}

/* ================= nav scroll ================= */
window.addEventListener('scroll',function(){$('#nav').classList.toggle('scrolled',window.scrollY>30)},{passive:true});

/* ================= reveal ================= */
var io=new IntersectionObserver(function(es){es.forEach(function(e){if(e.isIntersecting){e.target.classList.add('in');io.unobserve(e.target)}})},{threshold:.12});
document.querySelectorAll('.rv').forEach(function(el){io.observe(el)});

/* ================= strings oficiales ================= */
var THEME_CSS='/* BELENTANI DESIGN SYSTEM — THEME v1.0.0 · AGPLv3 · 2026-09-03\n   Teoría: 60-30-10 · WCAG 2.1 AA (>=4.5:1 texto) · glassmorfismo */\n:root{\n  /* 60% superficie */\n  --bel-bg:#0b1322;          /* navy = confianza */\n  --bel-paper:#f4f7fb;       /* modo claro */\n  --bel-ink:#0d1626;         /* tinta */\n  --bel-text:#e8f1ff;\n  --bel-muted:#9fb3c8;\n  /* 30% tecnología */\n  --bel-cyan:#38e1ff;        /* cyan = tecnología */\n  --bel-mint:#4ef0b0;\n  /* 10% acción */\n  --bel-lime:#b6ff2e;        /* lime = acción */\n  --bel-lime-ink:#0d1626;\n  /* apoyo */\n  --bel-coral:#ff6b5e;\n  --bel-saffron:#ffb52e;\n  /* glass: profundidad sin peso */\n  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(13,22,38,.55);\n  --bel-glass-border:rgba(56,225,255,.18);\n  --bel-glass-blur:18px;\n  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n  /* forma */\n  --radius-sm:10px; --radius:18px; --radius-lg:28px; --radius-pill:99px;\n  /* tipografía */\n  --font-display:"Sora",system-ui,sans-serif;\n  --font-sans:"Inter",system-ui,sans-serif;\n  --font-mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace;\n  /* foco accesible WCAG */\n  --focus-ring:0 0 0 2px #38e1ff;\n}\n[data-theme="light"]{\n  --bel-bg:#f4f7fb; --bel-text:#0d1626; --bel-muted:#41566d;\n  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(255,255,255,.66);\n  --bel-glass-border:rgba(13,22,38,.14);\n  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(13,22,38,.10);\n}';
var TAILWIND_PRESET='/** BELENTANI tailwind preset — AGPLv3 · espejo de belentani-theme.css\n *  Uso en los 5 repos: module.exports = require(\'@bel/tokens/tailwind.preset\') */\nmodule.exports = {\n  theme: {\n    extend: {\n      colors: {\n        bel: {\n          bg:     \'#0b1322\',   /* 60 navy confianza */\n          paper:  \'#f4f7fb\',\n          ink:    \'#0d1626\',\n          cyan:   \'#38e1ff\',   /* 30 tecnología */\n          mint:   \'#4ef0b0\',\n          lime:   \'#b6ff2e\',   /* 10 acción */\n          coral:  \'#ff6b5e\',\n          saffron:\'#ffb52e\',\n        },\n      },\n      fontFamily: {\n        display: [\'Sora\', \'system-ui\', \'sans-serif\'],\n        sans:    [\'Inter\', \'system-ui\', \'sans-serif\'],\n        mono:    [\'"JetBrains Mono"\', \'ui-monospace\', \'monospace\'],\n      },\n      borderRadius: { sm: \'10px\', DEFAULT: \'18px\', lg: \'28px\', pill: \'99px\' },\n      backdropBlur: { glass: \'18px\' },\n      boxShadow: {\n        glass: \'0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35)\',\n        lime:  \'0 6px 24px rgba(182,255,46,.28)\',\n      },\n      borderColor: { glass: \'rgba(56,225,255,.18)\' },\n    },\n  },\n};';
var GLASS_CSS='/* 04a glass-card · dark */\n.glass-card{\n  background:rgba(13,22,38,.55);\n  backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(140%);\n  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(140%);\n  border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.18);\n  box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n  border-radius:18px;\n}\n/* 04b glass-nav fija + blur al scroll */\n.glass-nav{position:fixed;inset:0 0 auto 0;transition:.35s}\n.glass-nav.scrolled{background:rgba(13,22,38,.55);backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(140%);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.18)}\n/* 04c botones */\n.btn-primary{background:#b6ff2e;color:#0d1626;border-radius:99px}\n.btn-primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08)}\n.btn-primary:active{filter:brightness(.96)}\n.btn-primary:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}\n.btn-ghost{border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:#7de9ff;background:transparent;border-radius:99px}\n.btn-ghost:hover{background:rgba(56,225,255,.1)}\n:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #38e1ff;outline-offset:2px} /* anillo cyan 2px */\n/* 04d badge-chip */\n.badge-chip{border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:#7de9ff;border-radius:99px;\n  padding:6px 14px;font:500 10.5px/1 "JetBrains Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase}';

$('#codeTheme').textContent=THEME_CSS;
$('#codePreset').textContent=TAILWIND_PRESET;
$('#codeGlass').textContent=GLASS_CSS;

/* ================= swatches ================= */
var SW=[
 {n:'bg / navy',v:'--bel-bg',h:'#0b1322',r:'60% SUPERFICIE · CONFIANZA'},
 {n:'cyan',v:'--bel-cyan',h:'#38e1ff',r:'30% TECNOLOGÍA'},
 {n:'lime',v:'--bel-lime',h:'#b6ff2e',r:'10% ACCIÓN'},
 {n:'mint',v:'--bel-mint',h:'#4ef0b0',r:'APOYO · LOGRO'},
 {n:'paper',v:'--bel-paper',h:'#f4f7fb',r:'MODO CLARO'},
 {n:'ink',v:'--bel-ink',h:'#0d1626',r:'TINTA'},
 {n:'coral',v:'--bel-coral',h:'#ff6b5e',r:'APOYO · ALERTA'},
 {n:'saffron',v:'--bel-saffron',h:'#ffb52e',r:'APOYO · BETA'}
];
$('#swatches').innerHTML=SW.map(function(s){
 return '<button class="sw" data-c="'+s.v+': '+s.h+';" aria-label="Copiar '+s.v+' '+s.h+'">'+
 '<div class="blk" style="background:'+s.h+'"></div>'+
 '<div class="inf"><b>'+s.n+'</b><span>'+s.h+' · '+s.v+'</span><em>'+s.r+'</em></div></button>';
}).join('');
document.querySelectorAll('.sw').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){copyText(b.getAttribute('data-c'),'Token copiado ✓')})});

/* ================= iconos ================= */
var ICONS=[
 {k:'home',p:'<path d="M3 11.5 12 4l9 7.5"/><path d="M5.5 9.8V20h13V9.8"/><path d="M10 20v-5.2h4V20"/>'},
 {k:'cursos',p:'<path d="M6.5 3H20v14.2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 19.7V5.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 3Z"/><path d="M4 19.7a2.3 2.3 0 0 1 2.3-2.3H20"/><path d="M9 7.5h7"/>'},
 {k:'labs',p:'<path d="M9.8 3.5h4.4"/><path d="M10.2 3.5v5.6L5 17.9a2 2 0 0 0 1.8 3h10.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.8-3L13.8 9.1V3.5"/><path d="M7.3 14.5h9.4"/>'},
 {k:'perfil',p:'<circle cx="12" cy="8.2" r="3.6"/><path d="M4.8 20c.9-3.5 3.8-5.3 7.2-5.3s6.3 1.8 7.2 5.3"/>'},
 {k:'escudo',p:'<path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z"/><path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7"/>'},
 {k:'candado',p:'<rect x="5" y="10.5" width="14" height="9.5" rx="2.2"/><path d="M8.3 10.5V7.7a3.7 3.7 0 0 1 7.4 0v2.8"/><circle cx="12" cy="15.2" r="1.2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'},
 {k:'cerebro-ia',p:'<rect x="6.5" y="6.5" width="11" height="11" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/><path d="M9.5 6.5V4M14.5 6.5V4M9.5 20v-2.5M14.5 20v-2.5M6.5 9.5H4M6.5 14.5H4M20 9.5h-2.5M20 14.5h-2.5"/>'},
 {k:'nube',p:'<path d="M7.2 18.5h9.3a3.7 3.7 0 0 0 .5-7.37A5.6 5.6 0 0 0 6.3 9.4a4.5 4.5 0 0 0 .9 9.1Z"/>'},
 {k:'terminal',p:'<rect x="3.5" y="4.5" width="17" height="15" rx="2"/><path d="m7 9.5 3 2.7-3 2.7"/><path d="M12.5 15H17"/>'},
 {k:'trofeo',p:'<path d="M8 4.5h8v4.7a4 4 0 0 1-8 0Z"/><path d="M8 5.5H4.8v.8A3.2 3.2 0 0 0 8 9.5M16 5.5h3.2v.8A3.2 3.2 0 0 1 16 9.5"/><path d="M12 13.2v3"/><path d="M9.6 16.2h4.8l.6 3.8H9Z"/><path d="M8.8 20h6.4"/>'},
 {k:'racha',p:'<path d="M12.2 3.8c.9 2.6 3.9 4.2 4.3 7.6a4.6 4.6 0 0 1-9.1.5c0-2 1-3.5 2-5 .5 1.1 1 1.7 1.9 2.1.2-2 .1-3.5.9-5.2Z"/>'},
 {k:'xp',p:'<path d="m12 3.5 2.6 5.3 5.9.9-4.3 4.1 1 5.8-5.2-2.7-5.2 2.7 1-5.8-4.3-4.1 5.9-.9Z"/>'},
 {k:'check',p:'<path d="m4.5 12.6 5 5L19.5 6.5"/>'},
 {k:'alerta',p:'<path d="M10.3 4.6 3.1 17.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h14.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 4.6a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z"/><path d="M12 9.5v4.4"/><circle cx="12" cy="16.9" r="1" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'}
];
function iconSvg(ic){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="ic">'+ic.p+'</svg>'}
$('#iconGrid').innerHTML=ICONS.map(function(ic){
 return '<button class="iccard" data-k="'+ic.k+'" aria-label="Copiar SVG de '+ic.k+'">'+iconSvg(ic)+'<span>'+ic.k+'</span></button>';
}).join('');
document.querySelectorAll('.iccard').forEach(function(b){
 b.addEventListener('click',function(){
  var ic=ICONS.filter(function(x){return x.k===b.getAttribute('data-k')})[0];
  copyText(iconSvg(ic).replace(' class="ic"',''),'SVG '+ic.k+' copiado ✓');
 });
});
document.querySelectorAll('.seg [data-size]').forEach(function(btn){
 btn.addEventListener('click',function(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.seg [data-size]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('on')});
  btn.classList.add('on');
  $('#iconGrid').style.setProperty('--icon',btn.getAttribute('data-size')+'px');
 });
});

/* ================= fondos: grid preview + strings ================= */
(function(){
 var g='';for(var i=32;i<512;i+=32){g+='<path d="M'+i+' 0V512M0 '+i+'H512"/>'}
 $('#gridPrev').innerHTML='<g fill="none" stroke="rgba(13,22,38,.07)" stroke-width="1">'+g+'</g>';
})();
var CIRCUIT_SVG='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><g fill="none" stroke="rgba(56,225,255,.08)" stroke-width="1.5"><path d="M0 64h118l34 34h118v94h66"/><path d="M64 512V396l40-40h120"/><path d="M512 128H400l-36 36v88"/><path d="M256 0v72l40 40h84"/><path d="M448 512v-84l-44-44H300"/><path d="M0 320h72l44 44v84"/></g><g fill="rgba(56,225,255,.16)"><circle cx="336" cy="192" r="4"/><circle cx="224" cy="356" r="4"/><circle cx="404" cy="384" r="4"/><circle cx="296" cy="112" r="4"/><circle cx="116" cy="364" r="4"/><circle cx="400" cy="128" r="4"/></g></svg>';
var GRID_SVG=(function(){var g='';for(var i=32;i<512;i+=32){g+='<path d="M'+i+' 0V512M0 '+i+'H512"/>'}return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><g fill="none" stroke="rgba(13,22,38,.07)" stroke-width="1">'+g+'</g></svg>'})();
var GLOW_CSS='/* 03c glow-lime · radial 20% · reutilizable en hero CTA */\n.glow-lime{position:absolute;inset:-12%;pointer-events:none;\n  background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 55%, rgba(182,255,46,.20), transparent 62%);}';

/* ================= logos builders ================= */
function shieldStr(stroke){return '<path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="'+stroke+'" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="'+stroke+'" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>'}
var GRAD='<defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#38e1ff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b6ff2e"/></linearGradient></defs>';
function logoH(stroke,txt,sub){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="470" height="120" viewBox="0 0 470 120">'+(stroke==='url(#g)'?GRAD:'')+'<g transform="translate(16,18) scale(3.5)">'+shieldStr(stroke)+'</g><text x="122" y="66" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="38" letter-spacing="6" fill="'+txt+'">BELENTANI</text><text x="124" y="94" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="13" letter-spacing="4" fill="'+sub+'">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</text></svg>'}
function logoV(stroke,txt,sub){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="300" height="210" viewBox="0 0 300 210">'+(stroke==='url(#g)'?GRAD:'')+'<g transform="translate(108,14) scale(3.5)">'+shieldStr(stroke)+'</g><text x="150" y="150" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="30" letter-spacing="7" fill="'+txt+'">BELENTANI</text><text x="150" y="180" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="11" letter-spacing="3" fill="'+sub+'">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</text></svg>'}
var FAVICON_SVG='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="32" height="32" viewBox="0 0 24 24">'+GRAD+shieldStr('url(#g)')+'</svg>';

/* favicon-32.png vía canvas */
function faviconPng(cb){
 var c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=32;c.height=32;var x=c.getContext('2d');
 var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,32,32);g.addColorStop(0,'#38e1ff');g.addColorStop(1,'#b6ff2e');
 x.scale(32/24,32/24);x.strokeStyle=g;x.lineWidth=1.8;x.lineCap='round';x.lineJoin='round';
 x.stroke(new Path2D('M12 2.5 L19.5 5.5 V11.7 C19.5 16.6 16.3 19.8 12 21.5 C7.7 19.8 4.5 16.6 4.5 11.7 V5.5 Z'));
 x.stroke(new Path2D('M8.6 8.2 H15.4 M12 8.2 V15.2'));
 cb(c.toDataURL('image/png'));
}

/* ================= QR determinista (certificado) ================= */
function mulberry32(a){return function(){a|=0;a=a+0x6D2B79F5|0;var t=Math.imul(a^a>>>15,1|a);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296}}
(function(){
 var rnd=mulberry32(20260903),N=25,S=8,out='';
 function inFinder(cx,cy){return (cx<7&&cy<7)||(cx>=N-7&&cy<7)||(cx<7&&cy>=N-7)}
 function finder(fx,fy){var s='';for(var y=0;y<7;y++)for(var x=0;x<7;x++){var b=(x===0||x===6||y===0||y===6)||(x>=2&&x<=4&&y>=2&&y<=4);if(b)s+='<rect x="'+((fx+x)*S)+'" y="'+((fy+y)*S)+'" width="'+S+'" height="'+S+'"/>'}return s}
 for(var y=0;y<N;y++)for(var x=0;x<N;x++){if(inFinder(x,y))continue;if(rnd()>0.52)out+='<rect x="'+(x*S)+'" y="'+(y*S)+'" width="'+S+'" height="'+S+'"/>'}
 out=finder(0,0)+finder(N-7,0)+finder(0,N-7)+out;
 var q=$('#certQR');q.innerHTML='<g fill="#0d1626">'+out+'</g>';
})();

/* ================= certificado toggles ================= */
$('#certColor').addEventListener('click',function(){$('#certWrap').classList.remove('bn');$('#certColor').classList.add('on');$('#certBN').classList.remove('on')});
$('#certBN').addEventListener('click',function(){$('#certWrap').classList.add('bn');$('#certBN').classList.add('on');$('#certColor').classList.remove('on')});

/* ================= descargas ================= */
var DL={
 theme:function(){dl('belentani-theme.css',THEME_CSS,'text/css')},
 preset:function(){dl('tailwind.preset.js',TAILWIND_PRESET,'text/javascript')},
 glasscss:function(){copyText(GLASS_CSS,'glass.css copiado ✓')},
 circuit:function(){dl('bg-circuit-dark.svg',CIRCUIT_SVG)},
 grid:function(){dl('bg-grid-light.svg',GRID_SVG)},
 glowcss:function(){copyText(GLOW_CSS,'glow-lime CSS copiado ✓')},
 logoh:function(){dl('logo-madre.svg',logoH('url(#g)','#e8f1ff','#38e1ff'))},
 logov:function(){dl('logo-stacked.svg',logoV('url(#g)','#e8f1ff','#38e1ff'))},
 monow:function(){dl('logo-mono-blanco.svg',logoH('#ffffff','#ffffff','rgba(255,255,255,.8)'))},
 monok:function(){dl('logo-mono-negro.svg',logoH('#0d1626','#0d1626','rgba(13,22,38,.75)'))},
 favicon:function(){dl('favicon.svg',FAVICON_SVG)},
 faviconpng:function(){faviconPng(function(u){var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download='favicon-32.png';a.click();toast('Descargando favicon-32.png')})},
 cert:function(){dl('certificado-base.svg','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n'+$('#certSvg').outerHTML)}
};
document.querySelectorAll('[data-dl]').forEach(function(b){
 b.addEventListener('click',function(){var f=DL[b.getAttribute('data-dl')];if(f)f()});
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

## [ASSISTANT] 2026-09-03 07:04 · Qwen3.8-Max

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es" data-theme="dark">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>BELENTANI · Edición Cromática Viva Centelleante — Galería Oficial</title>
<meta name="description" content="BELENTANI Design System v1.1 CHROMA — luz líquida extrema, lab cromático, tokens, glass, iconos, logos, fondos, hero y certificado. AGPLv3.">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-600-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/sora@latest/latin-700-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/inter@latest/latin-600-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<style>
/* ============================================================
   BELENTANI THEME v1.1 · CHROMA EDITION · AGPLv3 · 2026-09-03
   60-30-10 · WCAG 2.1 AA · glassmorfismo · luz líquida extrema
   ============================================================ */
@property --ang{syntax:'<angle>';initial-value:0deg;inherits:false}
:root{
  --bel-bg:#0b1322; --bel-paper:#f4f7fb; --bel-ink:#0d1626;
  --bel-text:#e8f1ff; --bel-muted:#9fb3c8;
  --bel-cyan:#38e1ff; --bel-mint:#4ef0b0;
  --bel-lime:#b6ff2e; --bel-lime-ink:#0d1626;
  --bel-coral:#ff6b5e; --bel-saffron:#ffb52e; --bel-violet:#a78bfa;
  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(13,22,38,.55);
  --bel-glass-border:rgba(56,225,255,.18);
  --bel-glass-blur:18px;
  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
  --radius-sm:10px; --radius:18px; --radius-lg:28px; --radius-pill:99px;
  --font-display:"Sora",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --font-sans:"Inter",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --font-mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace;
  --acc-cyan:#7de9ff; --acc-lime:#c8ff5e; --acc-coral:#ff8a80; --acc-saffron:#ffc95e; --acc-mint:#7df5c6; --acc-violet:#c4b5fd;
  --circuit:rgba(56,225,255,.08); --circuit-node:rgba(56,225,255,.18);
  --panel:#0a1120; --line:rgba(56,225,255,.14);
  --liquid-op:.62; --spark-op:.9;
}
[data-theme="light"]{
  --bel-bg:#f4f7fb; --bel-text:#0d1626; --bel-muted:#41566d;
  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(255,255,255,.66); --bel-glass-border:rgba(13,22,38,.14);
  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(13,22,38,.10);
  --acc-cyan:#067a94; --acc-lime:#4d7a00; --acc-coral:#b3372c; --acc-saffron:#8a5a00; --acc-mint:#0a7a52; --acc-violet:#5b21b6;
  --circuit:rgba(13,22,38,.06); --circuit-node:rgba(13,22,38,.12);
  --panel:#ffffff; --line:rgba(13,22,38,.12);
  --liquid-op:.3; --spark-op:.35;
}
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
html{scroll-behavior:smooth}
body{background:var(--bel-bg);color:var(--bel-text);font-family:var(--font-sans);font-size:15.5px;line-height:1.65;overflow-x:hidden;transition:background .4s,color .4s}
::selection{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink)}
:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--bel-cyan);outline-offset:2px;border-radius:6px}
h1,h2,h3,h4{font-family:var(--font-display);line-height:1.15}
.mono{font-family:var(--font-mono)}
img{max-width:100%;display:block}
button{font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer}
input[type="color"]{appearance:none;-webkit-appearance:none;border:none;background:none;width:56px;height:56px;cursor:pointer;padding:0}
input[type="color"]::-webkit-color-swatch-wrapper{padding:0}
input[type="color"]::-webkit-color-swatch{border-radius:14px;border:1px solid var(--line)}
input[type="range"]{accent-color:var(--bel-lime);width:100%}

/* ---------- barra espectro superior ---------- */
.spectrum{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;height:3px;z-index:80;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#38e1ff,#b6ff2e,#ffb52e,#ff6b5e,#a78bfa,#4ef0b0,#38e1ff);background-size:300% 100%;animation:flow 9s linear infinite}

/* ---------- capas vivas: circuitos + aurora cromática + destellos ---------- */
.bg-circuit{position:fixed;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:0;pointer-events:none}
.cl{fill:none;stroke:var(--circuit);stroke-width:1.5}
.cn{fill:var(--circuit-node)}
.liquid{position:fixed;inset:-25%;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;filter:blur(90px) saturate(150%);opacity:var(--liquid-op);animation:huecycle 26s linear infinite}
.blob{position:absolute;border-radius:50%;mix-blend-mode:screen;will-change:transform}
[data-theme="light"] .blob{mix-blend-mode:multiply}
.b1{width:52vmax;height:52vmax;left:-12vmax;top:-14vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 40% 40%,rgba(56,225,255,.6),transparent 62%);animation:drift1 42s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b2{width:46vmax;height:46vmax;right:-10vmax;top:20vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 55% 45%,rgba(182,255,46,.46),transparent 60%);animation:drift2 54s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b3{width:42vmax;height:42vmax;left:16vmax;bottom:-16vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%,rgba(78,240,176,.42),transparent 60%);animation:drift3 48s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b4{width:34vmax;height:34vmax;right:20vmax;bottom:-6vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%,rgba(255,107,94,.32),transparent 62%);animation:drift4 60s ease-in-out infinite alternate}
.b5{width:30vmax;height:30vmax;left:-6vmax;top:36vmax;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%,rgba(167,139,250,.3),transparent 62%);animation:drift2 66s ease-in-out infinite alternate-reverse}
#sparks{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;opacity:var(--spark-op)}
@keyframes drift1{to{transform:translate(9vmax,7vmax) scale(1.15)}}
@keyframes drift2{to{transform:translate(-8vmax,-6vmax) scale(.9)}}
@keyframes drift3{to{transform:translate(7vmax,-8vmax) scale(1.12)}}
@keyframes drift4{to{transform:translate(-9vmax,-5vmax) scale(1.05)}}
@keyframes huecycle{0%{filter:blur(90px) saturate(150%) hue-rotate(0)}50%{filter:blur(90px) saturate(160%) hue-rotate(38deg)}100%{filter:blur(90px) saturate(150%) hue-rotate(0)}}
@keyframes flow{to{background-position:300% 0}}

/* ---------- texto cromático vivo ---------- */
.chroma{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#38e1ff,#b6ff2e,#ffb52e,#ff6b5e,#a78bfa,#4ef0b0,#38e1ff);background-size:300% 100%;-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent;animation:flow 8s linear infinite}
.prismline{height:3px;width:180px;border-radius:99px;margin-top:10px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#38e1ff,#b6ff2e,#ffb52e,#ff6b5e,transparent);background-size:200% 100%;animation:flow 5s linear infinite}

/* ---------- anillo cónico animado ---------- */
.ring{position:relative}
.ring::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:-1px;border-radius:inherit;padding:1.5px;background:conic-gradient(from var(--ang),#38e1ff,#b6ff2e,#ffb52e,#ff6b5e,#a78bfa,#4ef0b0,#38e1ff);-webkit-mask:linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,linear-gradient(#000 0 0);-webkit-mask-composite:xor;mask-composite:exclude;animation:spin 7s linear infinite;pointer-events:none;opacity:.85}
@keyframes spin{to{--ang:360deg}}

/* ---------- glass ---------- */
.glass{background:var(--bel-glass-bg);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(150%);backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(150%);border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);box-shadow:var(--bel-shadow);border-radius:var(--radius)}

/* ---------- nav ---------- */
.nav{position:fixed;top:3px;left:0;right:0;z-index:60;padding:13px 0;transition:.35s}
.nav.scrolled{background:var(--bel-glass-bg);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(150%);backdrop-filter:blur(var(--bel-glass-blur)) saturate(150%);border-bottom:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);padding:8px 0}
.nav-in{max-width:1220px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:16px}
.brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--bel-text)}
.brand b{font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.14em;font-size:15px}
.brand small{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;color:var(--acc-cyan);letter-spacing:.2em}
.nav-links{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none}
.nav-links::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.nav-links a{color:var(--bel-muted);text-decoration:none;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;padding:7px 11px;border-radius:var(--radius-pill);white-space:nowrap;transition:.25s}
.nav-links a:hover{color:var(--acc-cyan);background:rgba(56,225,255,.08)}
.theme-btn{width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);background:var(--bel-glass-bg);color:var(--bel-text);display:grid;place-items:center;transition:.25s}
.theme-btn:hover{border-color:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--acc-lime)}

/* ---------- layout ---------- */
.wrap{max-width:1220px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 22px;position:relative;z-index:1}
section{padding:84px 0 10px;scroll-margin-top:96px}
.kick{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.24em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.kick::before{content:"";width:26px;height:1px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--bel-cyan),var(--bel-lime))}
h2{font-size:clamp(26px,3.4vw,40px);font-weight:700;margin:10px 0 6px;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.lead{color:var(--bel-muted);max-width:780px}
.meta-chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-top:14px}

/* ---------- botones / chips ---------- */
.btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;border-radius:var(--radius-pill);font-weight:600;font-size:14px;padding:13px 24px;text-decoration:none;border:1px solid transparent;transition:.25s;font-family:var(--font-sans)}
.btn-primary{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(182,255,46,.3)}
.btn-primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08);transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 10px 34px rgba(182,255,46,.45)}
.btn-primary:active{transform:translateY(0);filter:brightness(.96)}
.btn-primary:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;transform:none;box-shadow:none}
.btn-ghost{border-color:rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:var(--acc-cyan);background:transparent}
.btn-ghost:hover{background:rgba(56,225,255,.1);transform:translateY(-2px)}
.btn-ghost:active{transform:translateY(0)}
.btn-ghost:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}
.btn-sm{padding:9px 16px;font-size:12.5px}
.chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:var(--acc-cyan);border-radius:var(--radius-pill);padding:6px 14px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase}
.chip.lime{border-color:rgba(182,255,46,.5);color:var(--acc-lime)}
.chip.mint{border-color:rgba(78,240,176,.5);color:var(--acc-mint)}
.chip.coral{border-color:rgba(255,107,94,.5);color:var(--acc-coral)}
.chip.saff{border-color:rgba(255,181,46,.5);color:var(--acc-saffron)}
.chip.violet{border-color:rgba(167,139,250,.5);color:var(--acc-violet)}

/* ---------- hero ---------- */
.hero{padding:150px 0 40px}
.hero-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.05fr .95fr;gap:44px;align-items:center}
.hero h1{font-size:clamp(34px,5vw,62px);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:16px 0 14px}
.hero p{color:var(--bel-muted);max-width:580px}
.hero-cta{display:flex;gap:14px;margin:26px 0 18px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.hero-stats{display:flex;gap:22px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:22px}
.hstat{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;color:var(--bel-muted);letter-spacing:.08em}
.hstat b{display:block;font-size:19px;color:var(--bel-text);font-family:var(--font-display)}
.hero-visual{position:relative}
.glow-lime{position:absolute;inset:-12%;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 55%,rgba(182,255,46,.24),transparent 62%);pointer-events:none;z-index:0}
.hero-frame{position:relative;z-index:1;border-radius:var(--radius-lg);overflow:hidden;box-shadow:var(--bel-shadow)}
.floaty{animation:float 7s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes float{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0)}50%{transform:translateY(-12px)}}
.hero-tag{position:absolute;z-index:2;left:18px;bottom:18px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}

/* ---------- grids / tarjetas ---------- */
.grid{display:grid;gap:18px}
.g2{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
.g3{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}
.g4{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}
.card{padding:22px;border-radius:var(--radius);border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel);transition:.3s}
.card:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);border-color:var(--bel-glass-border)}
.card h4{font-size:15px;margin-bottom:8px}
.card p{font-size:13px;color:var(--bel-muted)}
.card .tag{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase}

/* ---------- swatches vivos ---------- */
.sw{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);cursor:pointer;background:var(--panel);transition:.25s;text-align:left;padding:0;position:relative}
.sw:hover{transform:translateY(-4px);border-color:var(--bel-cyan);box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.3)}
.sw .blk{height:78px;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
.sw .blk::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(115deg,transparent 30%,rgba(255,255,255,.35) 48%,transparent 62%);transform:translateX(-110%);transition:transform .7s ease}
.sw:hover .blk::after{transform:translateX(110%)}
.sw .inf{padding:12px 14px}
.sw .inf b{font-size:13px;display:block}
.sw .inf span{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--bel-muted);display:block;margin-top:2px}
.sw .inf em{font-style:normal;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:var(--acc-lime)}
.ratio-bar{display:flex;height:58px;border-radius:var(--radius-sm);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);margin:18px 0 6px}
.ratio-bar div{display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em;transition:width .3s}
.r60{background:#0b1322;color:#9fb3c8}
.r30{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(56,225,255,.9),rgba(56,225,255,.5));color:#0d1626}
.r10{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#b6ff2e,#d8ff7a);color:#0d1626}

/* ---------- lab cromático ---------- */
.lab-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr;gap:18px;margin-top:24px}
.lab-panel{padding:24px}
.pickers{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.pick{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:center}
.pick span{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--bel-muted);letter-spacing:.1em}
.ratio-big{font-family:var(--font-display);font-size:54px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.02em;line-height:1;margin:14px 0 10px}
.wcag-row{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.badge{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--bel-muted)}
.badge.pass{border-color:rgba(78,240,176,.6);color:var(--acc-mint);background:rgba(78,240,176,.08)}
.badge.fail{border-color:rgba(255,107,94,.6);color:var(--acc-coral);background:rgba(255,107,94,.08)}
.contrast-prev{border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:20px;margin-top:16px;text-align:center;font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:700;font-size:19px;border:1px solid var(--line)}
.slider-row{display:grid;gap:14px;margin-top:8px}
.slider-row label{display:grid;grid-template-columns:90px 1fr 44px;align-items:center;gap:12px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.1em}
.wheel{width:210px;height:210px;border-radius:50%;margin:6px auto;background:conic-gradient(#38e1ff 0 60deg,#4ef0b0 60deg 120deg,#b6ff2e 120deg 180deg,#ffb52e 180deg 240deg,#ff6b5e 240deg 300deg,#a78bfa 300deg 360deg);animation:spinSlow 26s linear infinite;position:relative;box-shadow:0 0 60px rgba(56,225,255,.25)}
.wheel::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:34%;border-radius:50%;background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line)}
@keyframes spinSlow{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}

/* ---------- código ---------- */
.codebox{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;background:var(--panel)}
.codebox header{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);background:rgba(56,225,255,.05)}
.codebox header .fn{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.codebox header button{margin-left:auto}
.codebox pre{padding:16px;overflow:auto;max-height:420px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--bel-text)}

/* ---------- demo glass ---------- */
.demo-stage{position:relative;border-radius:var(--radius-lg);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--bel-glass-border);padding:34px;background:#0b1322}
[data-theme="light"] .demo-stage{background:#eef3f9}
.demo-stage .abs-circ{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
.demo-inner{position:relative;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr;gap:22px}
.gcard{padding:24px}
.gcard .row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:14px}
.gcard .icbox{width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:12px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:rgba(56,225,255,.12);color:var(--acc-cyan);border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.25)}
.gcard h4{color:#e8f1ff}
.gcard .sub{font-size:12px;color:#9fb3c8}
.prog{height:8px;border-radius:99px;background:rgba(56,225,255,.15);overflow:hidden;margin:12px 0 6px}
.prog i{display:block;height:100%;width:72%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--bel-cyan),var(--bel-mint),var(--bel-lime));background-size:200% 100%;animation:flow 4s linear infinite;border-radius:99px}
.btnrow{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}
.state-note{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10.5px;color:#9fb3c8;margin-top:12px;letter-spacing:.06em}

/* ---------- iconos ---------- */
.seg{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius-pill);overflow:hidden}
.seg button{background:transparent;border:0;color:var(--bel-muted);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:11px;padding:8px 16px;letter-spacing:.08em}
.seg button.on{background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);font-weight:600}
.icgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(120px,1fr));gap:14px;margin-top:20px}
.iccard{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);background:var(--panel);padding:18px 10px 12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:10px;transition:.25s;color:var(--bel-text);position:relative;overflow:hidden}
.iccard::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%,rgba(56,225,255,.16),transparent 70%);opacity:0;transition:.3s}
.iccard:hover{border-color:var(--bel-cyan);transform:translateY(-3px);color:var(--acc-cyan)}
.iccard:hover::before{opacity:1}
.ic{width:var(--icon,24px);height:var(--icon,24px);transition:width .25s,height .25s}
.iccard span{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--bel-muted);letter-spacing:.08em}

/* ---------- logos ---------- */
.logo-stage{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:18px}
.logo-cell{border-radius:var(--radius);border:1px solid var(--line);padding:30px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:170px;position:relative}
.logo-cell .lbl{position:absolute;top:10px;left:14px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;color:var(--bel-muted)}
.cell-dark{background:#0b1322}
.cell-light{background:#f4f7fb}
.lockup{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px}
.lockup .wm b{font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.16em;font-size:24px}
.lockup .wm small{display:block;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.3em;color:var(--acc-cyan);margin-top:3px}
.lockup-v{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:10px;text-align:center}
.favrow{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:26px}
.favrow figure{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.favrow figcaption{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--bel-muted)}

/* ---------- fondos ---------- */
.fondos-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:18px}
.fondo-cell{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line)}
.fondo-prev{height:170px;position:relative}
.fondo-prev svg{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
.fondo-meta{padding:14px 16px;background:var(--panel);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.fondo-meta b{font-size:12.5px;font-family:var(--font-mono);font-weight:500}
.fondo-meta button{margin-left:auto}

/* ---------- certificado ---------- */
.cert-wrap{border-radius:var(--radius);overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff}
.cert-wrap.bn svg{filter:grayscale(1) contrast(1.05)}
.cert-bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px}

/* ---------- vida útil ---------- */
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px}
th,td{padding:12px 14px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);vertical-align:top}
th{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.18em;color:var(--bel-muted);text-transform:uppercase}
td .mono{font-size:11.5px}
.st{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid}
.st-stable{color:var(--acc-lime);border-color:rgba(182,255,46,.5)}
.st-beta{color:var(--acc-saffron);border-color:rgba(255,181,46,.5)}
.st-draft{color:var(--acc-cyan);border-color:rgba(56,225,255,.5)}
.tree{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;color:var(--bel-muted);background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:20px;overflow:auto}
.tree b{color:var(--acc-cyan);font-weight:500}
.tree i{color:var(--acc-lime);font-style:normal}
.rule{border-left:3px solid var(--bel-lime);padding:14px 18px;border-radius:0 var(--radius-sm) var(--radius-sm) 0;background:rgba(182,255,46,.06);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12.5px}

/* ---------- reveal / toast / footer ---------- */
.rv{opacity:0;transform:translateY(26px);transition:opacity .7s,transform .7s}
.rv.in{opacity:1;transform:none}
.toast{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:26px;transform:translate(-50%,80px);z-index:99;background:var(--bel-lime);color:var(--bel-lime-ink);font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.06em;padding:11px 22px;border-radius:99px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(182,255,46,.35);transition:transform .35s;pointer-events:none}
.toast.show{transform:translate(-50%,0)}
footer{margin-top:90px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding:44px 0 34px;position:relative;z-index:1}
.foot{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr 1fr 1fr;gap:34px}
.foot h5{font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.2em;color:var(--acc-cyan);text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:12px}
.foot a{color:var(--bel-muted);text-decoration:none;display:block;font-size:12.5px;padding:3px 0}
.foot a:hover{color:var(--acc-lime)}
.foot p{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--bel-muted)}

@media (max-width:980px){
  .hero-grid,.demo-inner,.g3,.g4,.fondos-grid,.logo-stage,.lab-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .g2{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .foot{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .nav-links a{font-size:11.5px;padding:6px 8px}
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
  .blob,.floaty,.rv,.spectrum,.chroma,.prismline,.ring::before,.wheel,.prog i{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}
  #sparks{display:none}
  html{scroll-behavior:auto}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="spectrum" aria-hidden="true"></div>

<!-- defs globales -->
<svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute" aria-hidden="true">
  <defs>
    <linearGradient id="gradCL" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0" stop-color="#38e1ff"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#b6ff2e"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#4ef0b0"/>
    </linearGradient>
    <symbol id="shield" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
      <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </symbol>
    <symbol id="shieldGrad" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
      <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="url(#gradCL)" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
      <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="url(#gradCL)" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>
    </symbol>
    <pattern id="circuitPat" width="512" height="512" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
      <path class="cl" d="M0 64h118l34 34h118v94h66"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M64 512V396l40-40h120"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M512 128H400l-36 36v88"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M256 0v72l40 40h84"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M448 512v-84l-44-44H300"/>
      <path class="cl" d="M0 320h72l44 44v84"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="336" cy="192" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="224" cy="356" r="4"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="404" cy="384" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="296" cy="112" r="4"/>
      <circle class="cn" cx="116" cy="364" r="4"/><circle class="cn" cx="400" cy="128" r="4"/>
    </pattern>
  </defs>
</svg>

<svg class="bg-circuit" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#circuitPat)"/></svg>
<div class="liquid" aria-hidden="true">
  <div class="blob b1"></div><div class="blob b2"></div><div class="blob b3"></div><div class="blob b4"></div><div class="blob b5"></div>
</div>
<canvas id="sparks" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>

<!-- NAV -->
<nav class="nav" id="nav" aria-label="Navegación principal">
  <div class="nav-in">
    <a class="brand" href="#top">
      <svg class="ic" style="width:26px;height:26px" aria-hidden="true"><use href="#shieldGrad"/></svg>
      <span><b>BELENTANI</b><br><small>CHROMA EDITION · v1.1</small></span>
    </a>
    <div class="nav-links">
      <a href="#tokens">02 Tokens</a>
      <a href="#lab">LAB Cromático</a>
      <a href="#componentes">04 Glass</a>
      <a href="#iconos">05 Iconos</a>
      <a href="#logos">01 Logos</a>
      <a href="#fondos">03 Fondos</a>
      <a href="#heroasset">06 Hero</a>
      <a href="#certificado">07 Cert.</a>
      <a href="#vida">Vida útil</a>
    </div>
    <button class="theme-btn" id="themeBtn" aria-label="Cambiar tema claro/oscuro">
      <svg class="ic" style="width:18px;height:18px" id="icMoon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M20 13.5A8 8 0 0 1 10.5 4 8 8 0 1 0 20 13.5Z"/></svg>
      <svg class="ic" style="width:18px;height:18px;display:none" id="icSun" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2.5V5M12 19v2.5M2.5 12H5M19 12h2.5M4.9 4.9 6.7 6.7M17.3 17.3l1.8 1.8M19.1 4.9l-1.8 1.8M6.7 17.3l-1.8 1.8"/></svg>
    </button>
  </div>
</nav>

<main id="top">

<!-- HERO -->
<header class="hero wrap">
  <div class="hero-grid">
    <div class="rv">
      <div class="meta-chips">
        <span class="chip">2026-09-03</span>
        <span class="chip lime">AGPLv3</span>
        <span class="chip violet">EDICIÓN CHROMA · VIVA · CENTELLEANTE</span>
        <span class="chip mint">BELENTANI-DESIGN-SYSTEM.md</span>
      </div>
      <h1>Galería viva de activos.<br><span class="chroma">Espectro cromático líquido</span>, glass y <span style="color:var(--acc-lime)">código primero</span>.</h1>
      <p>Los 7 tipos de activos del Instituto Universal en su forma más luminosa: tokens 60-30-10, glass sobre circuitos, iconos stroke 1.8, logos vectoriales, fondos tileables, hero media y certificado QR. Multinacional, WCAG 2.1 AA, AGPLv3.</p>
      <div class="hero-cta">
        <a class="btn btn-primary" href="#lab">Entrar al LAB cromático</a>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" data-dl="theme">Descargar theme.css</button>
      </div>
      <div class="meta-chips">
        <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span>
        <span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
        <span class="chip mint">LINGUA · ES EN FR PT AR</span>
      </div>
      <div class="hero-stats">
        <div class="hstat"><b>7</b>tipos de activo</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>14</b>iconos stroke 1.8</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>≥4.5:1</b>contraste AA</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>60·30·10</b>regla viva</div>
        <div class="hstat"><b>∞</b>luz líquida</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="hero-visual rv">
      <div class="glow-lime" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="hero-frame ring floaty">
        <img src="https://image.qwenlm.ai/public_source/37ecb3cd-4c0d-4e6e-895e-c2e026a5dbf8/11cde6d88-4903-4cb5-bc5e-7471e39904e6.png" alt="Escudo T iridiscente atravesado por luz líquida espectral sobre navy profundo — hero chromático 1600×900" width="1600" height="900">
      </div>
      <div class="hero-tag">
        <span class="chip">06a · hero-secure-dark CHROMA</span>
        <span class="chip lime">1600×900</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<!-- 02 TOKENS -->
<section id="tokens" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 02 · Paleta + Tokens</div>
    <h2>Tokens de diseño — <span class="chroma">código fuente, no imagen</span></h2>
    <div class="prismline" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <p class="lead">Psicología aplicada: <b style="color:var(--acc-cyan)">navy = confianza</b> · <b style="color:var(--acc-lime)">lime = acción</b> · <b style="color:var(--acc-cyan)">cyan = tecnología</b> · <b style="color:var(--acc-violet)">violeta = IA</b> · <b style="color:var(--acc-coral)">coral = alerta</b>. Click en cualquier muestra copia su token.</p>
  </div>

  <div class="ratio-bar rv" id="ratioBar" role="img" aria-label="Regla 60-30-10 aplicada">
    <div class="r60" id="seg60">60 · NAVY</div><div class="r30" id="seg30">30 · CYAN</div><div class="r10" id="seg10">10 · LIME</div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid g4 rv" id="swatches" style="margin-top:18px"></div>

  <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:26px">
    <div class="codebox rv">
      <header><span class="fn">tokens/belentani-theme.css</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="theme">Copiar / bajar</button></header>
      <pre><code id="codeTheme"></code></pre>
    </div>
    <div class="codebox rv">
      <header><span class="fn">tokens/tailwind.preset.js</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="preset">Copiar / bajar</button></header>
      <pre><code id="codePreset"></code></pre>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid g4 rv" style="margin-top:22px">
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">60-30-10</span><h4>Superficie dominante</h4><p>Navy 60% base; cyan/glass 30% estructura; lime 10% reservado para CTA y logro.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">WCAG 2.1 AA</span><h4>Contraste ≥ 4.5:1</h4><p>Verificado sobre navy y paper. Lime solo con tinta navy encima. Valídalo en el LAB.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">Psicología</span><h4>Confianza · Acción · Tec</h4><p>Navy institucional, lime dopamina de logro, cyan feedback de sistema, violeta para IA.</p></div>
    <div class="card"><span class="tag">Glass</span><h4>Profundidad sin peso</h4><p>blur(18px) saturate(150%) + borde cyan 18%. Nunca sobre texto sin capa sólida.</p></div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- LAB CROMÁTICO -->
<section id="lab" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Extra · Laboratorio vivo</div>
    <h2>LAB cromático — <span class="chroma">contraste y balance en tiempo real</span></h2>
    <div class="prismline" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <p class="lead">Verificador WCAG integrado: elige dos colores y obtén el ratio exacto con pases AA / AAA. Ajusta el balance 60-30-10 y observa la barra respirar.</p>
  </div>

  <div class="lab-grid">
    <div class="glass lab-panel rv">
      <h4 style="margin-bottom:14px">Verificador de contraste WCAG</h4>
      <div class="pickers">
        <div class="pick"><input type="color" id="cFg" value="#e8f1ff" aria-label="Color de texto"><span id="cFgHex">#E8F1FF</span><span>TEXTO</span></div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="swapBtn" aria-label="Intercambiar colores">⇄ invertir</button>
        <div class="pick"><input type="color" id="cBg" value="#0b1322" aria-label="Color de fondo"><span id="cBgHex">#0B1322</span><span>FONDO</span></div>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="resetLab">reset navy/text</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ratio-big" id="ratioNum">—</div>
      <div class="wcag-row" id="wcagBadges">
        <span class="badge" id="bAA">AA NORMAL ≥4.5</span>
        <span class="badge" id="bAAL">AA GRANDE ≥3.0</span>
        <span class="badge" id="bAAA">AAA ≥7.0</span>
      </div>
      <div class="contrast-prev" id="contrastPrev">BELENTANI · Aprender sin barreras</div>
    </div>

    <div class="glass lab-panel rv" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
      <h4 style="margin-bottom:6px">Balance 60-30-10 vivo</h4>
      <div class="slider-row">
        <label>NAVY 60 <input type="range" id="r60" min="20" max="80" value="60" aria-label="Porcentaje navy"><span id="v60">60%</span></label>
        <label>CYAN 30 <input type="range" id="r30" min="10" max="60" value="30" aria-label="Porcentaje cyan"><span id="v30">30%</span></label>
        <label>LIME 10 <input type="range" id="r10" min="5" max="30" value="10" aria-label="Porcentaje lime"><span id="v10">10%</span></label>
      </div>
      <div class="wheel" role="img" aria-label="Rueda cromática BELENTANI en rotación lenta"></div>
      <div class="state-note" style="text-align:center">rueda: cyan→mint→lime→saffron→coral→violeta</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- 04 GLASS -->
<section id="componentes" class="wrap">
  <div class="rv">
    <div class="kick">Tipo 04 · Componentes Glass</div>
    <h2>glass-card · glass-nav · btn-* · badge-chip</h2>
    <div class="prismline" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <p class="lead">Blur real sobre circuitos vivos. Estados hover / active / disabled / focus-visible (anillo cyan 2px — pulsa <span class="mono">Tab</span>).</p>
  </div>

  <div class="demo-stage rv" style="margin-top:26px">
    <svg class="abs-circ" aria-hidden="true"><rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#circuitPat)"/></svg>
    <div class="demo-inner">
      <div class="glass gcard ring">
        <div class="row">
          <div class="icbox"><svg class="ic" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10.2 3.5v5.6L5 17.9a2 2 0 0 0 1.8 3h10.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.8-3L13.8 9.1V3.5"/><path d="M9.8 3.5h4.4"/><path d="M7.3 14.5h9.4"/></svg></div>
          <div><h4>Lab · Ciberseguridad básica</h4><div class="sub">Ruta SECURE-T · Módulo 03 · 25 min</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="prog" role="progressbar" aria-valuenow="72" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><i></i></div>
        <div class="sub" style="font-size:11px;color:#9fb3c8">72% · 3 insignias · racha 6 días · XP ×120</div>
        <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:16px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">Continuar</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm">Ver ruta</button>
        </div>
        <div class="meta-chips" style="margin-top:14px">
          <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span><span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">04a · rgba(13,22,38,.55) · blur 18px · borde cyan .18 · sombra 0 8 32</div>
      </div>
      <div class="glass gcard">
        <h4 style="color:#e8f1ff;margin-bottom:14px">Estados de botón</h4>
        <div class="btnrow">
          <button class="btn btn-primary">btn-primary</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost">btn-ghost</button>
        </div>
        <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:12px">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" disabled>disabled</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" disabled>disabled</button>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">hover ↑ brillo · active ↓ · focus-visible: anillo cyan 2px (Tab)</div>
        <h4 style="color:#e8f1ff;margin:18px 0 12px">badge-chip · radius 99px</h4>
        <div class="meta-chips">
          <span class="chip">100% ONLINE</span>
          <span class="chip lime">APRENDE HACIENDO</span>
          <span class="chip mint">WCAG AA</span>
          <span class="chip saff">XP ×120</span>
          <span class="chip coral">RACHA 6</span>
          <span class="chip violet">AGENTE VOZ</span>
        </div>
        <div class="state-note">04d · outline cyan · texto legible · nunca fondo sólido</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="codebox rv" style="margin-top:22px">
    <header><span class="fn">components/glass.css (extracto oficial)</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-dl="glasscss">Copiar</button></header>
    <pre><code id="codeGlass"></code></pre>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- 05 ICONOS -->
<section id="iconos" class="wrap">

</section>

    </div>

      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="logo-cell cell-light"><span class="lbl" style="color:#41566d">01c · mono negro</span>

      <g transform="translate(540,64) scale(5)" opacity="0.07">
        <path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.8"/>
        <path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>

      <line x1="500" y1="700" x2="820" y2="700" stroke="#0d1626" stroke-width="1.2"/>
      <text x="660" y="724" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Inter, sans-serif" font-size="13" fill="#0d1626" opacity=".8">Mentoría Técnica</text>

└─ assets/

   ├─ <b>fondos/</b>       <i>(03)</i> bg-circuit-dark.svg · bg-grid-light.svg · glow-lime

   ├─ <b>iconos/</b>       <i>(05)</i> set-24px.svg (14 iconos · stroke 1.8 · round)
   ├─ <b>hero/</b>         <i>(06)</i> hero-secure-dark.png 1600×900 (+webp)
   └─ <b>docs/</b>         <i>(07)</i> certificado-base.svg · BELENTANI-DESIGN-SYSTEM.md</pre>
    <div class="rv" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:16px">
      <div class="rule">REGLA: ningún asset sale de HTML puro suelto. Todo vive en <b>open-school/assets/{logos,tokens,fondos,components,iconos,hero,docs}/</b> con README por carpeta.</div>
      <div class="card"><span class="tag">Vida útil</span><h4>Mantenimiento multinacional</h4><p>Los tokens se replican a los 5 repos vía preset; iconos y logos son fuente única; el hero se regenera con IA local por región sin romper el claim; el certificado versiona con la fase de certs.</p></div>

    </div>

<div class="toast" id="toast" role="status" aria-live="polite">Copiado ✓</div>

<script>
/* ================= utilidades ================= */
var $=function(s){return document.querySelector(s)};
var toastT;
function toast(msg){var t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg||'Copiado ✓';t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastT);toastT=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('show')},1600)}
function copyText(txt,msg){if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(function(){toast(msg)}).catch(function(){fallbackCopy(txt,msg)})}else{fallbackCopy(txt,msg)}}
function fallbackCopy(txt,msg){var ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=txt;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();try{document.execCommand('copy');toast(msg)}catch(e){toast('No se pudo copiar')}document.body.removeChild(ta)}
function dl(name,text,mime){var b=new Blob([text],{type:mime||'image/svg+xml'});var u=URL.createObjectURL(b);var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(u)},1200);toast('Descargando '+name)}

/* ================= tema ================= */
var root=document.documentElement;
function setTheme(th){root.setAttribute('data-theme',th);$('#icMoon').style.display=th==='dark'?'':'none';$('#icSun').style.display=th==='light'?'':'none';try{localStorage.setItem('bel-theme',th)}catch(e){}}
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){setTheme(root.getAttribute('data-theme')==='dark'?'light':'dark')});
try{var st=localStorage.getItem('bel-theme');if(st)setTheme(st)}catch(e){}

/* ================= nav scroll + reveal ================= */
window.addEventListener('scroll',function(){$('#nav').classList.toggle('scrolled',window.scrollY>30)},{passive:true});
var io=new IntersectionObserver(function(es){es.forEach(function(e){if(e.isIntersecting){e.target.classList.add('in');io.unobserve(e.target)}})},{threshold:.12});
document.querySelectorAll('.rv').forEach(function(el){io.observe(el)});

/* ================= destellos centelleantes ================= */
(function(){
 var reduced=window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
 if(reduced)return;
 var cv=$('#sparks'),cx=cv.getContext('2d'),W,H,pts=[];
 var COLS=['56,225,255','182,255,46','255,181,46','255,107,94','167,139,250','78,240,176'];
 function size(){W=cv.width=window.innerWidth;H=cv.height=window.innerHeight}
 size();window.addEventListener('resize',size);
 for(var i=0;i<70;i++){pts.push({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),r:Math.random()*1.8+.5,p:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,s:.002+Math.random()*.006,c:COLS[i%COLS.length],vx:(Math.random()-.5)*.00012})}
 function tick(t){
  cx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  for(var i=0;i<pts.length;i++){var p=pts[i];
   p.p+=p.s;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.x<0)p.x=1;if(p.x>1)p.x=0;
   var a=(Math.sin(p.p)+1)/2*.85;
   cx.beginPath();cx.arc(p.x*W,p.y*H,p.r,0,6.283);
   cx.fillStyle='rgba('+p.c+','+a.toFixed(3)+')';cx.fill();
  }
  if(!document.hidden)requestAnimationFrame(tick);else setTimeout(function(){requestAnimationFrame(tick)},800);
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(tick);
})();

/* ================= strings oficiales ================= */
var THEME_CSS='/* BELENTANI DESIGN SYSTEM — THEME v1.1 CHROMA · AGPLv3 · 2026-09-03\n   Teoria: 60-30-10 · WCAG 2.1 AA (>=4.5:1 texto) · glassmorfismo */\n:root{\n  /* 60% superficie */\n  --bel-bg:#0b1322;          /* navy = confianza */\n  --bel-paper:#f4f7fb;\n  --bel-ink:#0d1626;\n  --bel-text:#e8f1ff;\n  --bel-muted:#9fb3c8;\n  /* 30% tecnologia */\n  --bel-cyan:#38e1ff;\n  --bel-mint:#4ef0b0;\n  /* 10% accion */\n  --bel-lime:#b6ff2e;\n  --bel-lime-ink:#0d1626;\n  /* apoyo cromatico */\n  --bel-coral:#ff6b5e;\n  --bel-saffron:#ffb52e;\n  --bel-violet:#a78bfa;\n  /* glass */\n  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(13,22,38,.55);\n  --bel-glass-border:rgba(56,225,255,.18);\n  --bel-glass-blur:18px;\n  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n  /* forma */\n  --radius-sm:10px; --radius:18px; --radius-lg:28px; --radius-pill:99px;\n  /* tipografia */\n  --font-display:"Sora",system-ui,sans-serif;\n  --font-sans:"Inter",system-ui,sans-serif;\n  --font-mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace;\n  /* foco accesible */\n  --focus-ring:0 0 0 2px #38e1ff;\n}\n[data-theme="light"]{\n  --bel-bg:#f4f7fb; --bel-text:#0d1626; --bel-muted:#41566d;\n  --bel-glass-bg:rgba(255,255,255,.66);\n  --bel-glass-border:rgba(13,22,38,.14);\n  --bel-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(13,22,38,.10);\n}';
var TAILWIND_PRESET='/** BELENTANI tailwind preset — AGPLv3 · espejo de belentani-theme.css */\nmodule.exports = {\n  theme: {\n    extend: {\n      colors: {\n        bel: {\n          bg:     \'#0b1322\',\n          paper:  \'#f4f7fb\',\n          ink:    \'#0d1626\',\n          cyan:   \'#38e1ff\',\n          mint:   \'#4ef0b0\',\n          lime:   \'#b6ff2e\',\n          coral:  \'#ff6b5e\',\n          saffron:\'#ffb52e\',\n          violet: \'#a78bfa\',\n        },\n      },\n      fontFamily: {\n        display: [\'Sora\', \'system-ui\', \'sans-serif\'],\n        sans:    [\'Inter\', \'system-ui\', \'sans-serif\'],\n        mono:    [\'"JetBrains Mono"\', \'ui-monospace\', \'monospace\'],\n      },\n      borderRadius: { sm: \'10px\', DEFAULT: \'18px\', lg: \'28px\', pill: \'99px\' },\n      backdropBlur: { glass: \'18px\' },\n      boxShadow: {\n        glass: \'0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35)\',\n        lime:  \'0 6px 24px rgba(182,255,46,.28)\',\n      },\n      borderColor: { glass: \'rgba(56,225,255,.18)\' },\n    },\n  },\n};';
var GLASS_CSS='/* 04a glass-card · dark */\n.glass-card{\n  background:rgba(13,22,38,.55);\n  backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(150%);\n  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(150%);\n  border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.18);\n  box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.35);\n  border-radius:18px;\n}\n/* 04b glass-nav fija + blur al scroll */\n.glass-nav{position:fixed;inset:0 0 auto 0;transition:.35s}\n.glass-nav.scrolled{background:rgba(13,22,38,.55);backdrop-filter:blur(18px) saturate(150%);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.18)}\n/* 04c botones */\n.btn-primary{background:#b6ff2e;color:#0d1626;border-radius:99px}\n.btn-primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08)}\n.btn-primary:active{filter:brightness(.96)}\n.btn-primary:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}\n.btn-ghost{border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:#7de9ff;background:transparent;border-radius:99px}\n.btn-ghost:hover{background:rgba(56,225,255,.1)}\n:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #38e1ff;outline-offset:2px}\n/* 04d badge-chip */\n.badge-chip{border:1px solid rgba(56,225,255,.45);color:#7de9ff;border-radius:99px;\n  padding:6px 14px;font:500 10.5px/1 "JetBrains Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase}';
$('#codeTheme').textContent=THEME_CSS;
$('#codePreset').textContent=TAILWIND_PRESET;
$('#codeGlass').textContent=GLASS_CSS;

/* ================= swatches ================= */
var SW=[
 {n:'bg / navy',v:'--bel-bg',h:'#0b1322',r:'60% SUPERFICIE · CONFIANZA'},
 {n:'cyan',v:'--bel-cyan',h:'#38e1ff',r:'30% TECNOLOGÍA'},
 {n:'lime',v:'--bel-lime',h:'#b6ff2e',r:'10% ACCIÓN'},
 {n:'mint',v:'--bel-mint',h:'#4ef0b0',r:'APOYO · LOGRO'},
 {n:'saffron',v:'--bel-saffron',h:'#ffb52e',r:'APOYO · ENERGÍA'},
 {n:'coral',v:'--bel-coral',h:'#ff6b5e',r:'APOYO · ALERTA'},
 {n:'violeta',v:'--bel-violet',h:'#a78bfa',r:'APOYO · IA'},
 {n:'ink',v:'--bel-ink',h:'#0d1626',r:'TINTA'}
];
$('#swatches').innerHTML=SW.map(function(s){
 return '<button class="sw" data-c="'+s.v+': '+s.h+';" aria-label="Copiar '+s.v+' '+s.h+'">'+
 '<div class="blk" style="background:'+s.h+'"></div>'+
 '<div class="inf"><b>'+s.n+'</b><span>'+s.h+' · '+s.v+'</span><em>'+s.r+'</em></div></button>';
}).join('');
document.querySelectorAll('.sw').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){copyText(b.getAttribute('data-c'),'Token copiado ✓')})});

/* ================= LAB cromático ================= */
function hexToRgb(h){h=h.replace('#','');if(h.length===3)h=h.split('').map(function(c){return c+c}).join('');var n=parseInt(h,16);return[(n>>16)&255,(n>>8)&255,n&255]}
function lum(rgb){var a=rgb.map(function(v){v/=255;return v<=.03928?v/12.92:Math.pow((v+.055)/1.055,2.4)});return .2126*a[0]+.7152*a[1]+.0722*a[2]}
function ratio(f,b){var l1=lum(hexToRgb(f)),l2=lum(hexToRgb(b));var hi=Math.max(l1,l2),lo=Math.min(l1,l2);return(hi+.05)/(lo+.05)}
function setBadge(el,pass){el.classList.toggle('pass',pass);el.classList.toggle('fail',!pass)}
function updateLab(){
 var fg=$('#cFg').value,bg=$('#cBg').value,r=ratio(fg,bg);
 $('#cFgHex').textContent=fg.toUpperCase();$('#cBgHex').textContent=bg.toUpperCase();
 $('#ratioNum').textContent=r.toFixed(2)+' : 1';
 $('#ratioNum').style.color=r>=4.5?'var(--acc-mint)':(r>=3?'var(--acc-saffron)':'var(--acc-coral)');
 setBadge($('#bAA'),r>=4.5);setBadge($('#bAAL'),r>=3);setBadge($('#bAAA'),r>=7);
 var pv=$('#contrastPrev');pv.style.background=bg;pv.style.color=fg;
}
$('#cFg').addEventListener('input',updateLab);
$('#cBg').addEventListener('input',updateLab);
$('#swapBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){var f=$('#cFg').value;$('#cFg').value=$('#cBg').value;$('#cBg').value=f;updateLab()});
$('#resetLab').addEventListener('click',function(){$('#cFg').value='#e8f1ff';$('#cBg').value='#0b1322';updateLab()});
updateLab();

function updateRatioBar(){
 var a=+$('#r60').value,b=+$('#r30').value,c=+$('#r10').value,tot=a+b+c;
 $('#seg60').style.width=(a/tot*100)+'%';$('#seg30').style.width=(b/tot*100)+'%';$('#seg10').style.width=(c/tot*100)+'%';
 $('#seg60').textContent=a+' · NAVY';$('#seg30').textContent=b+' · CYAN';$('#seg10').textContent=c+' · LIME';
 $('#v60').textContent=a+'%';$('#v30').textContent=b+'%';$('#v10').textContent=c+'%';
}
['r60','r30','r10'].forEach(function(id){$('#'+id).addEventListener('input',updateRatioBar)});
updateRatioBar();

/* ================= iconos ================= */
var ICONS=[
 {k:'home',p:'<path d="M3 11.5 12 4l9 7.5"/><path d="M5.5 9.8V20h13V9.8"/><path d="M10 20v-5.2h4V20"/>'},
 {k:'cursos',p:'<path d="M6.5 3H20v14.2H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 4 19.7V5.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 3Z"/><path d="M4 19.7a2.3 2.3 0 0 1 2.3-2.3H20"/><path d="M9 7.5h7"/>'},
 {k:'labs',p:'<path d="M9.8 3.5h4.4"/><path d="M10.2 3.5v5.6L5 17.9a2 2 0 0 0 1.8 3h10.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.8-3L13.8 9.1V3.5"/><path d="M7.3 14.5h9.4"/>'},
 {k:'perfil',p:'<circle cx="12" cy="8.2" r="3.6"/><path d="M4.8 20c.9-3.5 3.8-5.3 7.2-5.3s6.3 1.8 7.2 5.3"/>'},
 {k:'escudo',p:'<path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z"/><path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7"/>'},
 {k:'candado',p:'<rect x="5" y="10.5" width="14" height="9.5" rx="2.2"/><path d="M8.3 10.5V7.7a3.7 3.7 0 0 1 7.4 0v2.8"/><circle cx="12" cy="15.2" r="1.2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'},
 {k:'cerebro-ia',p:'<rect x="6.5" y="6.5" width="11" height="11" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/><path d="M9.5 6.5V4M14.5 6.5V4M9.5 20v-2.5M14.5 20v-2.5M6.5 9.5H4M6.5 14.5H4M20 9.5h-2.5M20 14.5h-2.5"/>'},
 {k:'nube',p:'<path d="M7.2 18.5h9.3a3.7 3.7 0 0 0 .5-7.37A5.6 5.6 0 0 0 6.3 9.4a4.5 4.5 0 0 0 .9 9.1Z"/>'},
 {k:'terminal',p:'<rect x="3.5" y="4.5" width="17" height="15" rx="2"/><path d="m7 9.5 3 2.7-3 2.7"/><path d="M12.5 15H17"/>'},
 {k:'trofeo',p:'<path d="M8 4.5h8v4.7a4 4 0 0 1-8 0Z"/><path d="M8 5.5H4.8v.8A3.2 3.2 0 0 0 8 9.5M16 5.5h3.2v.8A3.2 3.2 0 0 1 16 9.5"/><path d="M12 13.2v3"/><path d="M9.6 16.2h4.8l.6 3.8H9Z"/><path d="M8.8 20h6.4"/>'},
 {k:'racha',p:'<path d="M12.2 3.8c.9 2.6 3.9 4.2 4.3 7.6a4.6 4.6 0 0 1-9.1.5c0-2 1-3.5 2-5 .5 1.1 1 1.7 1.9 2.1.2-2 .1-3.5.9-5.2Z"/>'},
 {k:'xp',p:'<path d="m12 3.5 2.6 5.3 5.9.9-4.3 4.1 1 5.8-5.2-2.7-5.2 2.7 1-5.8-4.3-4.1 5.9-.9Z"/>'},
 {k:'check',p:'<path d="m4.5 12.6 5 5L19.5 6.5"/>'},
 {k:'alerta',p:'<path d="M10.3 4.6 3.1 17.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.7 3h14.4a2 2 0 0 0 1.7-3L13.7 4.6a2 2 0 0 0-3.4 0Z"/><path d="M12 9.5v4.4"/><circle cx="12" cy="16.9" r="1" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'}
];
function iconSvg(ic){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="ic">'+ic.p+'</svg>'}
$('#iconGrid').innerHTML=ICONS.map(function(ic){
 return '<button class="iccard" data-k="'+ic.k+'" aria-label="Copiar SVG de '+ic.k+'">'+iconSvg(ic)+'<span>'+ic.k+'</span></button>';
}).join('');
document.querySelectorAll('.iccard').forEach(function(b){
 b.addEventListener('click',function(){
  var ic=ICONS.filter(function(x){return x.k===b.getAttribute('data-k')})[0];
  copyText(iconSvg(ic).replace(' class="ic"',''),'SVG '+ic.k+' copiado ✓');
 });
});
document.querySelectorAll('.seg [data-size]').forEach(function(btn){
 btn.addEventListener('click',function(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.seg [data-size]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('on')});
  btn.classList.add('on');
  $('#iconGrid').style.setProperty('--icon',btn.getAttribute('data-size')+'px');
 });
});

/* ================= fondos ================= */
(function(){
 var g='';for(var i=32;i<512;i+=32){g+='<path d="M'+i+' 0V512M0 '+i+'H512"/>'}
 $('#gridPrev').innerHTML='<g fill="none" stroke="rgba(13,22,38,.07)" stroke-width="1">'+g+'</g>';
})();
var CIRCUIT_SVG='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><g fill="none" stroke="rgba(56,225,255,.08)" stroke-width="1.5"><path d="M0 64h118l34 34h118v94h66"/><path d="M64 512V396l40-40h120"/><path d="M512 128H400l-36 36v88"/><path d="M256 0v72l40 40h84"/><path d="M448 512v-84l-44-44H300"/><path d="M0 320h72l44 44v84"/></g><g fill="rgba(56,225,255,.18)"><circle cx="336" cy="192" r="4"/><circle cx="224" cy="356" r="4"/><circle cx="404" cy="384" r="4"/><circle cx="296" cy="112" r="4"/><circle cx="116" cy="364" r="4"/><circle cx="400" cy="128" r="4"/></g></svg>';
var GRID_SVG=(function(){var g='';for(var i=32;i<512;i+=32){g+='<path d="M'+i+' 0V512M0 '+i+'H512"/>'}return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512"><g fill="none" stroke="rgba(13,22,38,.07)" stroke-width="1">'+g+'</g></svg>'})();
var GLOW_CSS='/* 03c glow-lime · radial 20% · reutilizable en hero CTA */\n.glow-lime{position:absolute;inset:-12%;pointer-events:none;\n  background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 55%, rgba(182,255,46,.20), transparent 62%);}';

/* ================= logos ================= */
function shieldStr(stroke){return '<path d="M12 2.5l7.5 3v6.2c0 4.9-3.2 8.1-7.5 9.8-4.3-1.7-7.5-4.9-7.5-9.8V5.5Z" fill="none" stroke="'+stroke+'" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M8.6 8.2h6.8M12 8.2v7" fill="none" stroke="'+stroke+'" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/>'}
var GRAD='<defs><linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#38e1ff"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#b6ff2e"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#4ef0b0"/></linearGradient></defs>';
function logoH(stroke,txt,sub){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="470" height="120" viewBox="0 0 470 120">'+(stroke.indexOf('url')===0?GRAD:'')+'<g transform="translate(16,18) scale(3.5)">'+shieldStr(stroke)+'</g><text x="122" y="66" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="38" letter-spacing="6" fill="'+txt+'">BELENTANI</text><text x="124" y="94" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="13" letter-spacing="4" fill="'+sub+'">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</text></svg>'}
function logoV(stroke,txt,sub){return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="300" height="210" viewBox="0 0 300 210">'+(stroke.indexOf('url')===0?GRAD:'')+'<g transform="translate(108,14) scale(3.5)">'+shieldStr(stroke)+'</g><text x="150" y="150" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Sora, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="30" letter-spacing="7" fill="'+txt+'">BELENTANI</text><text x="150" y="180" text-anchor="middle" font-family="JetBrains Mono, monospace" font-size="11" letter-spacing="3" fill="'+sub+'">SECURE-T · UX · LINGUA · MANOS</text></svg>'}
var FAVICON_SVG='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="32" height="32" viewBox="0 0 24 24">'+GRAD+shieldStr('url(#g)')+'</svg>';
function faviconPng(cb){
 var c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=32;c.height=32;var x=c.getContext('2d');
 var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,32,32);g.addColorStop(0,'#38e1ff');g.addColorStop(.5,'#b6ff2e');g.addColorStop(1,'#4ef0b0');
 x.scale(32/24,32/24);x.strokeStyle=g;x.lineWidth=1.8;x.lineCap='round';x.lineJoin='round';
 x.stroke(new Path2D('M12 2.5 L19.5 5.5 V11.7 C19.5 16.6 16.3 19.8 12 21.5 C7.7 19.8 4.5 16.6 4.5 11.7 V5.5 Z'));
 x.stroke(new Path2D('M8.6 8.2 H15.4 M12 8.2 V15.2'));
 cb(c.toDataURL('image/png'));
}

/* ================= QR determinista ================= */
function mulberry32(a){return function(){a|=0;a=a+0x6D2B79F5|0;var t=Math.imul(a^a>>>15,1|a);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296}}
(function(){
 var rnd=mulberry32(20260903),N=25,S=8,out='';
 function inFinder(cx,cy){return (cx<7&&cy<7)||(cx>=N-7&&cy<7)||(cx<7&&cy>=N-7)}
 function finder(fx,fy){var s='';for(var y=0;y<7;y++)for(var x=0;x<7;x++){var b=(x===0||x===6||y===0||y===6)||(x>=2&&x<=4&&y>=2&&y<=4);if(b)s+='<rect x="'+((fx+x)*S)+'" y="'+((fy+y)*S)+'" width="'+S+'" height="'+S+'"/>'}return s}
 for(var y=0;y<N;y++)for(var x=0;x<N;x++){if(inFinder(x,y))continue;if(rnd()>0.52)out+='<rect x="'+(x*S)+'" y="'+(y*S)+'" width="'+S+'" height="'+S+'"/>'}
 out=finder(0,0)+finder(N-7,0)+finder(0,N-7)+out;
 $('#certQR').innerHTML='<g fill="#0d1626">'+out+'</g>';
})();

/* ================= certificado toggles ================= */
$('#certColor').addEventListener('click',function(){$('#certWrap').classList.remove('bn');$('#certColor').classList.add('on');$('#certBN').classList.remove('on')});
$('#certBN').addEventListener('click',function(){$('#certWrap').classList.add('bn');$('#certBN').classList.add('on');$('#certColor').classList.remove('on')});

/* ================= descargas ================= */
var DL={
 theme:function(){dl('belentani-theme.css',THEME_CSS,'text/css')},
 preset:function(){dl('tailwind.preset.js',TAILWIND_PRESET,'text/javascript')},
 glasscss:function(){copyText(GLASS_CSS,'glass.css copiado ✓')},
 circuit:function(){dl('bg-circuit-dark.svg',CIRCUIT_SVG)},
 grid:function(){dl('bg-grid-light.svg',GRID_SVG)},
 glowcss:function(){copyText(GLOW_CSS,'glow-lime CSS copiado ✓')},
 logoh:function(){dl('logo-madre.svg',logoH('url(#g)','#e8f1ff','#38e1ff'))},
 logov:function(){dl('logo-stacked.svg',logoV('url(#g)','#e8f1ff','#38e1ff'))},
 monow:function(){dl('logo-mono-blanco.svg',logoH('#ffffff','#ffffff','rgba(255,255,255,.8)'))},
 monok:function(){dl('logo-mono-negro.svg',logoH('#0d1626','#0d1626','rgba(13,22,38,.75)'))},
 favicon:function(){dl('favicon.svg',FAVICON_SVG)},
 faviconpng:function(){faviconPng(function(u){var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download='favicon-32.png';a.click();toast('Descargando favicon-32.png')})},
 cert:function(){dl('certificado-base.svg','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n'+$('#certSvg').outerHTML)}
};
document.querySelectorAll('[data-dl]').forEach(function(b){
 b.addEventListener('click',function(){var f=DL[b.getAttribute('data-dl')];if(f)f()});
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
