<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,maximum-scale=1,user-scalable=no">
<title>ArquiCAD Pro</title>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Space+Grotesk:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
:root{--bg:#0A0A0A;--surface:#131313;--card:#1C1C1C;--card2:#252525;--border:#282828;--border2:#363636;--fg:#EAEAEA;--fg2:#999;--fg3:#5A5A5A;--accent:#FF6B2B;--accent2:#FF8C55;--accent3:rgba(255,107,43,.12);--green:#22C55E;--red:#EF4444;--blue:#3B82F6;--yellow:#FBBF24;--purple:#A855F7;--cyan:#06B6D4;--pink:#EC4899;--radius:10px;--font:'Space Grotesk',sans-serif;--mono:'JetBrains Mono',monospace}
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
body{font-family:var(--font);background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);overflow:hidden;height:100dvh;width:100vw;position:fixed;top:0;left:0}
::-webkit-scrollbar{width:4px}::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:transparent}::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:var(--border2);border-radius:4px}
#splash{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;background:var(--bg);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:opacity .5s,transform .5s}
#splash.hide{opacity:0;transform:scale(1.05);pointer-events:none}
.slogo{width:76px;height:76px;border:2.5px solid var(--accent);border-radius:16px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:30px;color:var(--accent);animation:pg 1.5s ease infinite}
@keyframes pg{0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(255,107,43,.35)}50%{box-shadow:0 0 28px 8px rgba(255,107,43,.12)}}
#splash h1{margin-top:14px;font-size:20px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px}
#splash p{color:var(--fg3);font-size:11px;margin-top:3px;letter-spacing:1px}
.sbar{width:110px;height:3px;background:var(--border);border-radius:3px;margin-top:20px;overflow:hidden}
.sbar-f{height:100%;background:var(--accent);border-radius:3px;animation:sload 1.6s ease forwards}
@keyframes sload{0%{width:0}60%{width:80%}100%{width:100%}}
#app{display:none;height:100dvh;flex-direction:column}
#app.show{display:flex}
header{height:46px;background:var(--surface);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;padding:0 6px;gap:2px;flex-shrink:0;z-index:50}
header .title{flex:1;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;padding:0 6px}
header button{width:34px;height:34px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:all .12s}
header button:active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent)}
.hsep{width:1px;height:22px;background:var(--border);margin:0 1px}
.main-area{flex:1;display:flex;overflow:hidden;position:relative}
#toolstrip{width:42px;background:var(--surface);border-right:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:3px 0;gap:1px;overflow-y:auto;flex-shrink:0;z-index:40}
#toolstrip button{width:34px;height:34px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:all .12s;position:relative}
#toolstrip button:active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent)}
#toolstrip button.active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent);box-shadow:inset 2px 0 0 var(--accent)}
#toolstrip .sep{width:22px;height:1px;background:var(--border);margin:1px 0}
.cwrap{flex:1;position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:#0C0C0C}
#cad{display:block;touch-action:none;cursor:crosshair}
#coords{position:absolute;bottom:5px;left:5px;background:rgba(10,10,10,.88);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:2px 7px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);pointer-events:none;z-index:10}
#zoom-ind{position:absolute;bottom:5px;right:5px;background:rgba(10,10,10,.88);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:2px 7px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;color:var(--accent);pointer-events:none;z-index:10}
#tool-hint{position:absolute;top:5px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);background:rgba(10,10,10,.9);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:10px;color:var(--fg2);pointer-events:none;z-index:10;white-space:nowrap;transition:opacity .3s}
#bnav{height:50px;background:var(--surface);border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;z-index:50}
#bnav button{flex:1;height:100%;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);font-size:9px;font-family:var(--font);cursor:pointer;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:2px;transition:all .12s;position:relative}
#bnav button i{font-size:15px;transition:all .12s}
#bnav button:active,#bnav button.active{color:var(--accent)}
#bnav button.active i{transform:scale(1.12)}
#bnav button.active::before{content:'';position:absolute;top:0;width:28px;height:2px;background:var(--accent);border-radius:0 0 2px 2px}
.panel{position:fixed;bottom:50px;left:0;right:0;background:var(--surface);border-top:1px solid var(--border);z-index:45;transform:translateY(100%);transition:transform .28s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);max-height:62dvh;overflow-y:auto;border-radius:14px 14px 0 0}
.panel.open{transform:translateY(0)}
.phandle{width:32px;height:3px;background:var(--border2);border-radius:3px;margin:7px auto 0}
.phead{padding:8px 14px 10px;display:flex;align-items:center}
.phead h3{font-size:14px;font-weight:600;flex:1}
.phead button{width:30px;height:30px;border:none;background:var(--card);color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.pbody{padding:0 14px 16px}
.pr{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;margin-bottom:9px}
.pr label{font-size:10px;color:var(--fg2);width:68px;flex-shrink:0}
.pr input[type=number],.pr input[type=text],.pr select{flex:1;height:32px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 9px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--font);font-size:11px;outline:none}
.pr input:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.pr input[type=color]{width:32px;height:32px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;background:var(--card);padding:2px;cursor:pointer}
.pr input[type=range]{flex:1;accent-color:var(--accent)}
.pr .val{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--accent);width:32px;text-align:right}
.tog{width:36px;height:20px;background:var(--border2);border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;position:relative;transition:background .2s;flex-shrink:0}
.tog.on{background:var(--accent)}
.tog::after{content:'';position:absolute;top:2px;left:2px;width:16px;height:16px;background:#fff;border-radius:50%;transition:transform .2s}
.tog.on::after{transform:translateX(16px)}
.btn{height:32px;padding:0 14px;border:none;border-radius:7px;font-family:var(--font);font-size:11px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;transition:all .12s}
.btn:active{transform:scale(.96)}
.btn-a{background:var(--accent);color:#fff}
.btn-o{background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--fg2)}
.btn-d{background:var(--red);color:#fff}
.btn-s{height:28px;padding:0 9px;font-size:10px}
.li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:7px 9px;background:var(--card);border-radius:7px;margin-bottom:5px;border:1px solid transparent;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.li.active{border-color:var(--accent);background:var(--accent3)}
.li .ln{flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500}
.li button{width:26px;height:26px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.li button:active{color:var(--accent)}
#usearch{width:100%;height:35px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 11px 0 32px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--font);font-size:12px;outline:none}
#usearch:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.swrap{position:relative;margin-bottom:9px}
.swrap i{position:absolute;left:10px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);color:var(--fg3);font-size:12px}
.cscroll{display:flex;gap:5px;overflow-x:auto;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:8px}
.cscroll::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.cchip{height:26px;padding:0 10px;border-radius:13px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);font-size:10px;font-family:var(--font);cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;transition:all .12s}
.cchip.active{background:var(--accent);border-color:var(--accent);color:#fff}
.ugrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:7px}
.ucard{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:10px 5px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.ucard:active{transform:scale(.95);border-color:var(--accent)}
.ucard i{font-size:16px;margin-bottom:5px;display:block}
.ucard span{font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);line-height:1.25;display:block}
.mover{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.65);z-index:100;display:none;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:center}
.mover.open{display:flex}
.modal{background:var(--surface);border-radius:14px 14px 0 0;width:100%;max-height:78dvh;overflow-y:auto;animation:mup .25s ease}
@keyframes mup{from{transform:translateY(100%)}to{transform:translateY(0)}}
.mhead{display:flex;align-items:center;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--surface);z-index:1}
.mhead h3{flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600}
.mhead button{width:30px;height:30px;border:none;background:var(--card);color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px}
.mbody{padding:14px}
.ci{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;margin-bottom:10px}
.ci label{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg2);width:82px;flex-shrink:0}
.ci input{flex:1;height:38px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 9px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;outline:none}
.ci input:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.ci .unit{font-size:10px;color:var(--fg3);width:28px;text-align:right}
.cbtn{width:100%;height:40px;background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:9px;font-family:var(--font);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin:6px 0 14px;transition:all .12s}
.cbtn:active{transform:scale(.98);background:var(--accent2)}
.rbox{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:12px}
.rrow{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.rrow:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.rrow .rl{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg2)}
.rrow .rv{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--accent);font-weight:600;text-align:right;max-width:55%;word-break:break-all}
.ecard{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:7px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.ecard:active{border-color:var(--accent);background:var(--accent3)}
.ecard .eicon{width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:9px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:16px;flex-shrink:0}
.ecard .einfo h4{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:1px}
.ecard .einfo p{font-size:9px;color:var(--fg3)}
.scanarea{background:var(--card);border:2px dashed var(--border2);border-radius:10px;padding:28px 16px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:14px;cursor:pointer}
.scanarea i{font-size:32px;color:var(--fg3);margin-bottom:8px;display:block}
.scanarea p{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg3)}
#scan-cv{width:100%;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:10px;display:none;background:#000}
.fgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:7px;margin-top:8px}
.fbtn{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:9px 3px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);transition:all .12s}
.fbtn:active{border-color:var(--accent);color:var(--accent)}
.fbtn i{font-size:16px;display:block;margin-bottom:3px}
#toast-c{position:fixed;top:52px;right:10px;z-index:200;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px}
.toast{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:11px;color:var(--fg);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;animation:tin .25s ease;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4);max-width:260px}
.toast.out{animation:tout .25s ease forwards}
@keyframes tin{from{opacity:0;transform:translateX(30px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateX(0)}}
@keyframes tout{to{opacity:0;transform:translateX(30px)}}
.toast i{font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0}
.toast.success i{color:var(--green)}.toast.error i{color:var(--red)}.toast.info i{color:var(--blue)}
.sep-t{border-top:1px solid var(--border);margin:10px 0;padding-top:10px}
@media(min-width:600px){.ugrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}.fgrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(6,1fr)}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="splash"><div class="slogo"><i class="fas fa-drafting-compass"></i></div><h1>ARQUICAD PRO</h1><p>CAD de Arquitectura Movil</p><div class="sbar"><div class="sbar-f"></div></div></div>
<div id="app">
<header>
<button onclick="toast('Menu en desarrollo','info')"><i class="fas fa-bars"></i></button>
<span class="title" id="ptitle">Proyecto Sin Titulo</span>
<div class="hsep"></div>
<button onclick="cad.undo()" title="Deshacer"><i class="fas fa-undo"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad.redo()" title="Rehacer"><i class="fas fa-redo"></i></button>
<div class="hsep"></div>
<button onclick="cad.zoomIn()"><i class="fas fa-search-plus"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad.zoomOut()"><i class="fas fa-search-minus"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad.resetView()"><i class="fas fa-expand"></i></button>
</header>
<div class="main-area">
<div id="toolstrip">
<button data-tool="select" class="active" title="Seleccionar"><i class="fas fa-mouse-pointer"></i></button>
<button data-tool="move" title="Mover vista"><i class="fas fa-hand"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="line" title="Linea"><i class="fas fa-minus"></i></button>
<button data-tool="rect" title="Rectangulo"><i class="far fa-square"></i></button>
<button data-tool="circle" title="Circulo"><i class="far fa-circle"></i></button>
<button data-tool="arc" title="Arco"><i class="fas fa-bezier-curve"></i></button>
<button data-tool="polyline" title="Polilinea"><i class="fas fa-draw-polygon"></i></button>
<button data-tool="freehand" title="Mano alzada"><i class="fas fa-pencil"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="wall" title="Muro"><i class="fas fa-grip-lines-vertical"></i></button>
<button data-tool="door" title="Puerta"><i class="fas fa-door-open"></i></button>
<button data-tool="window" title="Ventana"><i class="fas fa-window-maximize"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="dimension" title="Cota"><i class="fas fa-ruler-horizontal"></i></button>
<button data-tool="text" title="Texto"><i class="fas fa-font"></i></button>
<button data-tool="furniture" title="Mobiliario"><i class="fas fa-couch"></i></button>
<button data-tool="symbol" title="Simbolos"><i class="fas fa-bolt"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="erase" title="Borrar"><i class="fas fa-eraser"></i></button>
</div>
<div class="cwrap" id="cwrap"><canvas id="cad"></canvas><div id="coords">X: 0.0 Y: 0.0</div><div id="zoom-ind">100%</div><div id="tool-hint">Seleccionar objeto</div></div>
</div>
<nav id="bnav">
<button data-panel="dibujo" class="active"><i class="fas fa-pen-ruler"></i><span>Dibujo</span></button>
<button data-panel="capas"><i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i><span>Capas</span></button>
<button data-panel="utilidades"><i class="fas fa-calculator"></i><span>Utilidades</span></button>
<button data-panel="exportar"><i class="fas fa-file-export"></i><span>Exportar</span></button>
<button data-panel="escanear"><i class="fas fa-camera"></i><span>Escanear</span></button>
</nav>
<div class="panel" id="panel-dibujo"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Propiedades de Dibujo</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="pr"><label>Color linea</label><input type="color" id="p-col" value="#FF6B2B"><input type="text" id="p-col-t" value="#FF6B2B" style="flex:1" oninput="document.getElementById('p-col').value=this.value;cad.strokeColor=this.value"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Grosor</label><input type="range" id="p-w" min="1" max="20" value="2" oninput="cad.lineWidth=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-wv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-wv">2</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Relleno</label><div class="tog" id="p-fill" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.fillEnabled=this.classList.contains('on')"></div><input type="color" id="p-fillc" value="#FF6B2B" oninput="cad.fillColor=this.value"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Grosor muro</label><input type="range" id="p-wt" min="8" max="40" value="15" oninput="cad.wallThickness=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-wtv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-wtv">15</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Tam. texto</label><input type="range" id="p-fs" min="8" max="72" value="16" oninput="cad.fontSize=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-fsv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-fsv">16</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Texto</label><input type="text" id="p-tc" value="Texto" oninput="cad.textContent=this.value"></div>
<div class="sep-t">
<div class="pr"><label>Cuadricula</label><div class="tog on" id="p-grid" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.showGrid=this.classList.contains('on');cad.dirty=true;cad.render()"></div></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Ajuste grid</label><div class="tog on" id="p-snap" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.snapEnabled=this.classList.contains('on')"></div></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Tam. grid</label><input type="range" id="p-gs" min="5" max="50" value="20" oninput="cad.gridSize=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-gsv').textContent=this.value;cad.dirty=true;cad.render()"><span class="val" id="p-gsv">20</span></div>
</div>
<div class="sep-t"><p style="font-size:10px;color:var(--fg3);margin-bottom:7px">ACCIONES</p><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap">
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.clearAll()"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Limpiar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.deleteSelected()"><i class="fas fa-times"></i> Eliminar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.duplicateSelected()"><i class="fas fa-clone"></i> Duplicar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.finishPolyline()"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Cerrar poli.</button>
</div></div>
</div></div>
<div class="panel" id="panel-capas"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Capas</h3><button onclick="addLayer()"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i></button></div><div class="pbody" id="layers-list"></div></div>
<div class="panel" id="panel-utilidades"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>200 Utilidades</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="swrap"><i class="fas fa-search"></i><input type="text" id="usearch" placeholder="Buscar utilidad..."></div>
<div class="cscroll" id="cat-chips"></div>
<div class="ugrid" id="ugrid"></div>
</div></div>
<div class="panel" id="panel-exportar"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Exportar y Convertir</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportPNG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.12);color:var(--red)"><i class="fas fa-image"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar PNG</h4><p>Imagen de alta resolucion</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportJPG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(251,191,36,.12);color:var(--yellow)"><i class="fas fa-file-image"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar JPG</h4><p>Imagen comprimida</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportSVG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(34,197,94,.12);color:var(--green)"><i class="fas fa-vector-square"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar SVG</h4><p>Vector escalable</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportJSON()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(59,130,246,.12);color:var(--blue)"><i class="fas fa-code"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Guardar Proyecto JSON</h4><p>Formato nativo reabrible</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="importJSON()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(6,182,212,.12);color:var(--cyan)"><i class="fas fa-file-import"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Importar Proyecto JSON</h4><p>Cargar archivo guardado</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportDXF()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(168,85,247,.12);color:var(--purple)"><i class="fas fa-file-lines"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar DXF texto</h4><p>Compatible con AutoCAD</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportCSV()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(148,163,184,.12);color:#94A3B8"><i class="fas fa-table"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar CSV</h4><p>Lista de objetos con coordenadas</p></div></div>
</div></div>
<div class="panel" id="panel-escanear"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Escanear y Reconocer</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="scanarea" onclick="openScanner()"><i class="fas fa-camera-retro"></i><p>Toca para capturar o carga imagen</p></div>
<canvas id="scan-cv"></canvas>
<div class="pr"><label>Brillo</label><input type="range" id="sc-br" min="-100" max="100" value="0" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Contraste</label><input type="range" id="sc-co" min="-100" max="100" value="30" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Umbral</label><input type="range" id="sc-th" min="0" max="255" value="128" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div style="display:flex;gap:7px;margin-top:10px">
<button class="btn btn-o" onclick="applyScan()" style="flex:1"><i class="fas fa-sliders-h"></i> Filtros</button>
<button class="btn btn-a" onclick="vectorizeScan()" style="flex:1"><i class="fas fa-magic"></i> Vectorizar</button>
</div>
<button class="btn btn-o" style="width:100%;margin-top:7px" onclick="document.getElementById('sc-file').click()"><i class="fas fa-folder-open"></i> Cargar galeria</button>
<input type="file" id="sc-file" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="loadScanFile(event)">
</div></div>
</div>
<div class="mover" id="util-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3 id="um-title">Calculadora</h3><button onclick="closeM('util-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody" id="um-body"></div></div></div>
<div class="mover" id="furn-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3>Insertar Mobiliario</h3><button onclick="closeM('furn-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody"><div class="fgrid" id="furn-g"></div></div></div></div>
<div class="mover" id="sym-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3>Simbolos Arquitectonicos</h3><button onclick="closeM('sym-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody"><div class="fgrid" id="sym-g"></div></div></div></div>
<div id="toast-c"></div>
<input type="file" id="json-in" accept=".json" style="display:none" onchange="handleJSONImport(event)">
</body>
<script>
/* ===== CATEGORIAS ===== */
const CATS=[
{id:'medicion',name:'Medicion',icon:'fa-ruler-combined',color:'#FF6B2B'},
{id:'areas',name:'Areas',icon:'fa-vector-square',color:'#22C55E'},
{id:'volumenes',name:'Volumenes',icon:'fa-cube',color:'#3B82F6'},
{id:'materiales',name:'Materiales',icon:'fa-bricks',color:'#F59E0B'},
{id:'estructural',name:'Estructural',icon:'fa-building',color:'#EF4444'},
{id:'electrico',name:'Electrico',icon:'fa-bolt',color:'#A855F7'},
{id:'fontaneria',name:'Fontaneria',icon:'fa-faucet-drip',color:'#06B6D4'},
{id:'climatizacion',name:'HVAC',icon:'fa-temperature-half',color:'#F97316'},
{id:'iluminacion',name:'Iluminacion',icon:'fa-lightbulb',color:'#FBBF24'},
{id:'conversion',name:'Conversiones',icon:'fa-arrows-rotate',color:'#6366F1'},
{id:'geometria',name:'Geometria',icon:'fa-compass-drafting',color:'#EC4899'},
{id:'costos',name:'Costos',icon:'fa-coins',color:'#14B8A6'},
{id:'escalas',name:'Escalas',icon:'fa-maximize',color:'#8B5CF6'},
{id:'terreno',name:'Terreno',icon:'fa-mountain',color:'#84CC16'},
{id:'acustica',name:'Acustica',icon:'fa-volume-high',color:'#D946EF'},
{id:'accesibilidad',name:'Accesibilidad',icon:'fa-wheelchair',color:'#0EA5E9'},
{id:'sol',name:'Sol/Sombra',icon:'fa-sun',color:'#FCD34D'},
{id:'misc',name:'Miscelanea',icon:'fa-toolbox',color:'#94A3B8'}
];

/* ===== 200 UTILIDADES ===== */
/* Formato: [id, nombre, idxCategoria, icono, [[idInput,label,unidad],...], funcion(vals)=>{resultado} ] */
const U=[
['d2p','Distancia 2 puntos',0,'fa-ruler',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m']],v=>({Distancia:Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1).toFixed(4)+' m',dX:(v.x2-v.x1).toFixed(4)+' m',dY:(v.y2-v.y1).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['d3p','Distancia 3D',0,'fa-ruler-combined',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['z1','Z1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['z2','Z2','m']],v=>({Distancia:Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1,v.z2-v.z1).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['ang3p','Angulo 3 puntos',0,'fa-drafting-compass',[['ax','Ax','m'],['ay','Ay','m'],['bx','Bx vertice','m'],['by','By vertice','m'],['cx','Cx','m'],['cy','Cy','m']],v=>{const a1=Math.atan2(v.ay-v.by,v.ax-v.bx),a2=Math.atan2(v.cy-v.by,v.cx-v.bx);let g=((a2-a1)*180/Math.PI+360)%360;return{Angulo:g.toFixed(2)+'°',Rad:((a2-a1+2*Math.PI)%(2*Math.PI)).toFixed(4)}}],
['pend','Pendiente',0,'fa-chart-line',[['dh','Desnivel','m'],['dd','Dist. horiz.','m']],v=>({Porcentaje:(v.dh/v.dd*100).toFixed(2)+'%',Angulo:(Math.atan(v.dh/v.dd)*180/Math.PI).toFixed(2)+'°',Razon:'1:'+Math.round(v.dd/Math.abs(v.dh))})],
['perim','Perimetro lados',0,'fa-draw-polygon',[['l','Lados (coma sep.)','m']],v=>{const a=v.l.split(',').map(Number).filter(n=>!isNaN(n));return{Perimetro:a.reduce((s,b)=>s+b,0).toFixed(3)+' m',Lados:a.length}]},
['pmed','Punto medio',0,'fa-crosshairs',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m']],v=>({PM_X:((v.x1+v.x2)/2).toFixed(4)+' m',PM_Y:((v.y1+v.y2)/2).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['dacum','Dist. acumulada',0,'fa-route',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m']],v=>{const p=v.pts.split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number));let d=0;for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)d+=Math.hypot(p[i][0]-p[i-1][0],p[i][1]-p[i-1][1]);return{Total:d.toFixed(3)+' m',Tramos:p.length-1}}],
['divseg','Dividir segmento',0,'fa-cut',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['n','Divisiones','']],v=>{const r={};for(let i=0;i<=v.n;i++){const t=i/v.n;r['P'+i]=`(${(v.x1+(v.x2-v.x1)*t).toFixed(2)}, ${(v.y1+(v.y2-v.y1)*t).toFixed(2)})`}return r}],
['rcurv','Radio de curva',0,'fa-circle-notch',[['c','Cuerda','m'],['f','Flecha','m']],v=>{const r=v.c*v.c/(8*v.f)+v.f/2;return{Radio:r.toFixed(4)+' m',Angulo:(2*Math.asin(v.c/(2*r))*180/Math.PI).toFixed(2)+'°',Largo:(2*r*Math.asin(v.c/(2*r))).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['inter2r','Interseccion 2 rectas',0,'fa-times',[['x1','X1 R1','m'],['y1','Y1 R1','m'],['x2','X2 R1','m'],['y2','Y2 R1','m'],['x3','X1 R2','m'],['y3','Y1 R2','m'],['x4','X2 R2','m'],['y4','Y2 R2','m']],v=>{const d=(v.x1-v.x2)*(v.y3-v.y4)-(v.y1-v.y2)*(v.x3-v.x4);if(Math.abs(d)<1e-10)return{R:'Paralelas'};const t=((v.x1-v.x3)*(v.y3-v.y4)-(v.y1-v.y3)*(v.x3-v.x4))/d;return{IX:(v.x1+t*(v.x2-v.x1)).toFixed(4)+' m',IY:(v.y1+t*(v.y2-v.y1)).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['offl','Linea paralela',0,'fa-arrows-left-right',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['d','Offset','m']],v=>{const l=Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1),nx=-(v.y2-v.y1)/l,ny=(v.x2-v.x1)/l;return{P1:`(${(v.x1+nx*v.d).toFixed(2)}, ${(v.y1+ny*v.d).toFixed(2)})`,P2:`(${(v.x2+nx*v.d).toFixed(2)}, ${(v.y2+ny*v.d).toFixed(2)})`}}],
['escd','Escala con distancia',0,'fa-ruler-horizontal',[['dr','Dist. real','m'],['dp','Dist. plano','cm']],v=>({Escala:'1:'+Math.round(v.dr*100/v.dp),Factor:(v.dr*100/v.dp).toFixed(4)})],
['arect','Area rectangulo',1,'fa-square',[['a','Ancho','m'],['b','Alto','m']],v=>({Area:(v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(2*(v.a+v.b)).toFixed(4)+' m',Diagonal:Math.hypot(v.a,v.b).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['atri','Area triangulo',1,'fa-play',[['b','Base','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(v.b*v.h/2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['acirc','Area circulo',1,'fa-circle',[['r','Radio','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(2*Math.PI*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m',Diametro:(2*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['atrap','Area trapecio',1,'fa-minus',[['B','Base mayor','m'],['b','Base menor','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:((v.B+v.b)*v.h/2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['aelip','Area elipse',1,'fa-egg',[['a','Semieje may.','m'],['b','Semieje men.','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:'≈'+(Math.PI*(3*(v.a+v.b)-Math.sqrt((3*v.a+v.b)*(v.a+3*v.b)))).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['aromb','Area rombo',1,'fa-diamond',[['D','Diag. may.','m'],['d','Diag. men.','m']],v=>({Area:(v.D*v.d/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Lado:Math.hypot(v.D/2,v.d/2).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['apara','Area paralelogramo',1,'fa-rectangle-xmark',[['b','Base','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(v.b*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['apolir','Area poligono irregular',1,'fa-draw-polygon',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m']],v=>{const p=v.pts.split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number));let a=0;for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%p.length;a+=p[i][0]*p[j][1]-p[j][0]*p[i][1]}return{Area:Math.abs(a/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Vertices:p.length}}],
['asect','Area sector circular',1,'fa-pie-chart',[['r','Radio','m'],['ang','Angulo','°']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.ang/360).toFixed(4)+' m²',Arco:(2*Math.PI*v.r*v.ang/360).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['acorona','Area corona circular',1,'fa-bullseye',[['R','Radio ext.','m'],['r','Radio int.','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*(v.R*v.R-v.r*v.r)).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['apolreg','Area poligono regular',1,'fa-hexagon-xmark',[['n','Lados',''],['l','Lado','m']],v=>({Area:(v.n*v.l*v.l/(4*Math.tan(Math.PI/v.n))).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(v.n*v.l).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['acomp','Area compuesta',1,'fa-object-group',[['a1','Area 1','m²'],['a2','Area 2','m²'],['op','Op (+/-)','']],v=>({Resultado:(v.op==='-'?v.a1-v.a2:v.a1+v.a2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['aterreno','Area por coordenadas',1,'fa-map',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m']],v=>{const p=v.pts.split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number));let a=0,per=0;for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%p.length;a+=p[i][0]*p[j][1]-p[j][0]*p[i][1];per+=Math.hypot(p[j][0]-p[i][0],p[j][1]-p[i][1])}return{Area:Math.abs(a/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:per.toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['averted','Area vertedero',1,'fa-water',[['L','Long. cresta','m'],['H','Carga','m'],['n','Coef (1-1.8)','']],v=>({Caudal:(2/3*v.n*v.L*Math.sqrt(2*9.81)*Math.pow(v.H,1.5)).toFixed(4)+' m³/s'})],
['vpris','Vol. prisma',2,'fa-square',[['a','Ancho','m'],['b','Largo','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.a*v.b*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area base':(v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vcil','Vol. cilindro',2,'fa-circle',[['r','Radio','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area lat.':(2*Math.PI*v.r*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m²','Area total':(2*Math.PI*v.r*(v.r+v.h)).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vesf','Vol. esfera',2,'fa-circle',[['r','Radio','m']],v=>({Volumen:(4/3*Math.PI*Math.pow(v.r,3)).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area sup.':(4*Math.PI*v.r*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vcono','Vol. cono',2,'fa-caret-up',[['r','Radio','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.h/3).toFixed(4)+' m³',Generatriz:Math.hypot(v.r,v.h).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['vpira','Vol. piramide',2,'fa-mountain',[['ab','Area base','m²'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.ab*v.h/3).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtronco','Tronco de cono',2,'fa-filter',[['R','R mayor','m'],['r','R menor','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.h/3*(v.R*v.R+v.r*v.r+v.R*v.r)).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtrap','Vol. trapecial',2,'fa-minus',[['B','Ancho sup.','m'],['b','Ancho inf.','m'],['l','Largo','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.B+v.b)/2*v.l*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtub','Vol. tuberia',2,'fa-grip-lines',[['di','Diam. int.','m'],['L','Longitud','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*Math.pow(v.di/2,2)*v.L).toFixed(4)+' m³',Litros:(Math.PI*Math.pow(v.di/2,2)*v.L*1000).toFixed(2)+' L'})],
['vexc','Vol. excavacion',2,'fa-hole',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho sup.','m'],['a','Ancho inf.','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.A+v.a)/2*v.L*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vterra','Vol. terraza',2,'fa-layer-group',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(4)+' m³',Peso:(v.L*v.A*v.e*2400).toFixed(0)+' kg'})],
['vlosa','Vol. losa',2,'fa-table-cells',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(4)+' m³',Peso:(v.L*v.A*v.e*2400).toFixed(0)+' kg',Area:(v.L*v.A).toFixed(2)+' m²'})],
['vzanja','Vol. zanja',2,'fa-grip-lines-vertical',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho sup.','m'],['a','Ancho inf.','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.A+v.a)/2*v.L*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vcist','Cap. cisterna',2,'fa-droplet',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³',Litros:(v.L*v.A*v.P*1000).toFixed(0)+' L',Galones:(v.L*v.A*v.P*264.172).toFixed(0)+' gal'})],
['vrell','Vol. relleno',2,'fa-mound',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m'],['c','Compact. %','']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e*(1+v.c/100)).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['mconcr','Concreto por vol.',3,'fa-cube',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>{const vol=v.L*v.A*v.e,s=Math.ceil(vol*7.5);return{Volumen:vol.toFixed(3)+' m³','Cemento (50kg)':s,'Arena (m³)':(vol*0.5).toFixed(3),'Grava (m³)':(vol*0.7).toFixed(3),'Agua (L)':Math.round(s*20)}}],
['mladr','Ladrillos por muro',3,'fa-border-all',[['L','Largo muro','m'],['H','Alto muro','m'],['la','Largo lad.','cm'],['al','Alto lad.','cm'],['j','Junta','cm']],v=>{const areaM=v.L*v.H,n=Math.ceil(areaM/(((v.la+v.j)/100)*((v.al+v.j)/100)));return{Area:areaM.toFixed(2)+' m²',Ladrillos:n,'Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(n*0.003)}}],
['mbloq','Bloques por muro',3,'fa-th-large',[['L','Largo','m'],['H','Alto','m'],['la','Largo bloq.','cm'],['al','Alto bloq.','cm'],['j','Junta','cm']],v=>{const n=Math.ceil(v.L*v.H/(((v.la+v.j)/100)*((v.al+v.j)/100)));return{Area:(v.L*v.H).toFixed(2)+' m²',Bloques:n,'Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(n*0.002)}}],
['mpiso','Pisos/ceramicos',3,'fa-table-cells-large',[['A','Area','m²'],['la','Largo pieza','cm'],['an','Ancho pieza','cm'],['j','Junta','cm'],['dp','Desperdicio %','']],v=>{const ap=(v.la+v.j)/100*(v.an+v.j)/100;return{Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A/ap*(1+v.dp/100)),'Area pieza':ap.toFixed(4)+' m²','Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(v.A*0.003)}}],
['mpint','Pintura necesaria',3,'fa-paint-roller',[['A','Area','m²'],['man','Manos',''],['rend','Rend. m²/L','']],v=>({Litros:(v.A*v.man/v.rend).toFixed(1)+' L',Galones:(v.A*v.man/v.rend/3.785).toFixed(1)+' gal'})],
['mazul','Azulejos pared',3,'fa-border-top-left',[['A','Area','m²'],['la','Largo','cm'],['an','Ancho','cm'],['j','Junta','cm'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>{const ap=(v.la+v.j)/100*(v.an+v.j)/100;return{Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A/ap*(1+v.dp/100)),'Area pieza':ap.toFixed(4)+' m²'}}],
['mmort','Mortero',3,'fa-flask',[['vol','Volumen','m³'],['c','Cemento',''],['a','Arena','']],v=>{const t=v.c+v.a;return{Cemento:(vol*v.c/t*1.5).toFixed(3)+' m³ ('+Math.ceil(v.vol*v.c/t*1.5*1500/50)+' sacos)',Arena:(vol*v.a/t*1.5).toFixed(3)+' m³'}}],
['macero','Acero refuerzo',3,'fa-bars',[['n','Varillas',''],['d','Diametro','mm'],['L','Largo c/u','m'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>{const vol=Math.PI*Math.pow(v.d/2000,2)*v.L*v.n;return{Peso:(vol*7850).toFixed(2)+' kg','Long. total':(v.L*v.n*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(2)+' m'}}],
['mencof','Encofrado',3,'fa-box',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['H','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(2*(v.L+v.A)*v.H).toFixed(2)+' m²','Tableros 1.22x2.44':Math.ceil(2*(v.L+v.A)*v.H/2.9768)})],
['myeso','Yeso/pegamento',3,'fa-paste',[['A','Area','m²'],['e','Espesor','mm'],['rend','Rend. kg/m²/mm','']],v=>({Kilos:(v.A*v.e*v.rend).toFixed(1)+' kg',Sacos:Math.ceil(v.A*v.e*v.rend/30)})],
['mteja','Tejas techo',3,'fa-home',[['A','Area','m²'],['rend','Piezas/m²',''],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>({Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A*v.rend*(1+v.dp/100))})],
['mlam','Laminas cubierta',3,'fa-layer-group',[['A','Area','m²'],['an','Ancho lam.','m'],['la','Largo lam.','m'],['sol','Solape %','']],v=>{const au=v.an*v.la*(1-v.sol/100);return{Laminas:Math.ceil(v.A/au),'Area util':au.toFixed(2)+' m²'}}],
['mvidr','Vidrio/cristal',3,'fa-clone',[['a','Ancho','m'],['h','Alto','m'],['n','Piezas','']],v=>({'Area total':(v.a*v.h*v.n).toFixed(2)+' m²','Area c/u':(v.a*v.h).toFixed(2)+' m²'})],
['mbarro','Tierra/barro',3,'fa-mound',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(3)+' m³',Toneladas:(v.L*v.A*v.e*1.5).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
['marenav','Arena por vol.',3,'fa-hill-rockslide',[['vol','Volumen','m³']],v=>({Toneladas:(v.vol*1.6).toFixed(2)+' t',Kilos:(v.vol*1600).toFixed(0)+' kg','Camiones 5m³':Math.ceil(v.vol/5)})],
['mpiedr','Piedra bruta',3,'fa-gem',[['A','Area','m²'],['e','Espesor','cm'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>({Volumen:(v.A*v.e/100*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(3)+' m³',Toneladas:(v.A*v.e/100*2.5*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
['evigam','Momento viga simple',4,'fa-arrows-left-right',[['c','Carga total','kN'],['L','Luz','m']],v=>({Momento:(v.c*v.L/8).toFixed(2)+' kN·m',Corte:(v.c/2).toFixed(2)+' kN'})],
['evigav','Viga voladizo',4,'fa-arrow-right',[['c','Carga','kN'],['L','Luz','m']],v=>({Momento:(v.c*v.L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m',Corte:v.c+' kN'})],
['ecol','Carga columna',4,'fa-arrows-up-down',[['A','Area trib.','m²'],['cv','Carga viva','kg/m²'],['cm','Carga muerta','kg/m²']],v=>({Total:((v.cv+v.cm)*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Viva:(v.cv*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Muerta:(v.cm*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Ton:((v.cv+v.cm)*v.A/1000).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
['elosa','Momento losa',4,'fa-table-cells',[['w','Carga','kN/m²'],['L','Luz corto','m'],['lx','Luz largo','m']],v=>{const r=lx/L;const m=r<=1?0.036:0.038;return{M_pos:(m*v.w*L*L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m/m',M_neg:(m*1.33*v.w*L*L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m/m',Corte:(v.w*L/2).toFixed(2)+' kN/m'}}],

['miscron','Estimativa tiempo obra',17,'fa-stopwatch',[['m2','m²',''],['r','Rend. m²/dia',''],['c','Cuadrillas','']],v=>({Dias:Math.ceil(m2/r/c),Semanas:(m2/r/c/6).toFixed(1),Meses:(m2/r/c/26).toFixed(2)})],
['misnum','Numeracion ambientes',17,'fa-list-ol',[['p','Plantas',''],['a','Amb./planta','']],v=>({Total:p*a,Formato:'101-'+(p*100+a)})],
['misconc','Diseno mezcla concreto',17,'fa-flask-vial',[['fc','fc (kg/cm²)',''],['sl','Slump (cm)','']],v=>{const ag=180+sl*5,w_c=fc<=210?0.5:0.45;return{Agua:ag+' L/m³',Cemento:Math.ceil(ag/w_c/50)+' sacos',Arena:Math.round(ag/w_c*2.2/1600*1000)+' kg',Grava:Math.round(ag/w_c*3/1500*1000)+' kg'}}],
['misplan','Plantilla catalogo',17,'fa-file-lines',[['n','Piezas',''],['an','Ancho cm',''],['al','Alto cm',''],['j','Junta cm','']],v=>({Ancho:((an+j)*Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(n))).toFixed(1)+' cm',Alto:((al+j)*Math.ceil(n/Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(n)))).toFixed(1)+' cm',Fila:Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(n)),Filas:Math.ceil(n/Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(n)))})],
['mischeck','Checklist obra',17,'fa-clipboard-check',[['f','1:ciment,2:estruc,3:muros,4:acab,5:instal','']],v=>{const l={1:['Trazo','Excavacion','Plantilla','Acero','Cimbra','Concreto','Curado','Relleno'],2:['Columnas','Trabes','Losa','Descimbra'],3:['Muros','Castillos','Dalas','Cadena'],4:['Aplanados','Pintura','Pisos','Azulejos','Canceleria'],5:['Electrico','Hidraulico','Sanitario','Gas','Aire acond.']};return Object.fromEntries((l[f]||[]).map(i=>[i,'☐']))}]
];

/* ===== MOTOR CAD ===== */
class CAD{
constructor(c){
  this.c=c;this.x=c.getContext('2d');
  this.objs=[];this.layers=[{id:0,name:'Capa 0',vis:true,col:'#EAEAEA',lock:false}];this.aLayer=0;
  this.tool='select';this.zoom=1;this.px=0;this.py=0;this.gs=20;this.showGrid=true;this.snap=true;
  this.sCol='#FF6B2B';this.fCol='#FF6B2B';this.fill=false;this.lw=2;this.wt=15;this.fs=16;this.tTxt='Texto';
  this.undoS=[];this.redoS=[];this.selIdx=-1;
  this.drawing=false;this.panning=false;this.pinching=false;
  this.sp=null;this.cp=null;this.polyPts=[];this.freePts=[];
  this.td=0;this.tc=null;this.tcc=0;
  this.dirty=true;this.dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
  this.resize();this.events();this.render();
}
resize(){
  const w=this.c.parentElement,h=w.clientWidth,v=w.clientHeight;
  this.c.width=h*this.dpr;this.c.height=v*this.dpr;
  this.c.style.width=h+'px';this.c.style.height=v+'px';
  this.x.setTransform(this.dpr,0,0,this.dpr,0,0);
  this.W=h;this.H=v;this.dirty=true;this.render();
}
s2w(sx,sy){return{x:(sx-this.px)/this.zoom,y:(sy-this.py)/this.zoom}}
w2s(wx,wy){return{x:wx*this.zoom+this.px,y:wy*this.zoom+this.py}}
sn(x,y){if(!this.snap)return{x,y};const g=this.gs;return{x:Math.round(x/g)*g,y:Math.round(y/g)*g}}
mp(e){const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();return{x:e.clientX-r.left,y:e.clientY-r.top}}

events(){
  const c=this.c;
  c.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>this.tStart(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>this.tMove(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchend',e=>this.tEnd(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>this.mDown(e));
  c.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>this.mMove(e));
  c.addEventListener('mouseup',e=>this.mUp(e));
  c.addEventListener('wheel',e=>this.whl(e),{passive:false});
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>this.resize());
  // Doble tap para cerrar polilínea
  let lastTap=0;
  c.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-lastTap<300&&this.tool==='polyline'){this.closePolyline()}
    lastTap=now;
  });
}

tStart(e){
  e.preventDefault();this.td=e.touches.length;
  if(this.td===2){
    this.pinching=true;this.drawing=false;
    const t=e.touches;
    this.td2=Math.hypot(t[1].clientX-t[0].clientX,t[1].clientY-t[0].clientY);
    this.tc={x:(t[0].clientX+t[1].clientX)/2,y:(t[0].clientY+t[1].clientY)/2};
  }else if(this.td===1){
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.hStart({x:e.touches[0].clientX-r.left,y:e.touches[0].clientY-r.top});
  }
}
tMove(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  if(this.pinching&&e.touches.length===2){
    const t=e.touches,d=Math.hypot(t[1].clientX-t[0].clientX,t[1].clientY-t[0].clientY);
    const cn={x:(t[0].clientX+t[1].clientX)/2,y:(t[0].clientY+t[1].clientY)/2};
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cx=cn.x-r.left,cy=cn.y-r.top,lx=this.tc.x-r.left,ly=this.tc.y-r.top;
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(20,this.zoom*d/this.td2));
    this.px+=cx-lx;this.py+=cy-ly;
    this.td2=d;this.tc=cn;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom();
  }else if(this.td===1&&e.touches.length===1){
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.hMove({x:e.touches[0].clientX-r.left,y:e.touches[0].clientY-r.top});
  }
}
tEnd(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  if(e.touches.length<2)this.pinching=false;
  if(e.touches.length===0){this.hEnd();this.td=0}else this.td=e.touches.length;
}
mDown(e){
  if(e.button===1){this.panning=true;this.sp=this.mp(e);return}
  if(e.button===2)return;this.hStart(this.mp(e));
}
mMove(e){
  if(this.panning){const p=this.mp(e);this.px+=p.x-this.sp.x;this.py+=p.y-this.sp.y;this.sp=p;this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  this.hMove(this.mp(e));
}
mUp(e){if(this.panning){this.panning=false;return}this.hEnd()}
whl(e){
  e.preventDefault();const p=this.mp(e),wb=this.s2w(p.x,p.y);
  this.zoom*=e.deltaY<0?1.1:.9;this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(20,this.zoom));
  const wa=this.w2s(wb.x,wb.y);this.px+=p.x-wa.x;this.py+=p.y-wa.y;
  this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom();
}

hStart(pos){
  const w=this.s2w(pos.x,pos.y),s=this.sn(w.x,w.y);
  if(this.tool==='select'){
    this.selIdx=this.hit(w.x,w.y);this.drawing=this.selIdx>=0;
    if(this.drawing){const o=this.objs[this.selIdx];this.sp={x:w.x-(o.x||o.x1||0),y:w.y-(o.y||o.y1||0)}}
    this.dirty=true;this.render();return;
  }
  if(this.tool==='move'){this.panning=true;this.sp=pos;return}
  if(this.tool==='erase'){const i=this.hit(w.x,w.y);if(i>=0){this.saveU();this.objs.splice(i,1);this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Eliminado','success')}return}
  if(this.tool==='polyline'){this.polyPts.push(s);this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(this.tool==='text'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'text',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x,y:s.y,content:this.tTxt,size:this.fs,color:this.sCol});this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(this.tool==='door'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'door',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x,y:s.y,width:80,height:10,color:this.sCol,swing:1});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Puerta insertada','success');return}
  if(this.tool==='window'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'window',layer:this.aLayer,x1:s.x-60,y1:s.y,x2:s.x+60,y2:s.y,width:60,color:this.sCol});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Ventana insertada','success');return}
  if(this.tool==='furniture'||this.tool==='symbol')return;
  this.drawing=true;this.sp=s;this.cp=s;
  if(this.tool==='freehand')this.freePts=[s];
}
hMove(pos){
  const w=this.s2w(pos.x,pos.y),s=this.sn(w.x,w.y);this.uCoord(w.x,w.y);
  if(this.tool==='select'&&this.drawing&&this.selIdx>=0){
    const o=this.objs[this.selIdx],dx=w.x-this.sp.x,dy=w.y-this.sp.y;this.mvObj(o,dx,dy);this.dirty=true;this.render();return;
  }
  if(this.tool==='move'&&this.panning){this.px+=pos.x-this.sp.x;this.py+=pos.y-this.sp.y;this.sp=pos;this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(!this.drawing)return;this.cp=s;
  if(this.tool==='freehand')this.freePts.push(s);
  this.dirty=true;this.render();
}
hEnd(){
  if(this.tool==='move'){this.panning=false;return}
  if(this.tool==='select'&&this.drawing){this.drawing=false;return}
  if(!this.drawing||!this.sp||!this.cp)return;this.drawing=false;
  const s=this.sp,c=this.cp;
  if(Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y)<2){this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  this.saveU();const b={layer:this.aLayer,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lw};
  switch(this.tool){
    case'line':this.objs.push({...b,type:'line',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y});break;
    case'rect':{const x=Math.min(s.x,c.x),y=Math.min(s.y,c.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'rect',x,y,w:Math.abs(c.x-s.x),h:Math.abs(c.y-s.y),fill:this.fill?this.fCol:null});break}
    case'circle':{const r=Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'circle',cx:s.x,cy:s.y,r,fill:this.fill?this.fCol:null});break}
    case'arc':{const r=Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'arc',cx:s.x,cy:s.y,r,startAngle:0,endAngle:Math.atan2(c.y-s.y,c.x-s.x)});break}
    case'wall':this.objs.push({...b,type:'wall',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y,thickness:this.wt});break;
    case'dimension':this.objs.push({...b,type:'dimension',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y,offset:30});break;
    case'freehand':if(this.freePts.length>2)this.objs.push({...b,type:'freehand',points:[...this.freePts]});this.freePts=[];break;
  }
  this.dirty=true;this.render();
}

mvObj(o,dx,dy){
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':o.x1+=dx;o.y1+=dy;o.x2+=dx;o.y2+=dy;break;
    case'rect':case'furniture':case'symbol':o.x+=dx;o.y+=dy;break;
    case'circle':case'arc':o.cx+=dx;o.cy+=dy;break;
    case'text':case'door':o.x+=dx;o.y+=dy;break;
    case'freehand':case'polyline':o.points.forEach(p=>{p.x+=dx;p.y+=dy});break;
  }
}

hit(wx,wy){
  for(let i=this.objs.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    if(!this.layers[this.objs[i].layer]?.vis)continue;
    if(this.hitO(this.objs[i],wx,wy))return i;
  }return-1;
}
hitO(o,wx,wy){
  const t=8/this.zoom;
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':return this.dSeg(wx,wy,o.x1,o.y1,o.x2,o.y2)<t;
    case'rect':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.w+t&&wy>=o.y-t&&wy<=o.y+o.h+t;
    case'circle':return Math.abs(Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy)-o.r)<t||Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy)<o.r;
    case'arc':{const d=Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy);if(Math.abs(d-o.r)>t)return false;let a=Math.atan2(wy-o.cy,wx-o.cx);if(a<o.startAngle)a+=Math.PI*2;return a>=o.startAngle&&a<=o.endAngle}
    case'text':this.x.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';const tw=this.x.measureText(o.content).width;return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+tw+t&&wy>=o.y-o.size-t&&wy<=o.y+t;
    case'door':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.width+t&&wy>=o.y-o.width/2-t&&wy<=o.y+o.width/2+t;
    case'freehand':case'polyline':for(let j=1;j<o.points.length;j++)if(this.dSeg(wx,wy,o.points[j-1].x,o.points[j-1].y,o.points[j].x,o.points[j].y)<t)return true;return false;
    case'furniture':case'symbol':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.w+t&&wy>=o.y-t&&wy<=o.y+o.h+t;
  }return false;
}
dSeg(px,py,x1,y1,x2,y2){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,l2=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(l2===0)return Math.hypot(px-x1,py-y1);let t=((px-x1)*dx+(py-y1)*dy)/l2;t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));return Math.hypot(px-(x1+t*dx),py-(y1+t*dy))}

/* --- RENDER --- */
render(){
  if(!this.dirty)return;this.dirty=false;
  const c=this.x;c.save();c.clearRect(0,0,this.W,this.H);
  c.fillStyle='#0C0C0C';c.fillRect(0,0,this.W,this.H);
  if(this.showGrid)this.drawGrid();
  for(let i=0;i<this.objs.length;i++){const o=this.objs[i];if(!this.layers[o.layer]?.vis)continue;this.drawObj(o,i===this.selIdx)}
  if(this.drawing&&this.sp&&this.cp)this.drawPrev();
  if(this.tool==='polyline'&&this.polyPts.length>0){
    c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
    c.fillStyle='#FF6B2B';this.polyPts.forEach(p=>{c.beginPath();c.arc(p.x,p.y,4/this.zoom,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill()});
    if(this.polyPts.length>1){c.strokeStyle='#FF6B2B';c.lineWidth=1/this.zoom;c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(this.polyPts[0].x,this.polyPts[0].y);for(let i=1;i<this.polyPts.length;i++)c.lineTo(this.polyPts[i].x,this.polyPts[i].y);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([])}
    c.restore();
  }
  const o=this.w2s(0,0);
  if(o.x>-20&&o.x<this.W+20&&o.y>-20&&o.y<this.H+20){
    c.strokeStyle='#FF6B2B22';c.lineWidth=1;
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x,0);c.lineTo(o.x,this.H);c.stroke();
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,o.y);c.lineTo(this.W,o.y);c.stroke();
  }
  c.restore();
}
drawGrid(){
  const c=this.x,g=this.gs*this.zoom;if(g<5)return;
  const ox=this.px%g,oy=this.py%g;
  c.strokeStyle=g<15?'#181818':'#1E1E1E';c.lineWidth=.5;c.beginPath();
  for(let x=ox;x<this.W;x+=g){c.moveTo(x,0);c.lineTo(x,this.H)}
  for(let y=oy;y<this.H;y+=g){c.moveTo(0,y);c.lineTo(this.W,y)}
  c.stroke();
}
drawObj(o,sel){
  const c=this.x;c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
  c.strokeStyle=sel?'#FFF':o.color;c.fillStyle=o.fill||'transparent';
  c.lineWidth=(o.lineWidth||2)/this.zoom;c.lineCap='round';c.lineJoin='round';
  if(sel){c.shadowColor=o.color;c.shadowBlur=10/this.zoom}
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1,o.y1);c.lineTo(o.x2,o.y2);c.stroke();break;
    case'rect':if(o.fill){c.fillStyle=o.fill+'44';c.fillRect(o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h)}c.strokeRect(o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h);break;
    case'circle':c.beginPath();c.arc(o.cx,o.cy,Math.max(.1,o.r),0,Math.PI*2);if(o.fill){c.fillStyle=o.fill+'44';c.fill()}c.stroke();break;
    case'arc':c.beginPath();c.arc(o.cx,o.cy,Math.max(.1,o.r),o.startAngle,o.endAngle);c.stroke();break;
    case'wall':{const dx=o.x2-o.x1,dy=o.y2-o.y1,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(l<.1)break;const nx=-dy/l*o.thickness/2,ny=dx/l*o.thickness/2;c.fillStyle=o.color+'33';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1+nx,o.y1+ny);c.lineTo(o.x2+nx,o.y2+ny);c.lineTo(o.x2-nx,o.y2-ny);c.lineTo(o.x1-nx,o.y1-ny);c.closePath();c.fill();c.stroke();break}
    case'door':{const w=o.width;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x,o.y-5);c.lineTo(o.x,o.y+5);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.arc(o.x,o.y+w/2,w/2,-Math.PI/2,0);c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x,o.y);c.lineTo(o.x+w/2,o.y+w/2);c.stroke();break}
    case'window':{const mx=(o.x1+o.x2)/2,my=(o.y1+o.y2)/2,l=Math.hypot(o.x2-o.x1,o.y2-o.y1);if(l<.1)break;const dx=(o.x2-o.x1)/l*o.width/2,dy=(o.y2-o.y1)/l*o.width/2,nx=-(o.y2-o.y1)/l*8,ny=(o.x2-o.x1)/l*8;c.lineWidth=3/this.zoom;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(mx-dx,my-dy);c.lineTo(mx-dx+nx,my-dy+ny);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(mx+dx,my+dy);c.lineTo(mx+dx+nx,my+dy+ny);c.stroke();c.lineWidth=1/this.zoom;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(mx-dx*.6,my-dy*.6);c.lineTo(mx+dx*.6,my+dy*.6);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(mx-dx*.6+nx,my-dy*.6+ny);c.lineTo(mx+dx*.6+nx,my+dy*.6+ny);c.stroke();break}
    case'dimension':{const dx=o.x2-o.x1,dy=o.y2-o.y1,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(l<.1)break;const nx=-dy/l*o.offset,ny=dx/l*o.offset;const ax=o.x1+nx,ay=o.y1+ny,bx=o.x2+nx,by=o.y2+ny;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(ax,ay);c.lineTo(bx,by);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([3/this.zoom,3/this.zoom]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1,o.y1);c.lineTo(ax,ay);c.moveTo(o.x2,o.y2);c.lineTo(bx,by);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);const fs=12/this.zoom;c.font=`500 ${fs}px "JetBrains Mono"`;c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='bottom';const txt=l.toFixed(1),tw=c.measureText(txt).width,mx2=(ax+bx)/2,my2=(ay+by)/2;c.fillStyle='#0C0C0C';c.fillRect(mx2-tw/2-3/this.zoom,my2-fs-2/this.zoom,tw+6/this.zoom,fs+4/this.zoom);c.fillStyle=o.color;c.fillText(txt,mx2,my2-2/this.zoom);break}
    case'text':c.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.fillStyle=o.color;c.textAlign='left';c.textBaseline='bottom';c.fillText(o.content,o.x,o.y);break;
    case'freehand':if(o.points.length<2)break;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.points[0].x,o.points[0].y);for(let i=1;i<o.points.length;i++)c.lineTo(o.points[i].x,o.points[i].y);c.stroke();break;
    case'polyline':if(o.points.length<2)break;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.points[0].x,o.points[0].y);for(let i=1;i<o.points.length;i++)c.lineTo(o.points[i].x,o.points[i].y);if(o.closed)c.closePath();c.stroke();break;
    case'furniture':case'symbol':c.font=`${Math.min(o.w,o.h)*.6}px "Font Awesome 6 Free"`;c.fillStyle=o.color;c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillText(o.icon,o.x+o.w/2,o.y+o.h/2);c.strokeRect(o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h);break;
  }
  if(sel){c.shadowBlur=0;c.strokeStyle='#FFF';c.lineWidth=1/this.zoom;c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);const bb=this.bbox(o);c.strokeRect(bb.x-4/this.zoom,bb.y-4/this.zoom,bb.w+8/this.zoom,bb.h+8/this.zoom);c.setLineDash([]);c.fillStyle='#FFF';const hs=3/this.zoom;[[bb.x,bb.y],[bb.x+bb.w,bb.y],[bb.x,bb.y+bb.h],[bb.x+bb.w,bb.y+bb.h]].forEach(([hx,hy])=>{c.fillRect(hx-hs,hy-hs,hs*2,hs*2)})}
  c.restore();
}
bbox(o){
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':return{x:Math.min(o.x1,o.x2),y:Math.min(o.y1,o.y2),w:Math.abs(o.x2-o.x1)||1,h:Math.abs(o.y2-o.y1)||1};
    case'rect':case'furniture':case'symbol':return{x:o.x,y:o.y,w:o.w,h:o.h};
    case'circle':case'arc':return{x:o.cx-o.r,y:o.cy-o.r,w:o.r*2,h:o.r*2};
    case'text':this.x.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';return{x:o.x,y:o.y-o.size,w:this.x.measureText(o.content).width,h:o.size};
    case'door':return{x:o.x,y:o.y-o.width/2,w:o.width,h:o.width};
    case'freehand':case'polyline':{let mx=Infinity,my=Infinity,Mx=-Infinity,My=-Infinity;o.points.forEach(p=>{mx=Math.min(mx,p.x);my=Math.min(my,p.y);Mx=Math.max(Mx,p.x);My=Math.max(My,p.y)});return{x:mx,y:my,w:(Mx-mx)||1,h:(My-my)||1}}
    default:return{x:0,y:0,w:10,h:10};
  }
}
drawPrev(){
  const c=this.x,s=this.sp,p=this.cp;
  c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
  c.strokeStyle=this.sCol+'AA';c.lineWidth=this.lw/this.zoom;c.setLineDash([6/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.lineCap='round';
  switch(this.tool){
    case'line':case'wall':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(s.x,s.y);c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);c.stroke();break;
    case'rect':{const x=Math.min(s.x,p.x),y=Math.min(s.y,p.y);c.strokeRect(x,y,Math.abs(p.x-s.x),Math.abs(p.y-s.y));break}
    case'circle':{const r=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);c.beginPath();c.arc(s.x,s.y,Math.max(.1,r),0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'arc':{const r=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);c.beginPath();c.arc(s.x,s.y,Math.max(.1,r),0,Math.atan2(p.y-s.y,p.x-s.x));c.stroke();break}
    case'dimension':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(s.x,s.y);c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);c.stroke();break;
    case'freehand':if(this.freePts.length>1){c.setLineDash([]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(this.freePts[0].x,this.freePts[0].y);for(let i=1;i<this.freePts.length;i++)c.lineTo(this.freePts[i].x,this.freePts[i].y);c.stroke()}break;
  }
  c.setLineDash([]);const d=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);
  if(d>5){const fs=11/this.zoom;c.font=`500 ${fs}px "JetBrains Mono"`;c.fillStyle='#FF6B2B';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='bottom';c.fillText(d.toFixed(1),(s.x+p.x)/2,(s.y+p.y)/2-8/this.zoom)}
  c.restore();
}

/* Acciones */
saveU(){this.undoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));if(this.undoS.length>50)this.undoS.shift();this.redoS=[]}
undo(){if(!this.undoS.length)return;this.redoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));this.objs=JSON.parse(this.undoS.pop());this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Deshacer','info')}
redo(){if(!this.redoS.length)return;this.undoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));this.objs=JSON.parse(this.redoS.pop());this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Rehacer','info')}
zoomIn(){this.zoom=Math.min(20,this.zoom*1.3);this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
zoomOut(){this.zoom=Math.max(.1,this.zoom/1.3);this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
resetView(){this.zoom=1;this.px=this.W/2;this.py=this.H/2;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
clearAll(){if(!this.objs.length)return;this.saveU();this.objs=[];this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Lienzo limpio','success')}
deleteSelected(){if(this.selIdx<0)return;this.saveU();this.objs.splice(this.selIdx,1);this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Eliminado','success')}
duplicateSelected(){if(this.selIdx<0)return;this.saveU();const c=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.objs[this.selIdx]));this.mvObj(c,20,20);this.objs.push(c);this.selIdx=this.objs.length-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Duplicado','success')}
selectAll(){toast(this.objs.length+' objetos','info')}
finishPolyline(){if(this.polyPts.length>1){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'polyline',layer:this.aLayer,points:[...this.polyPts],color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lw,closed:false});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Polilinea ('+this.polyPts.length+' pts)','success')}this.polyPts=[];this.dirty=true;this.render()}
closePolyline(){if(this.polyPts.length>2){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'polyline',layer:this.aLayer,points:[...this.polyPts],color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lw,closed:true});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Poligono cerrado','success')}this.polyPts=[];this.dirty=true;this.render()}
insertFurn(icon,name,w,h){this.saveU();const s=this.s2w(this.W/2/this.dpr,this.H/2/this.dpr);this.objs.push({type:'furniture',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x-w/2,y:s.y-h/2,w,h,icon,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:1});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast(name+' insertado','success');closeM('furn-modal')}
insertSym(icon,name,w,h){this.saveU();const s=this.s2w(this.W/2/this.dpr,this.H/2/this.dpr);this.objs.push({type:'symbol',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x-w/2,y:s.y-h/2,w,h,icon,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:1});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast(name+' insertado','success');closeM('sym-modal')}
uCoord(x,y){document.getElementById('coords').textContent='X: '+x.toFixed(1)+' Y: '+y.toFixed(1)}
uZoom(){document.getElementById('zoom-ind').textContent=Math.round(this.zoom*100)+'%'}
setTool(t){
  if(this.tool==='polyline'&&t!=='polyline')this.finishPolyline();
  this.tool=t;this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();
  document.querySelectorAll('#toolstrip button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.tool===t));
  const hints={select:'Toca para seleccionar',move:'Arrastra para mover vista',line:'Arrastra para linea',rect:'Arrastra para rectangulo',circle:'Centro y arrastra radio',arc:'Centro y arrastra arco',polyline:'Toca puntos. Doble tap=cerrar',freehand:'Dibuja con el dedo',wall:'Arrastra para muro',door:'Toca para puerta',window:'Toca para ventana',dimension:'Arrastra para cota',text:'Toca para colocar texto',furniture:'Abre catalogo',symbol:'Abre catalogo',erase:'Toca para borrar'};
  document.getElementById('tool-hint').textContent=hints[t]||t;
}
}

/* ===== VARIABLES GLOBALES ===== */
let cad;

/* ===== TOAST ===== */
function toast(msg,type='info'){
  const c=document.getElementById('toast-c'),t=document.createElement('div');
  t.className='toast '+type;
  t.innerHTML=`<i class="fas fa-${type==='success'?'check-circle':type==='error'?'times-circle':'info-circle'}"></i>${msg}`;
  c.appendChild(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>t.remove(),300)},2200);
}

/* ===== MODALES ===== */
function openM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open')}
function closeM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open')}

/* ===== PANELES ===== */
let activePanel=null;
function togglePanel(id){
  const p=document.getElementById('panel-'+id);
  if(activePanel===id){p.classList.remove('open');activePanel=null;document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));return}
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(pp=>pp.classList.remove('open'));
  p.classList.add('open');activePanel=id;
  document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.panel===id));
}

/* ===== CAPAS ===== */
function renderLayers(){
  const c=document.getElementById('layers-list');c.innerHTML='';
  cad.layers.forEach((l,i)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='li'+(i===cad.aLayer?' active':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<button onclick="event.stopPropagation();cad.layers[${i}].vis=!cad.layers[${i}].vis;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers()" title="Visibilidad"><i class="fas fa-${l.vis?'eye':'eye-slash'}"></i></button>
    <span class="ln" style="color:${l.col}">${l.name}</span>
    <button onclick="event.stopPropagation();if(cad.layers.length>1){cad.layers.splice(${i},1);if(cad.aLayer>=cad.layers.length)cad.aLayer=cad.layers.length-1;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers()}" title="Eliminar"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{cad.aLayer=i;renderLayers()};
    c.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function addLayer(){
  const n=cad.layers.length;
  const cols=['#EAEAEA','#22C55E','#3B82F6','#FBBF24','#EF4444','#A855F7','#06B6D4','#EC4899','#FF6B2B','#84CC16'];
  cad.layers.push({id:n,name:'Capa '+n,vis:true,col:cols[n%cols.length],lock:false});
  cad.aLayer=n;renderLayers();toast('Capa '+n+' creada','success');
}

/* ===== UTILIDADES UI ===== */
let activeCat='all';
function renderCatChips(){
  const c=document.getElementById('cat-chips');c.innerHTML='<button class="cchip'+(activeCat==='all'?' active':'')+'" onclick="activeCat=\'all\';filterUtils()">Todas</button>';
  CATS.forEach(cat=>{c.innerHTML+=`<button class="cchip${activeCat===cat.id?' active':''}" onclick="activeCat='${cat.id}';filterUtils()"><i class="fas ${cat.icon}"></i> ${cat.name}</button>`});
}
function filterUtils(){
  const q=document.getElementById('usearch').value.toLowerCase();
  const g=document.getElementById('ugrid');g.innerHTML='';
  renderCatChips();
  U.forEach(u=>{
    const cat=CATS[u[2]];
    if(activeCat!=='all'&&cat.id!==activeCat)return;
    if(q&&!u[1].toLowerCase().includes(q)&&!cat.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))return;
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='ucard';
    d.innerHTML=`<i class="fas ${u[3]}" style="color:${cat.color}"></i><span>${u[1]}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>openUtil(u);
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function openUtil(u){
  const b=document.getElementById('um-body');
  document.getElementById('um-title').textContent=u[1];
  let h='';
  if(u[4].length>0){
    h+='<div class="mbody">';
    u[4].forEach(inp=>{h+=`<div class="ci"><label>${inp[1]}</label><input type="number" id="ui_${inp[0]}" step="any" placeholder="0"><span class="unit">${inp[2]||''}</span></div>`});
    h+=`<button class="cbtn" onclick="calcUtil(${U.indexOf(u)})">Calcular</button>`;
    h+='<div class="rbox" id="ures"></div></div>';
  }else{
    h='<p style="color:var(--fg3);text-align:center;padding:20px">Sin entradas</p>';
  }
  b.innerHTML=h;
  openM('util-modal');
}
function calcUtil(idx){
  const u=U[idx],vals={};
  u[4].forEach(inp=>{const el=document.getElementById('ui_'+inp[0]);vals[inp[0]]=parseFloat(el?.value)||0});
  try{
    const res=u[5](vals);
    let h='';
    for(const[k,v]of Object.entries(res)){
      const isNum=typeof v==='string'&&v!=='☐'&&v!=='Si'&&v!=='No'&&v!=='Sí';
      h+=`<div class="rrow"><span class="rl">${k}</span><span class="rv">${v}</span></div>`;
    }
    document.getElementById('ures').innerHTML=h;
  }catch(e){document.getElementById('ures').innerHTML=`<div class="rrow"><span class="rl">Error</span><span class="rv">${e.message}</span></div>`}
}

['\uf4b8','Cama individual',60,100],['\uf236','Cama doble',100,140],['\uf7c0','Sofa',80,180],

['\uf201','Mesa centro',50,80],['\uf008','TV',10,100],['\uf2dc','Refrigerador',60,60],
['\uf2e7','Estufa',50,60],['\uf5a0','Lavadora',50,50],['\uf015','Fregadero',50,50],
['\uf2f5','Inodoro',35,50],['\uf2cd','Lavabo',40,50],['\uf2cc','Banera',70,150],
['\uf2ce','Ducha',80,80],['\uf007','Estante',30,100],['\uf468','Closet',60,180],
['\uf6c3','Mesa noche',40,40],['\uf0f4','Sillón',60,60],['\uf0fc','Mesa cocina',60,120],
['\uf5d1','Colchoneta',60,180],['\uf1ad','Banco',30,80],['\uf0c0','Sofá esq.',80,80],

];

}

['\uf0e7','Toma eléctrico',20,20],['\uf578','Interruptor',20,20],['\uf6e3','Foco',20,20],
['\uf1e6','Contacto',20,20],['\uf7e4','Toma地板',20,20],['\uf085','Tablero',30,40],
['\uf043','Llave agua',20,20],['\uf133','Desagüe',20,20],['\uf06d','Regadera',20,20],
['\uf2f9','Sanitario',20,20],['\uf0d6','Lavabo',20,20],['\uf494','Calentador',20,30],
['\uf863','Aire acond.',30,30],['\uf72e','Ventilador',20,20],['\uf06d','Extintor',15,30],
['\uf2f2','Medidor gas',20,20],['\uf1b9','Estacionam.',20,20],['\uf518','Ascensor',30,30],
['\uf0b1','Rampa',20,40],['\uf554','Escalera',20,40],['\uf3c5','Norte',25,25],
['\uf024','Salida emerg.',20,20],['\uf7e9','Video vigil.',20,20],['\uf023','Cerradura',20,20],
['\uf1b0','Jardín',30,30],['\uf06c','Árbol',30,30],['\uf6fc','Nivel',20,20],
['\uf5e1','Eje simetría',20,20],['\uf140','Corte',20,20],['\uf070','Detalle',20,20],
['\uf4d8','Cota nivel',20,20]
];
function renderSyms(){
  const g=document.getElementById('sym-g');g.innerHTML='';
  SYMS.forEach(s=>{const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='fbtn';d.innerHTML=`<i class="fas">${s[0]}</i>${s[1]}`;d.onclick=()=>cad.insertSym(s[0],s[1],s[2],s[3]);g.appendChild(d)});
}

/* ===== EXPORTAR ===== */
function exportPNG(){
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.png';
  a.href=cad.c.toDataURL('image/png');a.click();toast('PNG exportado','success');
}
function exportJPG(){
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.jpg';
  a.href=cad.c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.9);a.click();toast('JPG exportado','success');
}
function exportSVG(){
  let svg='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="'+cad.W+'" height="'+cad.H+'" style="background:#0C0C0C">';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    const c=o.color||'#FFF',lw=o.lineWidth||2;
    switch(o.type){
      case'line':svg+=`<line x1="${o.x1}" y1="${o.y1}" x2="${o.x2}" y2="${o.y2}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${lw}"/>`;break;
      case'rect':svg+=`<rect x="${o.x}" y="${o.y}" width="${o.w}" height="${o.h}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${lw}" fill="${o.fill||'none'}"/>`;break;
      case'circle':svg+=`<circle cx="${o.cx}" cy="${o.cy}" r="${o.r}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${lw}" fill="${o.fill||'none'}"/>`;break;
      case'wall':svg+=`<line x1="${o.x1}" y1="${o.y1}" x2="${o.x2}" y2="${o.y2}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${o.thickness}" opacity="0.4"/><line x1="${o.x1}" y1="${o.y1}" x2="${o.x2}" y2="${o.y2}" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="${lw}"/>`;break;
      case'text':svg+=`<text x="${o.x}" y="${o.y}" fill="${c}" font-size="${o.size}">${o.content}</text>`;break;
    }
  });
  svg+='</svg>';
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.svg';a.href='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);a.click();toast('SVG exportado','success');
}
function exportJSON(){
  const data=JSON.stringify({objects:cad.objs,layers:cad.layers,activeLayer:cad.aLayer},null,2);
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-proyecto.json';a.href='data:application/json;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(data);a.click();toast('Proyecto guardado','success');
}
function importJSON(){document.getElementById('json-in').click()}
function handleJSONImport(e){
  const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;
  const r=new FileReader();r.onload=ev=>{
    try{
      const d=JSON.parse(ev.target.result);cad.objs=d.objects||[];cad.layers=d.layers||cad.layers;cad.aLayer=d.activeLayer||0;
      cad.selIdx=-1;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers();toast('Proyecto cargado','success');
    }catch(err){toast('Error al cargar','error')}
  };r.readAsText(f);e.target.value='';
}
function exportDXF(){
  let dxf='0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    switch(o.type){
      case'line':case'wall':dxf+=`0\nLINE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.x1}\n20\n${o.y1}\n30\n0\n11\n${o.x2}\n21\n${o.y2}\n31\n0\n`;break;
      case'circle':dxf+=`0\nCIRCLE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.cx}\n20\n${o.cy}\n30\n0\n40\n${o.r}\n`;break;
      case'rect':dxf+=`0\nLINE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.x}\n20\n${o.y}\n30\n0\n11\n${o.x+o.w}\n21\n${o.y}\n31\n0\n0\nLINE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.x+o.w}\n20\n${o.y}\n30\n0\n11\n${o.x+o.w}\n21\n${o.y+o.h}\n31\n0\n0\nLINE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.x+o.w}\n20\n${o.y+o.h}\n30\n0\n11\n${o.x}\n21\n${o.y+o.h}\n31\n0\n0\nLINE\n8\n${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n10\n${o.x}\n20\n${o.y+o.h}\n30\n0\n11\n${o.x}\n21\n${o.y}\n31\n0\n`;break;
    }
  });
  dxf+='0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n';
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.dxf';a.href='data:text/plain;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(dxf);a.click();toast('DXF exportado','success');
}
function exportCSV(){
  let csv='Tipo,X1,Y1,X2,Y2,Color,Layer\n';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    csv+=`${o.type},${o.x1||o.x||o.cx||''},${o.y1||o.y||o.cy||''},${o.x2||''},${o.y2||''},${o.color||''},${cad.layers[o.layer]?.name||'0'}\n`;
  });
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.csv';a.href='data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(csv);a.click();toast('CSV exportado','success');
}

/* ===== ESCANEAR ===== */
let scanImg=null;
function openScanner(){document.getElementById('sc-file').click()}
function loadScanFile(e){
  const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;
  const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{
    scanImg=img;const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv');
    cv.width=img.width;cv.height=img.height;cv.style.display='block';
    cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0);toast('Imagen cargada','success');
  };img.src=URL.createObjectURL(f);e.target.value='';
}
function applyScan(){
  if(!scanImg)return;const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv'),cx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const br=+document.getElementById('sc-br').value,co=+document.getElementById('sc-co').value,th=+document.getElementById('sc-th').value;
  cx.drawImage(scanImg,0,0);const id=cx.getImageData(0,0,cv.width,cv.height),d=id.data;
  const cf=(co+100)/100;
  for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
    d[i]=Math.min(255,Math.max(0,(d[i]+br)*cf));
    d[i+1]=Math.min(255,Math.max(0,(d[i+1]+br)*cf));
    d[i+2]=Math.min(255,Math.max(0,(d[i+2]+br)*cf));
    const g=(d[i]*0.299+d[i+1]*0.587+d[i+2]*0.114);
    const v=g>th?255:0;d[i]=v;d[i+1]=v;d[i+2]=v;
  }
  cx.putImageData(id,0,0);toast('Filtros aplicados','success');
}
function vectorizeScan(){
  if(!scanImg){toast('Carga una imagen primero','error');return}
  applyScan();
  const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv'),cx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const id=cx.getImageData(0,0,cv.width,cv.height),d=id.data;
  cad.saveU();
  const scale=1/cad.zoom;
  // Detección simple de bordes: buscar transiciones blanco-negro
  for(let y=0;y<cv.height;y+=4){
    for(let x=0;x<cv.width;x++){
      const i=(y*cv.width+x)*4;
      if(d[i]>128&&x+1<cv.width){
        const i2=(y*cv.width+x+1)*4;
        if(d[i2]<128){
          const wx=(x-cv.width/2)*scale+cad.W/2/cad.zoom;
          const wy=(y-cv.height/2)*scale+cad.H/2/cad.zoom;
          cad.objs.push({type:'line',layer:cad.aLayer,x1:wx,y1:wy,x2:wx+scale,y2:wy,color:'#22C55E',lineWidth:1});
        }
      }
    }
  }
  cad.dirty=true;cad.render();toast('Vectorización completada','success');
}

/* ===== COLOR PICKER SYNC ===== */
document.getElementById('p-col').addEventListener('input',function(){cad.sCol=this.value;document.getElementById('p-col-t').value=this.value});
document.getElementById('p-col-t').addEventListener('input',function(){if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(this.value)){cad.sCol=this.value;document.getElementById('p-col').value=this.value}});

/* ===== INICIALIZACIÓN ===== */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  // Splash
  setTimeout(()=>{document.getElementById('splash').classList.add('hide')},1800);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    document.getElementById('app').classList.add('show');
    cad=new CAD(document.getElementById('cad'));
    cad.px=cad.W/2;cad.py=cad.H/2;
    cad.dirty=true;cad.render();
    // Init UI
    renderLayers();renderCatChips();filterUtils();renderFurn();renderSyms();
    // Tool buttons
    document.querySelectorAll('#toolstrip button[data-tool]').forEach(b=>{
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const t=b.dataset.tool;
        if(t==='furniture'){renderFurn();openM('furn-modal');return}
        if(t==='symbol'){renderSyms();openM('sym-modal');return}
        cad.setTool(t);
      });
    });
    // Nav buttons
    document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button[data-panel]').forEach(b=>{
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>togglePanel(b.dataset.panel));
    });
    // Search
    document.getElementById('usearch').addEventListener('input',filterUtils);
    // Cerrar modales al tocar overlay
    document.querySelectorAll('.mover').forEach(m=>{
      m.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===m)m.classList.remove('open')});
    });
    toast('ArquiCAD Pro listo','success');
  },2200);
});
</script>
</html>
