<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,maximum-scale=1,user-scalable=no">
<title>ArquiCAD Pro</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/space-grotesk@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/space-grotesk@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/space-grotesk@latest/latin-600-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/space-grotesk@latest/latin-700-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-400-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/jetbrains-mono@latest/latin-500-normal.css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css">
<style>
:root{--bg:#0A0A0A;--surface:#131313;--card:#1C1C1C;--card2:#252525;--border:#282828;--border2:#363636;--fg:#EAEAEA;--fg2:#999;--fg3:#5A5A5A;--accent:#FF6B2B;--accent2:#FF8C55;--accent3:rgba(255,107,43,.12);--green:#22C55E;--red:#EF4444;--blue:#3B82F6;--yellow:#FBBF24;--purple:#A855F7;--cyan:#06B6D4;--pink:#EC4899;--radius:10px;--font:'Space Grotesk',sans-serif;--mono:'JetBrains Mono',monospace}
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
body{font-family:var(--font);background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);overflow:hidden;height:100dvh;width:100vw;position:fixed;top:0;left:0}
::-webkit-scrollbar{width:4px}::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:transparent}::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:var(--border2);border-radius:4px}
#splash{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;background:var(--bg);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:opacity .5s,transform .5s}
#splash.hide{opacity:0;transform:scale(1.05);pointer-events:none}
.slogo{width:76px;height:76px;border:2.5px solid var(--accent);border-radius:16px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:30px;color:var(--accent);animation:pg 1.5s ease infinite}
@keyframes pg{0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(255,107,43,.35)}50%{box-shadow:0 0 28px 8px rgba(255,107,43,.12)}}
#splash h1{margin-top:14px;font-size:20px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:2px}
#splash p{color:var(--fg3);font-size:11px;margin-top:3px;letter-spacing:1px}
.sbar{width:110px;height:3px;background:var(--border);border-radius:3px;margin-top:20px;overflow:hidden}
.sbar-f{height:100%;background:var(--accent);border-radius:3px;animation:sload 1.6s ease forwards}
@keyframes sload{0%{width:0}60%{width:80%}100%{width:100%}}
#app{display:none;height:100dvh;flex-direction:column}
#app.show{display:flex}
header{height:46px;background:var(--surface);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;padding:0 6px;gap:2px;flex-shrink:0;z-index:50}
header .title{flex:1;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;padding:0 6px}
header button{width:34px;height:34px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:all .12s}
header button:active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent)}
.hsep{width:1px;height:22px;background:var(--border);margin:0 1px}
.main-area{flex:1;display:flex;overflow:hidden;position:relative}
#toolstrip{width:42px;background:var(--surface);border-right:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:3px 0;gap:1px;overflow-y:auto;flex-shrink:0;z-index:40}
#toolstrip button{width:34px;height:34px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:all .12s;position:relative;flex-shrink:0}
#toolstrip button:active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent)}
#toolstrip button.active{background:var(--accent3);color:var(--accent);box-shadow:inset 2px 0 0 var(--accent)}
#toolstrip .sep{width:22px;height:1px;background:var(--border);margin:1px 0;flex-shrink:0}
.cwrap{flex:1;position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:#0C0C0C}
#cad{display:block;touch-action:none;cursor:crosshair}
#coords{position:absolute;bottom:5px;left:5px;background:rgba(10,10,10,.88);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:2px 7px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);pointer-events:none;z-index:10}
#zoom-ind{position:absolute;bottom:5px;right:5px;background:rgba(10,10,10,.88);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:2px 7px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;color:var(--accent);pointer-events:none;z-index:10}
#tool-hint{position:absolute;top:5px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);background:rgba(10,10,10,.9);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:10px;color:var(--fg2);pointer-events:none;z-index:10;white-space:nowrap;transition:opacity .3s}
#bnav{height:50px;background:var(--surface);border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;z-index:50}
#bnav button{flex:1;height:100%;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);font-size:9px;font-family:var(--font);cursor:pointer;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:2px;transition:all .12s;position:relative}
#bnav button i{font-size:15px;transition:all .12s}
#bnav button:active,#bnav button.active{color:var(--accent)}
#bnav button.active i{transform:scale(1.12)}
#bnav button.active::before{content:'';position:absolute;top:0;width:28px;height:2px;background:var(--accent);border-radius:0 0 2px 2px}
.panel{position:fixed;bottom:50px;left:0;right:0;background:var(--surface);border-top:1px solid var(--border);z-index:45;transform:translateY(100%);transition:transform .28s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);max-height:62dvh;overflow-y:auto;border-radius:14px 14px 0 0}
.panel.open{transform:translateY(0)}
.phandle{width:32px;height:3px;background:var(--border2);border-radius:3px;margin:7px auto 0}
.phead{padding:8px 14px 10px;display:flex;align-items:center}
.phead h3{font-size:14px;font-weight:600;flex:1}
.phead button{width:30px;height:30px;border:none;background:var(--card);color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.pbody{padding:0 14px 16px}
.pr{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;margin-bottom:9px}
.pr label{font-size:10px;color:var(--fg2);width:68px;flex-shrink:0}
.pr input[type=number],.pr input[type=text],.pr select{flex:1;height:32px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 9px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--font);font-size:11px;outline:none;min-width:0}
.pr input:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.pr input[type=color]{width:32px;height:32px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;background:var(--card);padding:2px;cursor:pointer;flex-shrink:0}
.pr input[type=range]{flex:1;accent-color:var(--accent)}
.pr .val{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--accent);width:32px;text-align:right;flex-shrink:0}
.tog{width:36px;height:20px;background:var(--border2);border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;position:relative;transition:background .2s;flex-shrink:0}
.tog.on{background:var(--accent)}
.tog::after{content:'';position:absolute;top:2px;left:2px;width:16px;height:16px;background:#fff;border-radius:50%;transition:transform .2s}
.tog.on::after{transform:translateX(16px)}
.btn{height:32px;padding:0 14px;border:none;border-radius:7px;font-family:var(--font);font-size:11px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;transition:all .12s}
.btn:active{transform:scale(.96)}
.btn-a{background:var(--accent);color:#fff}
.btn-o{background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--fg2)}
.btn-d{background:var(--red);color:#fff}
.btn-s{height:28px;padding:0 9px;font-size:10px}
.li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:7px 9px;background:var(--card);border-radius:7px;margin-bottom:5px;border:1px solid transparent;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.li.active{border-color:var(--accent);background:var(--accent3)}
.li .ln{flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500}
.li .ldot{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0}
.li button{width:26px;height:26px;border:none;background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);border-radius:5px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.li button:active{color:var(--accent)}
#usearch{width:100%;height:35px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 11px 0 32px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--font);font-size:12px;outline:none}
#usearch:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.swrap{position:relative;margin-bottom:9px}
.swrap i{position:absolute;left:10px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);color:var(--fg3);font-size:12px}
.cscroll{display:flex;gap:5px;overflow-x:auto;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:8px}
.cscroll::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.cchip{height:26px;padding:0 10px;border-radius:13px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:transparent;color:var(--fg3);font-size:10px;font-family:var(--font);cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;transition:all .12s}
.cchip.active{background:var(--accent);border-color:var(--accent);color:#fff}
.ucount{font-size:10px;color:var(--fg3);margin-bottom:7px;font-family:var(--mono)}
.ugrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:7px}
.ucard{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:10px 5px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.ucard:active{transform:scale(.95);border-color:var(--accent)}
.ucard i{font-size:16px;margin-bottom:5px;display:block}
.ucard span{font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);line-height:1.25;display:block}
.mover{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.65);z-index:100;display:none;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:center}
.mover.open{display:flex}
.modal{background:var(--surface);border-radius:14px 14px 0 0;width:100%;max-height:78dvh;overflow-y:auto;animation:mup .25s ease}
@keyframes mup{from{transform:translateY(100%)}to{transform:translateY(0)}}
.mhead{display:flex;align-items:center;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--surface);z-index:1}
.mhead h3{flex:1;font-size:14px;font-weight:600}
.mhead button{width:30px;height:30px;border:none;background:var(--card);color:var(--fg2);border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px}
.mbody{padding:14px}
.ci{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;margin-bottom:10px}
.ci label{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg2);width:96px;flex-shrink:0}
.ci input{flex:1;height:38px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:0 9px;color:var(--fg);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;outline:none;min-width:0}
.ci input:focus{border-color:var(--accent)}
.ci .unit{font-size:10px;color:var(--fg3);width:34px;text-align:right;flex-shrink:0}
.cbtn{width:100%;height:40px;background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:9px;font-family:var(--font);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin:6px 0 14px;transition:all .12s}
.cbtn:active{transform:scale(.98);background:var(--accent2)}
.rbox{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:4px 12px}
.rrow{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);gap:8px}
.rrow:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.rrow .rl{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg2)}
.rrow .rv{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--accent);font-weight:600;text-align:right;max-width:58%;word-break:break-all}
.ecard{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:9px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:7px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;cursor:pointer;transition:all .12s}
.ecard:active{border-color:var(--accent);background:var(--accent3)}
.ecard .eicon{width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:9px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:16px;flex-shrink:0}
.ecard .einfo h4{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:1px}
.ecard .einfo p{font-size:9px;color:var(--fg3)}
.scanarea{background:var(--card);border:2px dashed var(--border2);border-radius:10px;padding:28px 16px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:14px;cursor:pointer}
.scanarea i{font-size:32px;color:var(--fg3);margin-bottom:8px;display:block}
.scanarea p{font-size:11px;color:var(--fg3)}
#scan-cv{width:100%;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:10px;display:none;background:#000}
.fgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:7px;margin-top:8px}
.fbtn{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:7px;padding:9px 3px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;font-size:9px;color:var(--fg2);transition:all .12s}
.fbtn:active{border-color:var(--accent);color:var(--accent)}
.fbtn .fpv{display:block;width:30px;height:30px;margin:0 auto 4px;color:var(--fg)}
#toast-c{position:fixed;top:52px;right:10px;z-index:200;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px}
.toast{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:11px;color:var(--fg);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;animation:tin .25s ease;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4);max-width:260px}
.toast.out{animation:tout .25s ease forwards}
@keyframes tin{from{opacity:0;transform:translateX(30px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateX(0)}}
@keyframes tout{to{opacity:0;transform:translateX(30px)}}
.toast i{font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0}
.toast.success i{color:var(--green)}.toast.error i{color:var(--red)}.toast.info i{color:var(--blue)}
.sep-t{border-top:1px solid var(--border);margin:10px 0;padding-top:10px}
@media(min-width:600px){.ugrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}.fgrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(6,1fr)}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="splash"><div class="slogo"><i class="fas fa-drafting-compass"></i></div><h1>ARQUICAD PRO</h1><p>CAD de Arquitectura Movil</p><div class="sbar"><div class="sbar-f"></div></div></div>

<div id="app">
<header>
<button onclick="newProject()"><i class="fas fa-bars"></i></button>
<span class="title" id="ptitle">Proyecto Sin Titulo</span>
<div class="hsep"></div>
<button onclick="cad&&cad.undo()" title="Deshacer"><i class="fas fa-rotate-left"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad&&cad.redo()" title="Rehacer"><i class="fas fa-rotate-right"></i></button>
<div class="hsep"></div>
<button onclick="cad&&cad.zoomIn()"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass-plus"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad&&cad.zoomOut()"><i class="fas fa-magnifying-glass-minus"></i></button>
<button onclick="cad&&cad.resetView()"><i class="fas fa-expand"></i></button>
</header>

<div class="main-area">
<div id="toolstrip">
<button data-tool="select" class="active" title="Seleccionar"><i class="fas fa-arrow-pointer"></i></button>
<button data-tool="move" title="Mover vista"><i class="fas fa-hand"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="line" title="Linea"><i class="fas fa-minus"></i></button>
<button data-tool="rect" title="Rectangulo"><i class="far fa-square"></i></button>
<button data-tool="circle" title="Circulo"><i class="far fa-circle"></i></button>
<button data-tool="arc" title="Arco"><i class="fas fa-bezier-curve"></i></button>
<button data-tool="polyline" title="Polilinea"><i class="fas fa-draw-polygon"></i></button>
<button data-tool="freehand" title="Mano alzada"><i class="fas fa-pencil"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="wall" title="Muro"><i class="fas fa-grip-lines-vertical"></i></button>
<button data-tool="door" title="Puerta"><i class="fas fa-door-open"></i></button>
<button data-tool="window" title="Ventana"><i class="fas fa-window-maximize"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="dimension" title="Cota"><i class="fas fa-ruler-horizontal"></i></button>
<button data-tool="text" title="Texto"><i class="fas fa-font"></i></button>
<button data-tool="furniture" title="Mobiliario"><i class="fas fa-couch"></i></button>
<button data-tool="symbol" title="Simbolos"><i class="fas fa-bolt"></i></button>
<div class="sep"></div>
<button data-tool="erase" title="Borrar"><i class="fas fa-eraser"></i></button>
</div>

<div class="cwrap" id="cwrap">
<canvas id="cad"></canvas>
<div id="coords">X: 0.0 Y: 0.0</div>
<div id="zoom-ind">100%</div>
<div id="tool-hint">Seleccionar objeto</div>
</div>
</div>

<nav id="bnav">
<button data-panel="dibujo" class="active"><i class="fas fa-pen-ruler"></i><span>Dibujo</span></button>
<button data-panel="capas"><i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i><span>Capas</span></button>
<button data-panel="utilidades"><i class="fas fa-calculator"></i><span>Utilidades</span></button>
<button data-panel="exportar"><i class="fas fa-file-export"></i><span>Exportar</span></button>
<button data-panel="escanear"><i class="fas fa-camera"></i><span>Escanear</span></button>
</nav>

<div class="panel" id="panel-dibujo"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Propiedades de Dibujo</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="pr"><label>Color linea</label><input type="color" id="p-col" value="#FF6B2B"><input type="text" id="p-col-t" value="#FF6B2B" style="flex:1"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Grosor</label><input type="range" id="p-w" min="1" max="20" value="2" oninput="cad.lineWidth=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-wv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-wv">2</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Relleno</label><div class="tog" id="p-fill" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.fillEnabled=this.classList.contains('on')"></div><input type="color" id="p-fillc" value="#FF6B2B" oninput="cad.fillColor=this.value"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Grosor muro</label><input type="range" id="p-wt" min="8" max="40" value="15" oninput="cad.wallThickness=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-wtv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-wtv">15</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Tam. texto</label><input type="range" id="p-fs" min="8" max="72" value="16" oninput="cad.fontSize=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-fsv').textContent=this.value"><span class="val" id="p-fsv">16</span></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Texto</label><input type="text" id="p-tc" value="Texto" oninput="cad.textContent=this.value"></div>
<div class="sep-t">
<div class="pr"><label>Cuadricula</label><div class="tog on" id="p-grid" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.showGrid=this.classList.contains('on');cad.dirty=true;cad.render()"></div></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Ajuste grid</label><div class="tog on" id="p-snap" onclick="this.classList.toggle('on');cad.snapEnabled=this.classList.contains('on')"></div></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Tam. grid</label><input type="range" id="p-gs" min="5" max="50" value="20" oninput="cad.gridSize=+this.value;document.getElementById('p-gsv').textContent=this.value;cad.dirty=true;cad.render()"><span class="val" id="p-gsv">20</span></div>
</div>
<div class="sep-t"><p style="font-size:10px;color:var(--fg3);margin-bottom:7px">ACCIONES</p><div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap">
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.clearAll()"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> Limpiar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.deleteSelected()"><i class="fas fa-times"></i> Eliminar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.duplicateSelected()"><i class="fas fa-clone"></i> Duplicar</button>
<button class="btn btn-o btn-s" onclick="cad.finishPolyline()"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Cerrar poli.</button>
</div></div>
</div></div>

<div class="panel" id="panel-capas"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Capas</h3><button onclick="addLayer()"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i></button></div><div class="pbody" id="layers-list"></div></div>

<div class="panel" id="panel-utilidades"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Utilidades</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="swrap"><i class="fas fa-search"></i><input type="text" id="usearch" placeholder="Buscar utilidad..."></div>
<div class="cscroll" id="cat-chips"></div>
<div class="ucount" id="ucount"></div>
<div class="ugrid" id="ugrid"></div>
</div></div>

<div class="panel" id="panel-exportar"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Exportar y Convertir</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportPNG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,.12);color:var(--red)"><i class="fas fa-image"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar PNG</h4><p>Imagen de alta resolucion</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportJPG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(251,191,36,.12);color:var(--yellow)"><i class="fas fa-file-image"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar JPG</h4><p>Imagen comprimida</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportSVG()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(34,197,94,.12);color:var(--green)"><i class="fas fa-vector-square"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar SVG</h4><p>Vector escalable</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportJSON()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(59,130,246,.12);color:var(--blue)"><i class="fas fa-code"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Guardar Proyecto JSON</h4><p>Formato nativo reabrible</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="importJSON()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(6,182,212,.12);color:var(--cyan)"><i class="fas fa-file-import"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Importar Proyecto JSON</h4><p>Cargar archivo guardado</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportDXF()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(168,85,247,.12);color:var(--purple)"><i class="fas fa-file-lines"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar DXF texto</h4><p>Compatible con AutoCAD</p></div></div>
<div class="ecard" onclick="exportCSV()"><div class="eicon" style="background:rgba(148,163,184,.12);color:#94A3B8"><i class="fas fa-table"></i></div><div class="einfo"><h4>Exportar CSV</h4><p>Lista de objetos con coordenadas</p></div></div>
</div></div>

<div class="panel" id="panel-escanear"><div class="phandle"></div><div class="phead"><h3>Escanear y Reconocer</h3></div><div class="pbody">
<div class="scanarea" onclick="openScanner()"><i class="fas fa-camera-retro"></i><p>Toca para cargar imagen de plano</p></div>
<canvas id="scan-cv"></canvas>
<div class="pr"><label>Brillo</label><input type="range" id="sc-br" min="-100" max="100" value="0" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Contraste</label><input type="range" id="sc-co" min="-100" max="100" value="30" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div class="pr"><label>Umbral</label><input type="range" id="sc-th" min="0" max="255" value="128" oninput="applyScan()"></div>
<div style="display:flex;gap:7px;margin-top:10px">
<button class="btn btn-o" onclick="applyScan()" style="flex:1"><i class="fas fa-sliders"></i> Filtros</button>
<button class="btn btn-a" onclick="vectorizeScan()" style="flex:1"><i class="fas fa-wand-magic-sparkles"></i> Vectorizar</button>
</div>
<button class="btn btn-o" style="width:100%;margin-top:7px" onclick="document.getElementById('sc-file').click()"><i class="fas fa-folder-open"></i> Cargar galeria</button>
<input type="file" id="sc-file" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="loadScanFile(event)">
</div></div>
</div>

<div class="mover" id="util-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3 id="um-title">Calculadora</h3><button onclick="closeM('util-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody" id="um-body"></div></div></div>
<div class="mover" id="furn-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3>Insertar Mobiliario</h3><button onclick="closeM('furn-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody"><div class="fgrid" id="furn-g"></div></div></div></div>
<div class="mover" id="sym-modal"><div class="modal"><div class="mhead"><h3>Simbolos Arquitectonicos</h3><button onclick="closeM('sym-modal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button></div><div class="mbody"><div class="fgrid" id="sym-g"></div></div></div></div>
<div id="toast-c"></div>
<input type="file" id="json-in" accept=".json" style="display:none" onchange="handleJSONImport(event)">

<script>
/* ============================================================
   ARQUICAD PRO — motor CAD + 201 utilidades de arquitectura
   ============================================================ */

/* ===== CATEGORIAS ===== */
const CATS=[
{id:'medicion',name:'Medicion',icon:'fa-ruler-combined',color:'#FF6B2B'},
{id:'areas',name:'Areas',icon:'fa-vector-square',color:'#22C55E'},
{id:'volumenes',name:'Volumenes',icon:'fa-cube',color:'#3B82F6'},
{id:'materiales',name:'Materiales',icon:'fa-cubes',color:'#F59E0B'},
{id:'estructural',name:'Estructural',icon:'fa-building',color:'#EF4444'},
{id:'electrico',name:'Electrico',icon:'fa-bolt',color:'#A855F7'},
{id:'fontaneria',name:'Fontaneria',icon:'fa-faucet-drip',color:'#06B6D4'},
{id:'climatizacion',name:'HVAC',icon:'fa-temperature-half',color:'#F97316'},
{id:'iluminacion',name:'Iluminacion',icon:'fa-lightbulb',color:'#FBBF24'},
{id:'conversion',name:'Conversiones',icon:'fa-arrows-rotate',color:'#6366F1'},
{id:'geometria',name:'Geometria',icon:'fa-compass-drafting',color:'#EC4899'},
{id:'costos',name:'Costos',icon:'fa-coins',color:'#14B8A6'},
{id:'escalas',name:'Escalas',icon:'fa-maximize',color:'#8B5CF6'},
{id:'terreno',name:'Terreno',icon:'fa-mountain',color:'#84CC16'},
{id:'acustica',name:'Acustica',icon:'fa-volume-high',color:'#D946EF'},
{id:'accesibilidad',name:'Accesibilidad',icon:'fa-wheelchair',color:'#0EA5E9'},
{id:'sol',name:'Sol/Sombra',icon:'fa-sun',color:'#FCD34D'},
{id:'misc',name:'Miscelanea',icon:'fa-toolbox',color:'#94A3B8'}
];

/* ===== 201 UTILIDADES  [id,nombre,catIdx,icono,[[key,label,unidad,text?]],fn(v)] ===== */
const U=[
/* Medicion */
['d2p','Distancia 2 puntos',0,'fa-ruler',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m']],v=>({Distancia:Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1).toFixed(4)+' m',dX:(v.x2-v.x1).toFixed(4)+' m',dY:(v.y2-v.y1).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['d3p','Distancia 3D',0,'fa-ruler-combined',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['z1','Z1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['z2','Z2','m']],v=>({Distancia:Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1,v.z2-v.z1).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['ang3p','Angulo 3 puntos',0,'fa-compass-drafting',[['ax','Ax','m'],['ay','Ay','m'],['bx','Bx vertice','m'],['by','By vertice','m'],['cx','Cx','m'],['cy','Cy','m']],v=>{const a1=Math.atan2(v.ay-v.by,v.ax-v.bx),a2=Math.atan2(v.cy-v.by,v.cx-v.bx);const g=((a2-a1)*180/Math.PI+360)%360;return{Angulo:g.toFixed(2)+'°',Radianes:((a2-a1+2*Math.PI)%(2*Math.PI)).toFixed(4)}}],
['pend','Pendiente',0,'fa-chart-line',[['dh','Desnivel','m'],['dd','Dist. horiz.','m']],v=>({Porcentaje:(v.dh/v.dd*100).toFixed(2)+'%',Angulo:(Math.atan(v.dh/v.dd)*180/Math.PI).toFixed(2)+'°',Razon:'1:'+(v.dh===0?'∞':Math.round(Math.abs(v.dd/v.dh)))})],
['perim','Perimetro lados',0,'fa-draw-polygon',[['l','Lados (coma sep.)','m',1]],v=>{const a=String(v.l).split(',').map(Number).filter(n=>!isNaN(n));return{Perimetro:a.reduce((s,b)=>s+b,0).toFixed(3)+' m',Lados:a.length}}],
['pmed','Punto medio',0,'fa-crosshairs',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m']],v=>({PM_X:((v.x1+v.x2)/2).toFixed(4)+' m',PM_Y:((v.y1+v.y2)/2).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['dacum','Dist. acumulada',0,'fa-route',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m',1]],v=>{const p=String(v.pts).split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number)).filter(q=>q.length===2&&!isNaN(q[0]));let d=0;for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)d+=Math.hypot(p[i][0]-p[i-1][0],p[i][1]-p[i-1][1]);return{Total:d.toFixed(3)+' m',Tramos:p.length-1}}],
['divseg','Dividir segmento',0,'fa-scissors',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['n','Divisiones','']],v=>{const r={};const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(v.n));for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const t=i/n;r['P'+i]='('+(v.x1+(v.x2-v.x1)*t).toFixed(2)+', '+(v.y1+(v.y2-v.y1)*t).toFixed(2)+')'}return r}],
['rcurv','Radio de curva',0,'fa-circle-notch',[['c','Cuerda','m'],['f','Flecha','m']],v=>{const r=v.c*v.c/(8*v.f)+v.f/2;const ang=2*Math.asin(Math.min(1,v.c/(2*r)));return{Radio:r.toFixed(4)+' m',Angulo:(ang*180/Math.PI).toFixed(2)+'°','Largo arco':(r*ang).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['inter2r','Interseccion 2 rectas',0,'fa-xmark',[['x1','X1 R1','m'],['y1','Y1 R1','m'],['x2','X2 R1','m'],['y2','Y2 R1','m'],['x3','X1 R2','m'],['y3','Y1 R2','m'],['x4','X2 R2','m'],['y4','Y2 R2','m']],v=>{const d=(v.x1-v.x2)*(v.y3-v.y4)-(v.y1-v.y2)*(v.x3-v.x4);if(Math.abs(d)<1e-10)return{Resultado:'Paralelas'};const t=((v.x1-v.x3)*(v.y3-v.y4)-(v.y1-v.y3)*(v.x3-v.x4))/d;return{IX:(v.x1+t*(v.x2-v.x1)).toFixed(4)+' m',IY:(v.y1+t*(v.y2-v.y1)).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['offl','Linea paralela',0,'fa-arrows-left-right',[['x1','X1','m'],['y1','Y1','m'],['x2','X2','m'],['y2','Y2','m'],['d','Offset','m']],v=>{const l=Math.hypot(v.x2-v.x1,v.y2-v.y1)||1,nx=-(v.y2-v.y1)/l,ny=(v.x2-v.x1)/l;return{P1:'('+(v.x1+nx*v.d).toFixed(2)+', '+(v.y1+ny*v.d).toFixed(2)+')',P2:'('+(v.x2+nx*v.d).toFixed(2)+', '+(v.y2+ny*v.d).toFixed(2)+')'}}],
['escd','Escala con distancia',0,'fa-ruler-horizontal',[['dr','Dist. real','m'],['dp','Dist. plano','cm']],v=>({Escala:'1:'+Math.round(v.dr*100/v.dp),Factor:(v.dr*100/v.dp).toFixed(4)})],
/* Areas */
['arect','Area rectangulo',1,'fa-square',[['a','Ancho','m'],['b','Alto','m']],v=>({Area:(v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(2*(v.a+v.b)).toFixed(4)+' m',Diagonal:Math.hypot(v.a,v.b).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['atri','Area triangulo',1,'fa-play',[['b','Base','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(v.b*v.h/2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['acirc','Area circulo',1,'fa-circle',[['r','Radio','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(2*Math.PI*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m',Diametro:(2*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['atrap','Area trapecio',1,'fa-minus',[['B','Base mayor','m'],['b','Base menor','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:((v.B+v.b)*v.h/2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['aelip','Area elipse',1,'fa-egg',[['a','Semieje may.','m'],['b','Semieje men.','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:'≈'+(Math.PI*(3*(v.a+v.b)-Math.sqrt((3*v.a+v.b)*(v.a+3*v.b)))).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['aromb','Area rombo',1,'fa-diamond',[['D','Diag. may.','m'],['d','Diag. men.','m']],v=>({Area:(v.D*v.d/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Lado:Math.hypot(v.D/2,v.d/2).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['apara','Area paralelogramo',1,'fa-vector-square',[['b','Base','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(v.b*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['apolir','Area poligono irregular',1,'fa-draw-polygon',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m',1]],v=>{const p=String(v.pts).split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number)).filter(q=>q.length===2&&!isNaN(q[0]));let a=0;for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%p.length;a+=p[i][0]*p[j][1]-p[j][0]*p[i][1]}return{Area:Math.abs(a/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Vertices:p.length}}],
['asect','Area sector circular',1,'fa-chart-pie',[['r','Radio','m'],['ang','Angulo','°']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.ang/360).toFixed(4)+' m²',Arco:(2*Math.PI*v.r*v.ang/360).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['acorona','Area corona circular',1,'fa-bullseye',[['R','Radio ext.','m'],['r','Radio int.','m']],v=>({Area:(Math.PI*(v.R*v.R-v.r*v.r)).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['apolreg','Area poligono regular',1,'fa-shapes',[['n','Lados',''],['l','Lado','m']],v=>({Area:(v.n*v.l*v.l/(4*Math.tan(Math.PI/v.n))).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:(v.n*v.l).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['acomp','Area compuesta',1,'fa-object-group',[['a1','Area 1','m²'],['a2','Area 2','m²'],['op','Op: 1=suma 2=resta','']],v=>({Resultado:(v.op===2?v.a1-v.a2:v.a1+v.a2).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['aterreno','Area por coordenadas',1,'fa-map',[['pts','X,Y (sep ;)','m',1]],v=>{const p=String(v.pts).split(';').map(s=>s.split(',').map(Number)).filter(q=>q.length===2&&!isNaN(q[0]));let a=0,per=0;for(let i=0;i<p.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%p.length;a+=p[i][0]*p[j][1]-p[j][0]*p[i][1];per+=Math.hypot(p[j][0]-p[i][0],p[j][1]-p[i][1])}return{Area:Math.abs(a/2).toFixed(4)+' m²',Perimetro:per.toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['averted','Caudal vertedero',1,'fa-water',[['L','Long. cresta','m'],['H','Carga','m'],['n','Coef (1-1.8)','']],v=>({Caudal:(2/3*v.n*v.L*Math.sqrt(2*9.81)*Math.pow(v.H,1.5)).toFixed(4)+' m³/s'})],
/* Volumenes */
['vpris','Vol. prisma',2,'fa-cube',[['a','Ancho','m'],['b','Largo','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.a*v.b*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area base':(v.a*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vcil','Vol. cilindro',2,'fa-circle',[['r','Radio','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area lat.':(2*Math.PI*v.r*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m²','Area total':(2*Math.PI*v.r*(v.r+v.h)).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vesf','Vol. esfera',2,'fa-globe',[['r','Radio','m']],v=>({Volumen:(4/3*Math.PI*Math.pow(v.r,3)).toFixed(4)+' m³','Area sup.':(4*Math.PI*v.r*v.r).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['vcono','Vol. cono',2,'fa-caret-up',[['r','Radio','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.r*v.r*v.h/3).toFixed(4)+' m³',Generatriz:Math.hypot(v.r,v.h).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['vpira','Vol. piramide',2,'fa-mountain',[['ab','Area base','m²'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.ab*v.h/3).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtronco','Tronco de cono',2,'fa-filter',[['R','R mayor','m'],['r','R menor','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*v.h/3*(v.R*v.R+v.r*v.r+v.R*v.r)).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtrap','Vol. trapecial',2,'fa-minus',[['B','Ancho sup.','m'],['b','Ancho inf.','m'],['l','Largo','m'],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.B+v.b)/2*v.l*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vtub','Vol. tuberia',2,'fa-grip-lines',[['di','Diam. int.','m'],['L','Longitud','m']],v=>({Volumen:(Math.PI*Math.pow(v.di/2,2)*v.L).toFixed(4)+' m³',Litros:(Math.PI*Math.pow(v.di/2,2)*v.L*1000).toFixed(2)+' L'})],
['vexc','Vol. excavacion',2,'fa-hole',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho sup.','m'],['a','Ancho inf.','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.A+v.a)/2*v.L*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vterra','Vol. terraza',2,'fa-layer-group',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(4)+' m³',Peso:(v.L*v.A*v.e*2400).toFixed(0)+' kg'})],
['vlosa','Vol. losa',2,'fa-table-cells',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(4)+' m³',Peso:(v.L*v.A*v.e*2400).toFixed(0)+' kg',Area:(v.L*v.A).toFixed(2)+' m²'})],
['vzanja','Vol. zanja',2,'fa-grip-lines-vertical',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho sup.','m'],['a','Ancho inf.','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:((v.A+v.a)/2*v.L*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['vcist','Cap. cisterna',2,'fa-droplet',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['P','Prof.','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.P).toFixed(4)+' m³',Litros:(v.L*v.A*v.P*1000).toFixed(0)+' L',Galones:(v.L*v.A*v.P*264.172).toFixed(0)+' gal'})],
['vrell','Vol. relleno',2,'fa-mound',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m'],['c','Compact. %','']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e*(1+v.c/100)).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
/* Materiales */
['mconcr','Concreto por vol.',3,'fa-cube',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>{const vol=v.L*v.A*v.e,s=Math.ceil(vol*7.5);return{Volumen:vol.toFixed(3)+' m³','Cemento (50kg)':s,'Arena (m³)':(vol*0.5).toFixed(3),'Grava (m³)':(vol*0.7).toFixed(3),'Agua (L)':Math.round(s*20)}}],
['mladr','Ladrillos por muro',3,'fa-border-all',[['L','Largo muro','m'],['H','Alto muro','m'],['la','Largo lad.','cm'],['al','Alto lad.','cm'],['j','Junta','cm']],v=>{const areaM=v.L*v.H,n=Math.ceil(areaM/(((v.la+v.j)/100)*((v.al+v.j)/100)));return{Area:areaM.toFixed(2)+' m²',Ladrillos:n,'Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(n*0.003)}}],
['mbloq','Bloques por muro',3,'fa-table-cells-large',[['L','Largo','m'],['H','Alto','m'],['la','Largo bloq.','cm'],['al','Alto bloq.','cm'],['j','Junta','cm']],v=>{const n=Math.ceil(v.L*v.H/(((v.la+v.j)/100)*((v.al+v.j)/100)));return{Area:(v.L*v.H).toFixed(2)+' m²',Bloques:n,'Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(n*0.002)}}],
['mpiso','Pisos/ceramicos',3,'fa-table-cells-large',[['A','Area','m²'],['la','Largo pieza','cm'],['an','Ancho pieza','cm'],['j','Junta','cm'],['dp','Desperdicio %','']],v=>{const ap=(v.la+v.j)/100*(v.an+v.j)/100;return{Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A/ap*(1+v.dp/100)),'Area pieza':ap.toFixed(4)+' m²','Cemento (sacos)':Math.ceil(v.A*0.003)}}],
['mpint','Pintura necesaria',3,'fa-paint-roller',[['A','Area','m²'],['man','Manos',''],['rend','Rend. m²/L','']],v=>({Litros:(v.A*v.man/v.rend).toFixed(1)+' L',Galones:(v.A*v.man/v.rend/3.785).toFixed(1)+' gal'})],
['mazul','Azulejos pared',3,'fa-border-all',[['A','Area','m²'],['la','Largo','cm'],['an','Ancho','cm'],['j','Junta','cm'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>{const ap=(v.la+v.j)/100*(v.an+v.j)/100;return{Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A/ap*(1+v.dp/100)),'Area pieza':ap.toFixed(4)+' m²'}}],
['mmort','Mortero',3,'fa-flask',[['vol','Volumen','m³'],['c','Prop. cemento',''],['a','Prop. arena','']],v=>{const t=v.c+v.a||1;return{Cemento:(v.vol*v.c/t*1.5).toFixed(3)+' m³ ('+Math.ceil(v.vol*v.c/t*1.5*1500/50)+' sacos)',Arena:(v.vol*v.a/t*1.5).toFixed(3)+' m³'}}],
['macero','Acero refuerzo',3,'fa-bars',[['n','Varillas',''],['d','Diametro','mm'],['L','Largo c/u','m'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>{const vol=Math.PI*Math.pow(v.d/2000,2)*v.L*v.n;return{Peso:(vol*7850).toFixed(2)+' kg','Long. total':(v.L*v.n*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(2)+' m'}}],
['mencof','Encofrado',3,'fa-box',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['H','Altura','m']],v=>({Area:(2*(v.L+v.A)*v.H).toFixed(2)+' m²','Tableros 1.22x2.44':Math.ceil(2*(v.L+v.A)*v.H/2.9768)})],
['myeso','Yeso/pegamento',3,'fa-paste',[['A','Area','m²'],['e','Espesor','mm'],['rend','Rend. kg/m²/mm','']],v=>({Kilos:(v.A*v.e*v.rend).toFixed(1)+' kg',Sacos:Math.ceil(v.A*v.e*v.rend/30)})],
['mteja','Tejas techo',3,'fa-house',[['A','Area','m²'],['rend','Piezas/m²',''],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>({Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A*v.rend*(1+v.dp/100))})],
['mlam','Laminas cubierta',3,'fa-layer-group',[['A','Area','m²'],['an','Ancho lam.','m'],['la','Largo lam.','m'],['sol','Solape %','']],v=>{const au=v.an*v.la*(1-v.sol/100);return{Laminas:Math.ceil(v.A/au),'Area util':au.toFixed(2)+' m²'}}],
['mvidr','Vidrio/cristal',3,'fa-clone',[['a','Ancho','m'],['h','Alto','m'],['n','Piezas','']],v=>({'Area total':(v.a*v.h*v.n).toFixed(2)+' m²','Area c/u':(v.a*v.h).toFixed(2)+' m²'})],
['mbarro','Tierra/barro',3,'fa-mound',[['L','Largo','m'],['A','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L*v.A*v.e).toFixed(3)+' m³',Toneladas:(v.L*v.A*v.e*1.5).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
['marenav','Arena por vol.',3,'fa-hill-rockslide',[['vol','Volumen','m³']],v=>({Toneladas:(v.vol*1.6).toFixed(2)+' t',Kilos:(v.vol*1600).toFixed(0)+' kg','Camiones 5m³':Math.ceil(v.vol/5)})],
['mpiedr','Piedra bruta',3,'fa-gem',[['A','Area','m²'],['e','Espesor','cm'],['dp','Desperd. %','']],v=>({Volumen:(v.A*v.e/100*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(3)+' m³',Toneladas:(v.A*v.e/100*2.5*(1+v.dp/100)).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
/* Estructural */
['evigam','Momento viga simple',4,'fa-arrows-left-right',[['c','Carga total','kN'],['L','Luz','m']],v=>({Momento:(v.c*v.L/8).toFixed(2)+' kN·m',Corte:(v.c/2).toFixed(2)+' kN'})],
['evigav','Viga voladizo',4,'fa-arrow-right',[['c','Carga','kN'],['L','Luz','m']],v=>({Momento:(v.c*v.L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m',Corte:v.c.toFixed(2)+' kN'})],
['ecol','Carga columna',4,'fa-arrows-up-down',[['A','Area trib.','m²'],['cv','Carga viva','kg/m²'],['cm','Carga muerta','kg/m²']],v=>({Total:((v.cv+v.cm)*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Viva:(v.cv*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Muerta:(v.cm*v.A).toFixed(0)+' kg',Ton:((v.cv+v.cm)*v.A/1000).toFixed(2)+' t'})],
['elosa','Momento losa',4,'fa-table-cells',[['w','Carga','kN/m²'],['L','Luz corto','m'],['lx','Luz largo','m']],v=>{const r=Math.max(1,v.lx/v.L);const m=r<=1?0.036:0.038;return{M_pos:(m*v.w*v.L*v.L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m/m',M_neg:(m*1.33*v.w*v.L*v.L).toFixed(2)+' kN·m/m',Corte:(v.w*v.L/2).toFixed(2)+' kN/m'}}],
['ecimt','Area cimentacion',4,'fa-building',[['P','Carga','ton'],['qs','Cap. suelo','kg/cm²'],['fs','Seguridad','']],v=>{const a=v.P*1000/(v.qs/v.fs);return{Area:a.toFixed(2)+' cm²',Lado:Math.sqrt(a).toFixed(1)+' cm'}}],
['eacmin','Acero min. losa',4,'fa-bars-staggered',[['b','Ancho','m'],['e','Espesor','m']],v=>{const as=0.0018*v.b*v.e*100;return{As_min:as.toFixed(4)+' cm²/m','#3':Math.ceil(as/0.71)+' varillas','#4':Math.ceil(as/1.27)+' varillas'}}],
['edefl','Deflexion max.',4,'fa-arrow-down-long',[['w','Carga','kN/m'],['L','Luz','m'],['I','Inercia','cm4'],['E','Elasticidad','MPa']],v=>{const d=5*v.w*Math.pow(v.L,4)/(384*v.E*1e6*v.I*1e-8);return{Deflexion:d.toFixed(6)+' m','L/360':(v.L/360).toFixed(6)+' m',Cumple:d<v.L/360?'Si':'No'}}],
['epesov','Peso propio viga',4,'fa-weight-hanging',[['b','Ancho','cm'],['h','Alto','cm'],['L','Largo','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.b*v.h/10000*v.L).toFixed(4)+' m³',Peso:(v.b*v.h/10000*v.L*2400).toFixed(1)+' kg','Peso/m':(v.b*v.h/10000*2400).toFixed(1)+' kg/m'})],
['ezapata','Zapata aislada',4,'fa-square',[['P','Carga','ton'],['qs','Suelo','kg/cm²']],v=>{const a=Math.sqrt(v.P*1000/v.qs);return{Lado:a.toFixed(0)+' cm',Area:(a*a).toFixed(0)+' cm²',Esfuerzo:(v.P*1000/(a*a)).toFixed(2)+' kg/cm²'}}],
['eesc','Escalera estruct.',4,'fa-stairs',[['H','Altura total','m'],['ch','Contrahuella','cm'],['hp','Huella','cm']],v=>{const n=Math.max(2,Math.round(v.H*100/v.ch));return{Huellas:n-1,'Contrahuella real':(v.H*100/n).toFixed(1)+' cm',Largo:((n-1)*v.hp/100).toFixed(2)+' m',Angulo:(Math.atan(v.H*100/n/v.hp)*180/Math.PI).toFixed(1)+'°',Blondel:(2*v.H*100/n+v.hp).toFixed(1)+' cm (ideal 62-64)'}}],
['etrap','Trabe losa col.',4,'fa-grip-lines',[['b','Ancho losa','m'],['L','Luz','m'],['w','Carga','kN/m²']],v=>({Momento:(v.w*v.b*v.L*v.L/8).toFixed(2)+' kN·m',Corte:(v.w*v.b*v.L/2).toFixed(2)+' kN'})],
['emuro','Muro retencion',4,'fa-shield-halved',[['H','Altura','m'],['phi','Friccion','°'],['gam','Peso suelo','ton/m³']],v=>{const ka=Math.pow(Math.tan((45-v.phi/2)*Math.PI/180),2);const p=0.5*ka*v.gam*v.H*v.H;return{Empuje:p.toFixed(2)+' ton/m','Punto aplic.':(v.H/3).toFixed(2)+' m',Ka:ka.toFixed(4)}}],
['einer','Inercia rectangular',4,'fa-rectangle-list',[['b','Ancho','cm'],['h','Alto','cm']],v=>({Ix:(v.b*Math.pow(v.h,3)/12).toFixed(2)+' cm⁴',Iy:(v.h*Math.pow(v.b,3)/12).toFixed(2)+' cm⁴',Sx:(v.b*v.h*v.h/6).toFixed(2)+' cm³'})],
['eunion','Conexion soldadura',4,'fa-link',[['F','Fuerza','kN'],['fu','Esf. adm.','MPa'],['e','Espesor','mm']],v=>({'Long. min':(v.F*1000/(v.fu*v.e)).toFixed(1)+' mm'})],
/* Electrico */
['elcaida','Caida de voltaje',5,'fa-bolt',[['I','Corriente','A'],['L','Longitud','m'],['S','Seccion','mm²'],['V','Voltaje','V']],v=>{const cv=2*v.I*v.L/(56*v.S);return{Caida:(cv*100/v.V).toFixed(2)+'%',Caida_V:cv.toFixed(2)+' V',Cumple:cv*100/v.V<3?'Si (≤3%)':'No'}}],
['elcable','Seccion cable',5,'fa-plug',[['P','Potencia','W'],['V','Voltaje','V'],['L','Longitud','m'],['ca','Caida max %','']],v=>{const i=v.P/v.V,S=2*i*v.L/(56*v.ca/100*v.V);return{Corriente:i.toFixed(2)+' A',Seccion:S.toFixed(2)+' mm²',AWG:S<2.08?'14':S<3.31?'12':S<5.26?'10':S<8.37?'8':'6'}}],
['elcirc','Circuitos potencia',5,'fa-circle-nodes',[['P','Potencia','W'],['V','Voltaje','V'],['fp','Factor pot.','']],v=>({I:(v.P/(v.V*v.fp)).toFixed(2)+' A','Circ. 15A':Math.ceil(v.P/(v.V*15*v.fp)),'Circ. 20A':Math.ceil(v.P/(v.V*20*v.fp)),Disyuntor:Math.ceil(v.P/(v.V*v.fp)*1.25)+' A'})],
['elilum','Puntos luz',5,'fa-lightbulb',[['A','Area','m²'],['lux','Lux req.',''],['lp','Lumenes/punto','']],v=>({Puntos:Math.ceil(v.A*v.lux/v.lp),'W est.':Math.ceil(v.A*v.lux/v.lp*10)})],
['eltoma','Tomacorrientes',5,'fa-plug-circle-bolt',[['A','Area','m²'],['d','Dist. tomas','m']],v=>({Perim:(2*Math.sqrt(v.A)*2).toFixed(1)+' m',Tomas:Math.max(1,Math.ceil(2*Math.sqrt(v.A)*2/v.d))})],
['eltab','Cargas tablero',5,'fa-list-check',[['n','Circuitos',''],['pc','Pot/circuito','W'],['fp','Factor pot.',''],['d','Demanda %','']],v=>({'P. total':(v.n*v.pc).toFixed(0)+' W',Demanda:(v.n*v.pc*v.d/100).toFixed(0)+' W',I:(v.n*v.pc*v.d/100/(220*v.fp)).toFixed(2)+' A'})],
['elres','Resistencia elec.',5,'fa-wave-square',[['V','Voltaje','V'],['I','Corriente','A']],v=>({R:(v.V/v.I).toFixed(4)+' Ω',P:(v.V*v.I).toFixed(2)+' W'})],
['elpeso','Peso conductor',5,'fa-weight-hanging',[['S','Seccion','mm²'],['L','Longitud','m'],['den','Densidad g/cm³','']],v=>({Peso:(v.S*v.L*v.den*0.01).toFixed(2)+' kg'})],
['eltierra','Electrodo tierra',5,'fa-arrow-down-to-line',[['R','Resist. deseada','Ω'],['rho','Resist. suelo','Ω·m']],v=>({'Long. varilla':(v.R*v.rho/(Math.PI*0.016)).toFixed(2)+' m (Ø16mm)'})],
['eldif','Disyuntor dif.',5,'fa-shield-halved',[['I','Corriente','A'],['sen','Sensib. mA','']],v=>({Modelo:v.I+'A / '+v.sen+'mA','Tiempo disco.':'< 0.2s'})],
['eltransf','Transformador',5,'fa-right-left',[['P','Potencia','kVA'],['V1','Primario','V'],['V2','Secundario','V']],v=>({I1:(v.P*1000/v.V1).toFixed(2)+' A',I2:(v.P*1000/v.V2).toFixed(2)+' A',Relacion:(v.V1/v.V2).toFixed(4)})],
['elbat','Bateria/inversor',5,'fa-car-battery',[['P','Potencia','W'],['h','Autonomia h',''],['V','Voltaje bat.','V'],['ef','Rend. %','']],v=>({'Capacidad (Ah)':(v.P*v.h/(v.V*v.ef/100)).toFixed(0),'Energia (kWh)':(v.P*v.h/1000).toFixed(2)})],
/* Fontaneria */
['fotubo','Diam. tubo',6,'fa-faucet-drip',[['q','Caudal','L/s'],['vel','Vel. max','m/s']],v=>({Diametro:(Math.sqrt(4*v.q*0.001/(Math.PI*v.vel))*1000).toFixed(1)+' mm'})],
['focaid','Caida tuberia',6,'fa-arrow-trend-down',[['L','Longitud','m'],['D','Diam. int mm',''],['Q','Caudal L/s','']],v=>{const A=Math.PI*Math.pow(v.D/2000,2),vel=v.Q*0.001/A,f=0.02,hf=f*v.L*vel*vel/(2*9.81*v.D/1000);return{Velocidad:vel.toFixed(2)+' m/s',Caida:hf.toFixed(4)+' m','Caida/100m':(hf/v.L*100).toFixed(2)+' m/100m'}}],
['focist','Tiempo llenado',6,'fa-fill-drip',[['vol','Volumen','L'],['q','Caudal','L/min']],v=>({Tiempo:(v.vol/v.q).toFixed(1)+' min',Horas:(v.vol/v.q/60).toFixed(2)+' h'})],
['fobaj','Bajada pluvial',6,'fa-cloud-rain',[['A','Area techo','m²'],['i','Intensidad','mm/h']],v=>{const q=v.A*v.i/3600;return{Caudal:q.toFixed(3)+' L/s','Diam. min':q<2?'75mm':q<5?'100mm':'150mm'}}],
['fofosa','Fosa septica',6,'fa-house-chimney',[['per','Personas',''],['vol','L/persona','']],v=>{const v2=v.per*v.vol/1000,l=Math.cbrt(v2);return{Volumen:v2.toFixed(2)+' m³',Dim:l.toFixed(2)+' x '+l.toFixed(2)+' x '+(l*1.5).toFixed(2)+' m'}}],
['fodren','Drenaje frances',6,'fa-water',[['L','Longitud','m'],['A','Area drenada','m²'],['i','Intensidad','mm/h']],v=>({Caudal:(v.A*v.i/3600).toFixed(3)+' L/s','Grava (m³)':(v.L*0.3*0.2).toFixed(2)+' m³'})],
['fopres','Presion hidrost.',6,'fa-gauge-high',[['h','Altura col.','m']],v=>({Presion:(v.h*9.81).toFixed(2)+' kPa','kg/cm²':(v.h/10).toFixed(2),PSI:(v.h*1.422).toFixed(2)})],
['fogasto','Gasto sanitario',6,'fa-sink',[['per','Personas',''],['dot','L/persona/dia','']],v=>({'Gasto dia':(v.per*v.dot).toFixed(0)+' L/dia','Gasto medio':(v.per*v.dot/86400*1000).toFixed(2)+' mL/s','Gasto max':(v.per*v.dot/86400*1000*2.5).toFixed(2)+' mL/s'})],

['fovent','Ventilacion cam.',6,'fa-wind',[['vol','Volumen','m³'],['ren','Renov./hora','']],v=>({Caudal:(v.vol*v.ren/3600).toFixed(3)+' m³/s',Litros:(v.vol*v.ren*1000/3600).toFixed(0)+' L/h'})],
/* HVAC */
['hvacbtu','Calculo BTU',7,'fa-temperature-half',[['A','Area','m²'],['per','Personas',''],['tipo','1=sombra 2=normal 3=sol','']],v=>{const f=[50,60,80][Math.round(v.tipo)-1]||60,btu=v.A*f+v.per*600;return{BTU:Math.ceil(btu),Toneladas:(btu/12000).toFixed(2),Watts:Math.ceil(btu*0.293)}}],
['hvaccarga','Carga termica',7,'fa-fire',[['A','Area muros','m²'],['u','U muros','W/m²K'],['dt','dT','°C'],['Av','Area vidrio','m²'],['uv','U vidrio','W/m²K']],v=>({Q_muros:(v.A*v.u*v.dt).toFixed(0)+' W',Q_vidrio:(v.Av*v.uv*v.dt).toFixed(0)+' W',Total:(v.A*v.u*v.dt+v.Av*v.uv*v.dt).toFixed(0)+' W',BTU:Math.ceil((v.A*v.u*v.dt+v.Av*v.uv*v.dt)*3.41)})],
['hvacduct','Ducto rect.',7,'fa-square',[['Q','Caudal','m³/h'],['v2','Velocidad','m/s']],v=>{const a=v.Q/3600/v.v2;return{Area:a.toFixed(4)+' m²',Dim:Math.round(Math.sqrt(a)*100)+'x'+Math.round(Math.sqrt(a)*100)+' cm'}}],
['hvacaire','Renovacion aire',7,'fa-wind',[['V','Volumen','m³'],['per','Personas',''],['ren','Renov./h','']],v=>({Caudal:(v.V*v.ren/3600).toFixed(3)+' m³/s','L/s por persona':(v.V*v.ren/3600/v.per*1000).toFixed(0)})],
['hvacsplt','Split por area',7,'fa-snowflake',[['A','Area','m²']],v=>{const b=Math.ceil(v.A*600);return{BTU:b,Modelo:b<9000?'9000':b<12000?'12000':b<18000?'18000':b<24000?'24000':'36000'}}],
['hvaccirc','Ducto circular',7,'fa-circle',[['Q','Caudal','m³/h'],['v2','Velocidad','m/s']],v=>({Diametro:(Math.sqrt(4*v.Q/3600/(Math.PI*v.v2))*1000).toFixed(0)+' mm'})],
['hvacaisl','Aislamiento term.',7,'fa-shield',[['A','Area','m²'],['Ua','U actual','W/m²K'],['Ud','U deseado','W/m²K'],['k','Cond. aisl.','W/mK']],v=>({Espesor:((1/v.Ud-1/v.Ua)/v.k*1000).toFixed(1)+' mm',Ahorro:((1-v.Ud/v.Ua)*100).toFixed(1)+'%'})],
['hvachum','Humedad relativa',7,'fa-droplet',[['T','Temperatura','°C'],['Tr','T rocio','°C']],v=>({HR:Math.min(100,Math.exp(17.27*v.Tr/(237.7+v.Tr)-17.27*v.T/(237.7+v.T))*100).toFixed(1)+'%'})],

/* Iluminacion */
['ilulux','Lux a lumenes',8,'fa-lightbulb',[['A','Area','m²'],['lux','Lux',''],['uf','Factor util.',''],['fm','Factor mant.','']],v=>({Lumenes:(v.A*v.lux/(v.uf*v.fm)).toFixed(0)+' lm',Luminarias:Math.ceil(v.A*v.lux/(v.uf*v.fm)/3000)})],
['iluptos','Dist. puntos luz',8,'fa-border-all',[['a','Ancho','m'],['l','Largo','m'],['s','Separacion','m']],v=>({Nx:Math.ceil(v.a/v.s),Ny:Math.ceil(v.l/v.s),Total:Math.ceil(v.a/v.s)*Math.ceil(v.l/v.s)})],
['ilubrac','Braquete/punto',8,'fa-circle-dot',[['n','Cantidad',''],['lp','Lumenes/punto',''],['h','Altura','m']],v=>({'Total lm':v.n*v.lp,'Lux aprox':(v.n*v.lp/(4*Math.PI*v.h*v.h)).toFixed(1)})],
['iluef','Eficiencia lum.',8,'fa-gauge-high',[['lp','Lumenes',''],['pw','Potencia','W']],v=>({Eficiencia:(v.lp/v.pw).toFixed(1)+' lm/W',Clas:v.lp/v.pw>90?'A':v.lp/v.pw>70?'B':v.lp/v.pw>50?'C':'D'})],
['ilucont','Contraste luminancia',8,'fa-circle-half-stroke',[['L1','Luminancia 1','cd/m²'],['L2','Luminancia 2','cd/m²']],v=>{const c=(Math.max(v.L1,v.L2)+0.05)/(Math.min(v.L1,v.L2)+0.05);return{Contraste:c.toFixed(2)+':1',WCAG:c>=4.5?'AA Si':'AA No'}}],
['ilutemp','Temp. color',8,'fa-temperature-half',[['k','Kelvin','']],v=>({Tipo:v.k<3000?'Calido':v.k<4500?'Neutro':v.k<6500?'Frio':'Muy frio',Aprox:v.k<3000?'#FF9329':v.k<4000?'#FFB76B':v.k<5000?'#FFD4A0':v.k<6000?'#FFF1E0':'#E8F0FF'})],
['iluucr','Uniformidad UCR',8,'fa-equals',[['emin','Lux min',''],['emax','Lux max','']],v=>({UCR:(v.emin/v.emax).toFixed(3),Cumple:v.emin/v.emax>=0.6?'Si (≥0.6)':'No'})],
['iluemer','Ilumin. emergencia',8,'fa-triangle-exclamation',[['A','Area','m²'],['lux','Lux min','']],v=>({Lumenes:(v.A*v.lux/0.8).toFixed(0)+' lm',Luminarias:Math.ceil(v.A*v.lux/0.8/800)})],
/* Conversiones */
['cvlong','Longitud',9,'fa-ruler-horizontal',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:m 2:cm 3:mm 4:in 5:ft 6:yd 7:km 8:mi','']],v=>{const f=[1,.01,.001,.0254,.3048,.9144,1000,1609.344],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{m:m.toFixed(6),cm:(m*100).toFixed(4),mm:(m*1000).toFixed(2),in:(m/.0254).toFixed(4),ft:(m/.3048).toFixed(4),yd:(m/.9144).toFixed(4),km:(m/1000).toFixed(6),mi:(m/1609.344).toFixed(6)}}],
['cvarea','Area',9,'fa-vector-square',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:m² 2:cm² 3:mm² 4:ft² 5:yd² 6:ha 7:ac 8:in²','']],v=>{const f=[1,1e-4,1e-6,.092903,.836127,1e4,4046.86,6.4516e-4],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'m²':m.toFixed(6),'cm²':(m*1e4).toFixed(2),'ft²':(m/.092903).toFixed(4),ha:(m/1e4).toFixed(6),acres:(m/4046.86).toFixed(6)}}],
['cvvol','Volumen',9,'fa-cube',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:m³ 2:L 3:cm³ 4:gal 5:ft³ 6:yd³','']],v=>{const f=[1,.001,1e-6,.003785,.028317,.764555],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'m³':m.toFixed(6),Litros:(m*1000).toFixed(2),'cm³':(m*1e6).toFixed(0),galones:(m/.003785).toFixed(2),'ft³':(m/.028317).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvpeso','Peso/Masa',9,'fa-weight-hanging',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:kg 2:g 3:lb 4:oz 5:ton 6:tonUK','']],v=>{const f=[1,.001,.453592,.0283495,1000,1016.05],k=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{kg:k.toFixed(4),g:(k*1000).toFixed(1),lb:(k/.453592).toFixed(4),oz:(k/.0283495).toFixed(2),ton:(k/1000).toFixed(6)}}],
['cvtemp','Temperatura',9,'fa-temperature-half',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:°C 2:°F 3:K','']],v=>{let c;const d=Math.round(v.d);d===1?c=v.val:d===2?c=(v.val-32)*5/9:c=v.val-273.15;return{'°C':c.toFixed(2),'°F':(c*9/5+32).toFixed(2),K:(c+273.15).toFixed(2)}}],
['cvvel','Velocidad',9,'fa-gauge',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:m/s 2:km/h 3:ft/s 4:mph 5:nudo','']],v=>{const f=[1,1/3.6,.3048,.44704,.514444],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'m/s':m.toFixed(4),'km/h':(m*3.6).toFixed(2),'ft/s':(m/.3048).toFixed(2),mph:(m/.44704).toFixed(2),nudos:(m/.514444).toFixed(2)}}],
['cvpres','Presion',9,'fa-gauge-high',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:kPa 2:MPa 3:bar 4:psi 5:atm 6:kg/cm²','']],v=>{const f=[1,1000,100,6.89476,101.325,98.0665],k=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{kPa:k.toFixed(2),MPa:(k/1000).toFixed(4),bar:(k/100).toFixed(4),PSI:(k/6.89476).toFixed(2),atm:(k/101.325).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvang','Angulos',9,'fa-compass',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:grados 2:radianes 3:gradianes','']],v=>{let d;const t=Math.round(v.d);t===1?d=v.val:t===2?d=v.val*180/Math.PI:d=v.val*0.9;return{Grados:d.toFixed(4),Radianes:(d*Math.PI/180).toFixed(6),Gradianes:(d/0.9).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvpot','Potencia',9,'fa-bolt',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:W 2:kW 3:HP 4:BTU/h 5:cal/s','']],v=>{const f=[1,1000,745.7,.293071,4.1868],w=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{W:w.toFixed(2),kW:(w/1000).toFixed(4),HP:(w/745.7).toFixed(4),'BTU/h':(w/.293071).toFixed(2)}}],
['cven','Energia',9,'fa-battery-full',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:J 2:kJ 3:kWh 4:cal 5:kcal 6:BTU','']],v=>{const f=[1,1000,3.6e6,4.1868,4186.8,1055.06],j=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{J:j.toFixed(2),kJ:(j/1000).toFixed(4),kWh:(j/3.6e6).toFixed(6),kcal:(j/4186.8).toFixed(4),BTU:(j/1055.06).toFixed(2)}}],
['cvflujo','Flujo vol.',9,'fa-faucet-drip',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:L/s 2:L/min 3:m³/h 4:gpm 5:ft³/min','']],v=>{const f=[.001,1/60,1/3600,.00006309,.000471947],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'L/s':(m*1000).toFixed(4),'L/min':(m*60000).toFixed(2),'m³/h':(m*3600).toFixed(4),GPM:(m/.00006309).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvden','Densidad',9,'fa-atom',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:kg/m³ 2:g/cm³ 3:lb/ft³ 4:kg/L','']],v=>{const f=[1,1000,16.0185,1000],k=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'kg/m³':k.toFixed(2),'g/cm³':(k/1000).toFixed(4),'lb/ft³':(k/16.0185).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvfue','Fuerza',9,'fa-hand-fist',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:N 2:kN 3:kgf 4:lbf 5:tonf','']],v=>{const f=[1,1000,9.80665,4.44822,9806.65],n=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{N:n.toFixed(2),kN:(n/1000).toFixed(4),kgf:(n/9.80665).toFixed(4),lbf:(n/4.44822).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvtorq','Torque',9,'fa-rotate',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:N·m 2:kgf·m 3:lbf·ft 4:lbf·in','']],v=>{const f=[1,9.80665,1.35582,.112985],n=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'N·m':n.toFixed(4),'kgf·m':(n/9.80665).toFixed(4),'lbf·ft':(n/1.35582).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvpend','% a grados',9,'fa-chart-line',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:% 2:grados 3:razon 1:X','']],v=>{let g;const t=Math.round(v.d);t===1?g=Math.atan(v.val/100)*180/Math.PI:t===2?g=v.val:g=Math.atan(1/v.val)*180/Math.PI;return{Porcentaje:(Math.tan(g*Math.PI/180)*100).toFixed(2)+'%',Grados:g.toFixed(2)+'°'}}],
['cviner','Inercia',9,'fa-circle-dot',[['val','Valor',''],['d','1:cm⁴ 2:m⁴ 3:in⁴ 4:ft⁴','']],v=>{const f=[1e-8,1,4.16231e-7,8.63097e-3],m=v.val*f[Math.round(v.d)-1];return{'cm⁴':(m/1e-8).toFixed(4),'m⁴':m.toFixed(10),'in⁴':(m/4.16231e-7).toFixed(4)}}],
['cvpy','Pies/pulg a m',9,'fa-ruler',[['p','Pies',''],['pu','Pulgadas','']],v=>({Metros:((v.p*12+v.pu)*0.0254).toFixed(4),Centimetros:((v.p*12+v.pu)*2.54).toFixed(2)})],
['cvmp','Metros a pies/pulg',9,'fa-ruler-vertical',[['m','Metros','']],v=>({Resultado:Math.floor(v.m/.3048)+"' "+Math.round((v.m%.3048)/.0254)+'"',Pulgadas:(v.m/.0254).toFixed(2)+'"'})],
['cvcoord','Decimal a DMS',9,'fa-map-pin',[['val','Valor decimal','°']],v=>{const s=v.val<0?'-':'';const a=Math.abs(v.val);return{DMS:s+Math.floor(a)+'° '+Math.floor((a%1)*60)+"' "+(((a%1)*60%1)*60).toFixed(1)+'"'}}],
['cvesc','Escala numerica',9,'fa-maximize',[['e','Escala 1:X','']],v=>({'1cm = ':(v.e/100).toFixed(2)+' m','1m plano = ':(100/v.e).toFixed(4)+' m real','10m = ':(1000/v.e).toFixed(2)+' cm'})],
/* Geometria */
['geopit','Pitagoras',10,'fa-right-long',[['a','Cateto a','m'],['b','Cateto b','m']],v=>({Hipotenusa:Math.hypot(v.a,v.b).toFixed(4)+' m','a²+b²':(v.a*v.a+v.b*v.b).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['geotrig','Trigonometria',10,'fa-compass-drafting',[['ang','Angulo','°'],['h','Hipotenusa','m']],v=>({Seno:Math.sin(v.ang*Math.PI/180).toFixed(6),Coseno:Math.cos(v.ang*Math.PI/180).toFixed(6),Tangente:Math.tan(v.ang*Math.PI/180).toFixed(6),'Cat. op.':(Math.sin(v.ang*Math.PI/180)*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m','Cat. ady.':(Math.cos(v.ang*Math.PI/180)*v.h).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['geocos','Ley cosenos',10,'fa-draw-polygon',[['a','Lado a','m'],['b','Lado b','m'],['C','Ang. op. c','°']],v=>({c:Math.sqrt(v.a*v.a+v.b*v.b-2*v.a*v.b*Math.cos(v.C*Math.PI/180)).toFixed(4)+' m'})],
['geosen','Ley senos',10,'fa-shapes',[['a','Lado a','m'],['A','Angulo A','°'],['B','Angulo B','°']],v=>({b:(v.a*Math.sin(v.B*Math.PI/180)/Math.sin(v.A*Math.PI/180)).toFixed(4)+' m','Ang C':(180-v.A-v.B).toFixed(1)+'°'})],
['geodpr','Dist. punto-recta',10,'fa-ruler-combined',[['x0','X punto','m'],['y0','Y punto','m'],['x1','X1 recta','m'],['y1','Y1 recta','m'],['x2','X2 recta','m'],['y2','Y2 recta','m']],v=>{const d=Math.abs((v.y2-v.y1)*v.x0-(v.x2-v.x1)*v.y0+v.x2*v.y1-v.y2*v.x1)/Math.hypot(v.y2-v.y1,v.x2-v.x1);return{Distancia:d.toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['geocirc','Circunsc./inscr.',10,'fa-circle',[['a','Lado','m'],['n','Lados','']],v=>({R_insc:(v.a/(2*Math.tan(Math.PI/v.n))).toFixed(4)+' m',R_circum:(v.a/(2*Math.sin(Math.PI/v.n))).toFixed(4)+' m',Area:(v.n*v.a*v.a/(4*Math.tan(Math.PI/v.n))).toFixed(4)+' m²'})],
['geoheron','Formula Heron',10,'fa-play',[['a','Lado a','m'],['b','Lado b','m'],['c','Lado c','m']],v=>{const s=(v.a+v.b+v.c)/2,ar=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,s*(s-v.a)*(s-v.b)*(s-v.c)));return{Semiperim:s.toFixed(4)+' m',Area:ar.toFixed(4)+' m²',Valido:s>v.a&&s>v.b&&s>v.c?'Si':'No'}}],
['geocoord','Dist. coordenadas GPS',10,'fa-location-dot',[['la1','Lat 1','°'],['lo1','Lon 1','°'],['la2','Lat 2','°'],['lo2','Lon 2','°']],v=>{const R=6371,dLa=(v.la2-v.la1)*Math.PI/180,dLo=(v.lo2-v.lo1)*Math.PI/180,a2=Math.sin(dLa/2)**2+Math.cos(v.la1*Math.PI/180)*Math.cos(v.la2*Math.PI/180)*Math.sin(dLo/2)**2;return{Distancia:(R*2*Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a2),Math.sqrt(1-a2))).toFixed(2)+' km'}}],
['geoperp','Pie perpendicular',10,'fa-crosshairs',[['x0','X punto','m'],['y0','Y punto','m'],['x1','X1 recta','m'],['y1','Y1 recta','m'],['x2','X2 recta','m'],['y2','Y2 recta','m']],v=>{const dx=v.x2-v.x1,dy=v.y2-v.y1,l2=dx*dx+dy*dy||1,t=((v.x0-v.x1)*dx+(v.y0-v.y1)*dy)/l2,px=v.x1+t*dx,py=v.y1+t*dy;return{Pie:'('+px.toFixed(2)+', '+py.toFixed(2)+')',Dist:Math.hypot(v.x0-px,v.y0-py).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
['geobis','Bisectriz angulo',10,'fa-angle-right',[['ax','Ax','m'],['ay','Ay','m'],['bx','Bx vert.','m'],['by','By vert.','m'],['cx','Cx','m'],['cy','Cy','m']],v=>{const d1=Math.hypot(v.ax-v.bx,v.ay-v.by)||1,d2=Math.hypot(v.cx-v.bx,v.cy-v.by)||1;const px=(v.ax/d1+v.cx/d2)/(1/d1+1/d2)+v.bx,py=(v.ay/d1+v.cy/d2)/(1/d1+1/d2)+v.by;return{Punto:'('+px.toFixed(2)+', '+py.toFixed(2)+')'}}],
['georot','Rotacion punto',10,'fa-rotate-right',[['x','X','m'],['y','Y','m'],['ang','Angulo','°'],['cx','Centro X','m'],['cy','Centro Y','m']],v=>{const a=v.ang*Math.PI/180,dx=v.x-v.cx,dy=v.y-v.cy;return{X:(v.cx+dx*Math.cos(a)-dy*Math.sin(a)).toFixed(4)+' m',Y:(v.cy+dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a)).toFixed(4)+' m'}}],
/* Costos */
['cosmat','Presupuesto mats.',11,'fa-coins',[['it','nom:precio:cant (sep ;)','',1]],v=>{const r={};let t=0;String(v.it).split(';').forEach(s=>{const p=s.split(':');if(p.length>=3){const c=parseFloat(p[1])*parseFloat(p[2]);if(!isNaN(c)){r[p[0].trim()]=c.toFixed(2);t+=c}}});r.TOTAL=t.toFixed(2);return r}],
['cosmo','Mano de obra',11,'fa-helmet-safety',[['j','Jornal',''],['d','Dias',''],['o','Obreros','']],v=>({Total:(v.j*v.d*v.o).toFixed(2),'Por dia':(v.j*v.o).toFixed(2),'Por semana':(v.j*v.o*6).toFixed(2)})],
['cosm2','Costo por m²',11,'fa-calculator',[['c','Costo total',''],['a','Area','m²']],v=>({'Costo/m²':(v.c/v.a).toFixed(2),'Costo/ft²':(v.c/v.a/10.764).toFixed(2)})],
['cospu','Precio unitario',11,'fa-receipt',[['mat','Materiales',''],['mo','Mano obra',''],['ind','Indirectos %',''],['uti','Utilidad %','']],v=>{const s=+v.mat+ +v.mo;return{Subtotal:s.toFixed(2),Indirectos:(s*v.ind/100).toFixed(2),Utilidad:(s*(1+v.ind/100)*v.uti/100).toFixed(2),PU:(s*(1+v.ind/100)*(1+v.uti/100)).toFixed(2)}}],
['cosamort','Amortizacion eq.',11,'fa-truck',[['c','Costo eq.',''],['v2','Vida util años',''],['u','Uso anual dias',''],['d','Dias proyecto','']],v=>({'Costo/dia':(v.c/(v.v2*v.u)).toFixed(2),'Costo proy.':(v.c/(v.v2*v.u)*v.d).toFixed(2)})],
['cosconc','Costo concreto/m³',11,'fa-cube',[['fc','fc 150/200/250/300/350','kg/cm²']],v=>{const p={150:1800,200:2200,250:2600,300:3000,350:3500};const pr=p[Math.round(v.fc/50)*50]||2500;return{'Precio/m³':'$'+pr,'Precio saco':'$'+Math.round(pr/7.5)}}],
['cosesc','Costo escalera',11,'fa-stairs',[['n','Huellas',''],['a','Ancho','m'],['c','Costo/m²','']],v=>({Area:(v.n*0.28*v.a).toFixed(2)+' m²',Costo:(v.n*0.28*v.a*v.c).toFixed(2)})],
['cosven','Costo ventana',11,'fa-window-maximize',[['a','Ancho','m'],['h','Alto','m'],['c','Costo/m²','']],v=>({Area:(v.a*v.h).toFixed(2)+' m²',Costo:(v.a*v.h*v.c).toFixed(2)})],
['cospint','Costo pintura',11,'fa-paint-roller',[['A','Area','m²'],['m','Manos',''],['r','Rend. m²/L',''],['p','Precio/L',''],['mo','MO/m²','']],v=>({Litros:(v.A*v.m/v.r).toFixed(1),Pintura:(v.A*v.m/v.r*v.p).toFixed(2),MO:(v.A*v.mo).toFixed(2),TOTAL:(v.A*v.m/v.r*v.p+v.A*v.mo).toFixed(2)})],
['cosiva','Calculadora IVA',11,'fa-percent',[['s','Subtotal',''],['i','IVA %','']],v=>({IVA:(v.s*v.i/100).toFixed(2),Total:(v.s*(1+v.i/100)).toFixed(2)})],

['esconv','Conversion escala',12,'fa-maximize',[['r','Medida real','m'],['p','Medida plano','cm']],v=>({Escala:'1:'+Math.round(v.r*100/v.p),'1cm = ':(v.r*100/v.p).toFixed(2)+' m'})],

['terniv','Nivel promedio',13,'fa-mountain',[['p','Niveles (coma)','m',1]],v=>{const a=String(v.p).split(',').map(Number).filter(n=>!isNaN(n));return{Promedio:(a.reduce((s,b)=>s+b,0)/a.length).toFixed(3)+' m',Maximo:Math.max(...a).toFixed(3)+' m',Minimo:Math.min(...a).toFixed(3)+' m',Desnivel:(Math.max(...a)-Math.min(...a)).toFixed(3)+' m'}}],
['termt','Mov. tierras',13,'fa-truck-monster',[['A','Area','m²'],['ni','Nivel inicial','m'],['nf','Nivel final','m'],['h','Hincham. %','']],v=>{const d=v.nf-v.ni;return{Corte:d<0?(-v.A*d).toFixed(3)+' m³':'No',Relleno:d>0?(v.A*d).toFixed(3)+' m³':'No','Con hinch.':d>0?(v.A*d*(1+v.h/100)).toFixed(3)+' m³':'N/A'}}],

['tervp','Vol. prismoide',13,'fa-mound',[['A1','Area sec. 1','m²'],['A2','Area sec. 2','m²'],['Am','Area media','m²'],['L','Distancia','m']],v=>({Volumen:(v.L/6*(v.A1+4*v.Am+v.A2)).toFixed(4)+' m³'})],
['tercomp','Compactacion',13,'fa-compress',[['v2','Vol. suelto','m³'],['f','Factor %','']],v=>({Compactado:(v.v2*(1-v.f/100)).toFixed(3)+' m³',Original:(v.v2/(1+v.f/100)).toFixed(3)+' m³'})],

['terest','Nivel por estaca',13,'fa-map-pin',[['ni','Nivel inst.','m'],['le','Lectura mira','m']],v=>({'Nivel punto':(v.ni-v.le).toFixed(3)+' m'})],
['tercurv','Espaciam. curvas niv.',13,'fa-bezier-curve',[['eq','Equidist.','m'],['p','Pend. %','']],v=>({'Dist horiz':(v.eq/(v.p/100)).toFixed(2)+' m'})],

['acuabs','Absorcion total',14,'fa-ear-listen',[['a1','Area sup. 1','m²'],['n1','Coef. abs. 1',''],['a2','Area sup. 2','m²'],['n2','Coef. abs. 2','']],v=>({Total:(v.a1*v.n1+v.a2*v.n2).toFixed(3)+' sabines'})],
['acuniv','Nivel ruido',14,'fa-volume-low',[['W','Potencia acust.','W'],['r','Distancia','m']],v=>({Nivel:(120+10*Math.log10(Math.max(1e-12,v.W))-20*Math.log10(Math.max(0.01,v.r))-11).toFixed(1)+' dB'})],
['aculey','Ley de masas',14,'fa-shield-halved',[['m','Masa sup.','kg/m²'],['f','Frecuencia','Hz']],v=>({TL:(20*Math.log10(v.m*v.f)-47).toFixed(1)+' dB'})],
['acudist','Atenuac. distancia',14,'fa-arrows-left-right',[['L1','Nivel fuente','dB'],['d1','Dist. 1','m'],['d2','Dist. 2','m']],v=>({Nivel:(v.L1-20*Math.log10(v.d2/v.d1)).toFixed(1)+' dB',Atenuacion:(20*Math.log10(v.d2/v.d1)).toFixed(1)+' dB'})],
['acunc','Curva NC',14,'fa-chart-bar',[['nc','Valor NC (15-70)','']],v=>({'63Hz':v.nc+15,'125Hz':v.nc+10,'250Hz':v.nc+7,'500Hz':v.nc+4,'1000Hz':v.nc,'2000Hz':v.nc-3,'4000Hz':v.nc-6,'8000Hz':v.nc-10})],

['accramp','Rampa accesible',15,'fa-wheelchair',[['h','Desnivel','m'],['mx','Pend. max %','']],v=>({Longitud:(v.h/(v.mx/100)).toFixed(2)+' m',Escalones:Math.ceil(v.h/0.18),Cumple:v.mx<=8?'Si':'No (max 8%)'})],

['accpuer','Ancho puerta',15,'fa-door-open',[['a','Ancho','m']],v=>({Cumple:v.a>=0.9?'Si (min 0.90m)':'No (min 0.90m)',Ideal:v.a>=1?'Si (ideal 1.00m)':'No'})],

['solaz','Azimut solar',16,'fa-sun',[['la','Latitud','°'],['dec','Declinacion','°'],['hr','Hora solar','']],v=>{const h=(v.hr-12)*15*Math.PI/180,L=v.la*Math.PI/180,D=v.dec*Math.PI/180;const az=Math.atan2(-Math.cos(D)*Math.sin(h),Math.sin(D)*Math.cos(L)-Math.cos(D)*Math.sin(L)*Math.cos(h))*180/Math.PI;return{Azimut:((az+360)%360).toFixed(2)+'°'}}],
['solalt','Altura solar',16,'fa-arrow-up-long',[['la','Latitud','°'],['dec','Declinacion','°'],['hr','Hora solar','']],v=>{const h=(v.hr-12)*15*Math.PI/180;const alt=Math.asin(Math.sin(v.la*Math.PI/180)*Math.sin(v.dec*Math.PI/180)+Math.cos(v.la*Math.PI/180)*Math.cos(v.dec*Math.PI/180)*Math.cos(h))*180/Math.PI;return{Altura:alt.toFixed(2)+'°',Estado:alt>0?'Sol visible':'Bajo horizonte'}}],
['solsom','Long. sombra',16,'fa-square',[['h','Altura obj.','m'],['alt','Altura solar','°']],v=>v.alt>0?{Sombra:(v.h/Math.tan(v.alt*Math.PI/180)).toFixed(2)+' m'}:{Sombra:'Infinita (sin sol)'})],

['miscron','Estimativa tiempo obra',17,'fa-stopwatch',[['m2','m²',''],['r','Rend. m²/dia',''],['c','Cuadrillas','']],v=>({Dias:Math.ceil(v.m2/v.r/v.c),Semanas:(v.m2/v.r/v.c/6).toFixed(1),Meses:(v.m2/v.r/v.c/26).toFixed(2)})],
['misnum','Numeracion ambientes',17,'fa-list-ol',[['p','Plantas',''],['a','Amb./planta','']],v=>({Total:v.p*v.a,Formato:'101 al '+(v.p*100+v.a)})],
['misconc','Diseno mezcla concreto',17,'fa-flask-vial',[['fc','fc kg/cm²',''],['sl','Slump cm','']],v=>{const ag=180+v.sl*5,wc=v.fc<=210?0.5:0.45;return{Agua:ag+' L/m³',Cemento:Math.ceil(ag/wc/50)+' sacos',Arena:Math.round(ag/wc*2.2)+' kg aprox',Grava:Math.round(ag/wc*3)+' kg aprox'}}],
['misplan','Plantilla catalogo',17,'fa-file-lines',[['n','Piezas',''],['an','Ancho cm',''],['al','Alto cm',''],['j','Junta cm','']],v=>{const cols=Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(v.n)),rows=Math.ceil(v.n/cols);return{Ancho:((v.an+v.j)*cols).toFixed(1)+' cm',Alto:((v.al+v.j)*rows).toFixed(1)+' cm',Columnas:cols,Filas:rows}}],
['mischeck','Checklist obra',17,'fa-clipboard-check',[['f','1:ciment 2:estruc 3:muros 4:acab 5:instal','']],v=>{const l={1:['Trazo','Excavacion','Plantilla','Acero','Cimbra','Concreto','Curado','Relleno'],2:['Columnas','Trabes','Losa','Descimbra'],3:['Muros','Castillos','Dalas','Cadenas'],4:['Aplanados','Pintura','Pisos','Azulejos','Canceleria'],5:['Electrico','Hidraulico','Sanitario','Gas','Aire acond.']};const arr=l[Math.round(v.f)]||[];const r={};arr.forEach(i=>r[i]='☐');return r}],
['mispen','Pendiente azotea',17,'fa-house',[['L','Longitud','m'],['p','Pendiente %','']],v=>({Desnivel:(v.L*v.p/100).toFixed(3)+' m','Bajadas min':Math.ceil(v.L/10)})],
['mishue','Distribucion piso',17,'fa-table-cells-large',[['A','Area','m²'],['pz','Area pieza','m²'],['sep','Separacion mm','']],v=>({Piezas:Math.ceil(v.A/v.pz),ConJunta:Math.ceil(v.A/(v.pz+Math.pow(v.sep/1000,2)))})],

];
