/* ===== MOTOR CAD ===== */
class CAD{
constructor(c){
  this.c=c;this.x=c.getContext('2d');
  this.objs=[];this.layers=[{id:0,name:'Capa 0',vis:true,col:'#EAEAEA',lock:false}];this.aLayer=0;
  this.tool='select';this.zoom=1;this.px=0;this.py=0;this.gs=20;this.showGrid=true;this.snap=true;
  this.sCol='#FF6B2B';this.fCol='#FF6B2B';this.fillEnabled=false;this.fillColor='#FF6B2B';
  this.lineWidth=2;this.wallThickness=15;this.fontSize=16;this.textContent='Texto';
  this.gridSize=20;
  this.undoS=[];this.redoS=[];this.selIdx=-1;
  this.drawing=false;this.panning=false;this.pinching=false;
  this.sp=null;this.cp=null;this.polyPts=[];this.freePts=[];this.cursor=null;this.dragLast=null;
  this.td=0;this.td2=0;this.tc=null;
  this.dirty=true;this.dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
  this.resize();this.events();
}
resize(){
  const w=this.c.parentElement,h=w.clientWidth,v=w.clientHeight;
  this.c.width=h*this.dpr;this.c.height=v*this.dpr;
  this.c.style.width=h+'px';this.c.style.height=v+'px';
  this.x.setTransform(this.dpr,0,0,this.dpr,0,0);
  this.W=h;this.H=v;this.dirty=true;this.render();
}
get gridSize(){return this.gs} set gridSize(g){this.gs=g}
get snapEnabled(){return this.snap} set snapEnabled(s){this.snap=s}
s2w(sx,sy){return{x:(sx-this.px)/this.zoom,y:(sy-this.py)/this.zoom}}
w2s(wx,wy){return{x:wx*this.zoom+this.px,y:wy*this.zoom+this.py}}
sn(x,y){if(!this.snap)return{x,y};const g=this.gs;return{x:Math.round(x/g)*g,y:Math.round(y/g)*g}}
mp(e){const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();return{x:e.clientX-r.left,y:e.clientY-r.top}}

events(){
  const c=this.c;
  c.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>this.tStart(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>this.tMove(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('touchend',e=>this.tEnd(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>this.mDown(e));
  window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>this.mMove(e));
  window.addEventListener('mouseup',e=>this.mUp(e));
  c.addEventListener('wheel',e=>this.whl(e),{passive:false});
  c.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>this.resize());
  let lastTap=0;
  c.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-lastTap<300&&this.tool==='polyline')this.closePolyline();
    lastTap=now;
  });
  window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(e.target.tagName==='INPUT')return;
    if((e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'))this.deleteSelected();
    if(e.ctrlKey&&e.key==='z')this.undo();
    if(e.ctrlKey&&(e.key==='y'||e.key==='Z'))this.redo();
    if(e.key==='Escape'&&this.tool==='polyline')this.finishPolyline();
  });
}
tStart(e){
  e.preventDefault();this.td=e.touches.length;
  if(this.td===2){
    this.pinching=true;this.drawing=false;this.freePts=[];
    const t=e.touches;
    this.td2=Math.hypot(t[1].clientX-t[0].clientX,t[1].clientY-t[0].clientY);
    this.tc={x:(t[0].clientX+t[1].clientX)/2,y:(t[0].clientY+t[1].clientY)/2};
  }else if(this.td===1){
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.hStart({x:e.touches[0].clientX-r.left,y:e.touches[0].clientY-r.top});
  }
}
tMove(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  if(this.pinching&&e.touches.length===2){
    const t=e.touches,d=Math.hypot(t[1].clientX-t[0].clientX,t[1].clientY-t[0].clientY);
    const cn={x:(t[0].clientX+t[1].clientX)/2,y:(t[0].clientY+t[1].clientY)/2};
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cx=cn.x-r.left,cy=cn.y-r.top,lx=this.tc.x-r.left,ly=this.tc.y-r.top;
    const nz=Math.max(.1,Math.min(20,this.zoom*d/this.td2));
    const wb=this.s2w(cx,cy);
    this.zoom=nz;
    const wa=this.w2s(wb.x,wb.y);this.px+=cx-wa.x;this.py+=cy-wa.y;
    this.px+=cx-lx;this.py+=cy-ly;
    this.td2=d;this.tc=cn;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom();
  }else if(this.td===1&&e.touches.length===1){
    const r=this.c.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.hMove({x:e.touches[0].clientX-r.left,y:e.touches[0].clientY-r.top});
  }
}
tEnd(e){
  e.preventDefault();
  if(e.touches.length<2)this.pinching=false;
  if(e.touches.length===0){this.hEnd();this.td=0}else this.td=e.touches.length;
}
mDown(e){
  if(e.button===1||e.button===2){this.panning=true;this.sp=this.mp(e);return}
  this.hStart(this.mp(e));
}
mMove(e){
  if(this.panning){const p=this.mp(e);this.px+=p.x-this.sp.x;this.py+=p.y-this.sp.y;this.sp=p;this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(e.target===this.c)this.hMove(this.mp(e));
}
mUp(){if(this.panning){this.panning=false;return}this.hEnd()}
whl(e){
  e.preventDefault();const p=this.mp(e),wb=this.s2w(p.x,p.y);
  this.zoom*=e.deltaY<0?1.1:.9;this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(20,this.zoom));
  const wa=this.w2s(wb.x,wb.y);this.px+=p.x-wa.x;this.py+=p.y-wa.y;
  this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom();
}

hStart(pos){
  const w=this.s2w(pos.x,pos.y),s=this.sn(w.x,w.y);
  if(this.tool==='select'){
    this.selIdx=this.hit(w.x,w.y);
    this.drawing=this.selIdx>=0;this.dragLast=w;
    this.dirty=true;this.render();return;
  }
  if(this.tool==='move'){this.panning=true;this.sp=pos;return}
  if(this.tool==='erase'){const i=this.hit(w.x,w.y);if(i>=0){this.saveU();this.objs.splice(i,1);this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Eliminado','success')}return}
  if(this.tool==='polyline'){this.polyPts.push(s);this.cursor=s;this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(this.tool==='text'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'text',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x,y:s.y,content:this.textContent,size:this.fontSize,color:this.sCol});this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(this.tool==='door'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'door',layer:this.aLayer,x:s.x,y:s.y,width:80,color:this.sCol});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Puerta insertada','success');return}
  if(this.tool==='window'){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'window',layer:this.aLayer,x1:s.x-60,y1:s.y,x2:s.x+60,y2:s.y,width:60,color:this.sCol});this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Ventana insertada','success');return}
  if(this.tool==='furniture'||this.tool==='symbol')return;
  this.drawing=true;this.sp=s;this.cp=s;
  if(this.tool==='freehand')this.freePts=[s];
}
hMove(pos){
  const w=this.s2w(pos.x,pos.y),s=this.sn(w.x,w.y);this.uCoord(w.x,w.y);
  this.cursor=s;
  if(this.tool==='polyline'){this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(this.tool==='select'&&this.drawing&&this.selIdx>=0&&this.dragLast){
    const dx=w.x-this.dragLast.x,dy=w.y-this.dragLast.y;
    if(dx||dy){this.mvObj(this.objs[this.selIdx],dx,dy);this.dragLast=w;this.dirty=true;this.render()}return;
  }
  if(this.tool==='move'&&this.panning){this.px+=pos.x-this.sp.x;this.py+=pos.y-this.sp.y;this.sp=pos;this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  if(!this.drawing)return;this.cp=s;
  if(this.tool==='freehand')this.freePts.push(s);
  this.dirty=true;this.render();
}
hEnd(){
  if(this.tool==='move'){this.panning=false;return}
  if(this.tool==='select'){this.drawing=false;this.dragLast=null;return}
  if(!this.drawing||!this.sp||!this.cp)return;this.drawing=false;
  const s=this.sp,c=this.cp;
  if(Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y)<2){this.dirty=true;this.render();return}
  this.saveU();const b={layer:this.aLayer,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lineWidth};
  switch(this.tool){
    case'line':this.objs.push({...b,type:'line',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y});break;
    case'rect':{const x=Math.min(s.x,c.x),y=Math.min(s.y,c.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'rect',x,y,w:Math.abs(c.x-s.x),h:Math.abs(c.y-s.y),fill:this.fillEnabled?this.fillColor:null});break}
    case'circle':{const r=Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'circle',cx:s.x,cy:s.y,r,fill:this.fillEnabled?this.fillColor:null});break}
    case'arc':{const r=Math.hypot(c.x-s.x,c.y-s.y);this.objs.push({...b,type:'arc',cx:s.x,cy:s.y,r,startAngle:0,endAngle:Math.atan2(c.y-s.y,c.x-s.x)||Math.PI});break}
    case'wall':this.objs.push({...b,type:'wall',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y,thickness:this.wallThickness});break;
    case'dimension':this.objs.push({...b,type:'dimension',x1:s.x,y1:s.y,x2:c.x,y2:c.y,offset:30});break;
    case'freehand':if(this.freePts.length>2)this.objs.push({...b,type:'freehand',points:[...this.freePts]});this.freePts=[];break;
  }
  this.dirty=true;this.render();
}

mvObj(o,dx,dy){
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':o.x1+=dx;o.y1+=dy;o.x2+=dx;o.y2+=dy;break;
    case'rect':case'furniture':case'symbol':o.x+=dx;o.y+=dy;break;
    case'circle':case'arc':o.cx+=dx;o.cy+=dy;break;
    case'text':case'door':o.x+=dx;o.y+=dy;break;
    case'freehand':case'polyline':o.points.forEach(p=>{p.x+=dx;p.y+=dy});break;
  }
}

hit(wx,wy){
  for(let i=this.objs.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    if(!this.layers[this.objs[i].layer]||!this.layers[this.objs[i].layer].vis)continue;
    if(this.hitO(this.objs[i],wx,wy))return i;
  }return-1;
}
hitO(o,wx,wy){
  const t=8/this.zoom;
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':return this.dSeg(wx,wy,o.x1,o.y1,o.x2,o.y2)<t+(o.thickness||0)/2;
    case'rect':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.w+t&&wy>=o.y-t&&wy<=o.y+o.h+t;
    case'circle':return Math.abs(Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy)-o.r)<t||Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy)<o.r;
    case'arc':{const d=Math.hypot(wx-o.cx,wy-o.cy);if(Math.abs(d-o.r)>t)return false;let a=Math.atan2(wy-o.cy,wx-o.cx);if(a<0)a+=Math.PI*2;let s=o.startAngle,e=o.endAngle;if(e<s)e+=Math.PI*2;if(a<s)a+=Math.PI*2;return a>=s&&a<=e}
    case'text':{this.x.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';const tw=this.x.measureText(o.content).width;return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+tw+t&&wy>=o.y-o.size-t&&wy<=o.y+t}
    case'door':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.width+t&&wy>=o.y-t&&wy<=o.y+o.width+t;
    case'freehand':case'polyline':for(let j=1;j<o.points.length;j++)if(this.dSeg(wx,wy,o.points[j-1].x,o.points[j-1].y,o.points[j].x,o.points[j].y)<t)return true;return false;
    case'furniture':case'symbol':return wx>=o.x-t&&wx<=o.x+o.w+t&&wy>=o.y-t&&wy<=o.y+o.h+t;
  }return false;
}
dSeg(px,py,x1,y1,x2,y2){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,l2=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(l2===0)return Math.hypot(px-x1,py-y1);let t=((px-x1)*dx+(py-y1)*dy)/l2;t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));return Math.hypot(px-(x1+t*dx),py-(y1+t*dy))}

/* --- RENDER --- */
render(){
  if(!this.dirty)return;this.dirty=false;
  const c=this.x;c.save();c.clearRect(0,0,this.W,this.H);
  c.fillStyle='#0C0C0C';c.fillRect(0,0,this.W,this.H);
  if(this.showGrid)this.drawGrid();
  for(let i=0;i<this.objs.length;i++){const o=this.objs[i];if(!this.layers[o.layer]||!this.layers[o.layer].vis)continue;this.drawObj(o,i===this.selIdx)}
  if(this.drawing&&this.sp&&this.cp)this.drawPrev();
  if(this.tool==='polyline'&&this.polyPts.length>0){
    c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
    c.strokeStyle='#FF6B2B';c.lineWidth=1.5/this.zoom;
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(this.polyPts[0].x,this.polyPts[0].y);
    for(let i=1;i<this.polyPts.length;i++)c.lineTo(this.polyPts[i].x,this.polyPts[i].y);
    if(this.cursor)c.lineTo(this.cursor.x,this.cursor.y);
    c.setLineDash([5/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);
    c.fillStyle='#FF6B2B';this.polyPts.forEach(p=>{c.beginPath();c.arc(p.x,p.y,3/this.zoom,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill()});
    c.restore();
  }
  const o0=this.w2s(0,0);
  if(o0.x>-20&&o0.x<this.W+20&&o0.y>-20&&o0.y<this.H+20){
    c.strokeStyle='rgba(255,107,43,.12)';c.lineWidth=1;
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o0.x,0);c.lineTo(o0.x,this.H);c.stroke();
    c.beginPath();c.moveTo(0,o0.y);c.lineTo(this.W,o0.y);c.stroke();
  }
  c.restore();
}
drawGrid(){
  const c=this.x,g=this.gs*this.zoom;if(g<5)return;
  const ox=((this.px%g)+g)%g,oy=((this.py%g)+g)%g;
  c.strokeStyle=g<15?'#161616':'#1E1E1E';c.lineWidth=.5;c.beginPath();
  for(let x=ox;x<this.W;x+=g){c.moveTo(x,0);c.lineTo(x,this.H)}
  for(let y=oy;y<this.H;y+=g){c.moveTo(0,y);c.lineTo(this.W,y)}
  c.stroke();
}
drawObj(o,sel){
  const c=this.x;c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
  c.strokeStyle=sel?'#FFFFFF':o.color;c.fillStyle=o.fill||'transparent';
  c.lineWidth=Math.max(0.3,(o.lineWidth||2))/this.zoom;c.lineCap='round';c.lineJoin='round';
  if(sel){c.shadowColor=o.color;c.shadowBlur=8/this.zoom}
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1,o.y1);c.lineTo(o.x2,o.y2);c.stroke();break;
    case'rect':if(o.fill){c.fillStyle=o.fill+'44';c.fillRect(o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h)}c.strokeRect(o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h);break;
    case'circle':c.beginPath();c.arc(o.cx,o.cy,Math.max(.1,o.r),0,Math.PI*2);if(o.fill){c.fillStyle=o.fill+'44';c.fill()}c.stroke();break;
    case'arc':c.beginPath();c.arc(o.cx,o.cy,Math.max(.1,o.r),o.startAngle,o.endAngle);c.stroke();break;
    case'wall':{const dx=o.x2-o.x1,dy=o.y2-o.y1,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(l<.1)break;const nx=-dy/l*o.thickness/2,ny=dx/l*o.thickness/2;c.fillStyle=(sel?'#FFFFFF':o.color)+'2E';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1+nx,o.y1+ny);c.lineTo(o.x2+nx,o.y2+ny);c.lineTo(o.x2-nx,o.y2-ny);c.lineTo(o.x1-nx,o.y1-ny);c.closePath();c.fill();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1+nx,o.y1+ny);c.lineTo(o.x2+nx,o.y2+ny);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1-nx,o.y1-ny);c.lineTo(o.x2-nx,o.y2-ny);c.stroke();break}
    case'door':{const w=o.width,hh=Math.max(8,w*0.12);c.strokeRect(o.x,o.y,w,hh);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x,o.y+hh);c.lineTo(o.x,o.y+hh+w);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.arc(o.x,o.y+hh,w,-Math.PI/2,0);c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x,o.y+hh);c.lineTo(o.x+w,o.y+hh);c.stroke();break}
    case'window':{const l=Math.hypot(o.x2-o.x1,o.y2-o.y1);if(l<.1)break;const ux=(o.x2-o.x1)/l,uy=(o.y2-o.y1)/l,nx=-uy*5,ny=ux*5;c.lineWidth=Math.max(0.3,2/this.zoom);for(const s of[-1,1]){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1+nx*s,o.y1+ny*s);c.lineTo(o.x2+nx*s,o.y2+ny*s);c.stroke()}c.lineWidth=Math.max(0.3,1/this.zoom);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1,o.y1);c.lineTo(o.x2,o.y2);c.stroke();break}
    case'dimension':{const dx=o.x2-o.x1,dy=o.y2-o.y1,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(l<.1)break;const nx=-dy/l*o.offset,ny=dx/l*o.offset;const ax=o.x1+nx,ay=o.y1+ny,bx=o.x2+nx,by=o.y2+ny;
      c.beginPath();c.moveTo(ax,ay);c.lineTo(bx,by);c.stroke();
      const as=6/this.zoom,ang=Math.atan2(by-ay,bx-ax);
      [[ax,ay,0],[bx,by,Math.PI]].forEach(([px2,py2,extra])=>{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(px2,py2);c.lineTo(px2+Math.cos(ang+.4+extra)*as*1.6,py2+Math.sin(ang+.4+extra)*as*1.6);c.moveTo(px2,py2);c.lineTo(px2+Math.cos(ang-.4+extra)*as*1.6,py2+Math.sin(ang-.4+extra)*as*1.6);c.stroke()});
      c.setLineDash([3/this.zoom,3/this.zoom]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.x1,o.y1);c.lineTo(ax,ay);c.moveTo(o.x2,o.y2);c.lineTo(bx,by);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);
      const fs=12/this.zoom;c.font='500 '+fs+'px "JetBrains Mono"';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='bottom';
      const txt=l.toFixed(1),tw=c.measureText(txt).width,mx2=(ax+bx)/2,my2=(ay+by)/2;
      c.fillStyle='#0C0C0C';c.fillRect(mx2-tw/2-3/this.zoom,my2-fs-2/this.zoom,tw+6/this.zoom,fs+4/this.zoom);
      c.fillStyle=sel?'#FFF':o.color;c.fillText(txt,mx2,my2-2/this.zoom);break}
    case'text':c.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.fillStyle=sel?'#FFF':o.color;c.textAlign='left';c.textBaseline='bottom';c.fillText(o.content,o.x,o.y);break;
    case'freehand':if(o.points.length<2)break;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.points[0].x,o.points[0].y);for(let i=1;i<o.points.length;i++)c.lineTo(o.points[i].x,o.points[i].y);c.stroke();break;
    case'polyline':if(o.points.length<2)break;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(o.points[0].x,o.points[0].y);for(let i=1;i<o.points.length;i++)c.lineTo(o.points[i].x,o.points[i].y);if(o.closed)c.closePath();if(o.fill){c.fillStyle=o.fill+'33';c.fill()}c.stroke();break;
    case'furniture':this.drawFurn(o);break;
    case'symbol':this.drawSym(o);break;
  }
  if(sel){c.shadowBlur=0;c.strokeStyle='#FFF';c.lineWidth=1/this.zoom;c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);const bb=this.bbox(o);c.strokeRect(bb.x-5/this.zoom,bb.y-5/this.zoom,bb.w+10/this.zoom,bb.h+10/this.zoom);c.setLineDash([]);c.fillStyle='#FFF';const hs=3/this.zoom;[[bb.x,bb.y],[bb.x+bb.w,bb.y],[bb.x,bb.y+bb.h],[bb.x+bb.w,bb.y+bb.h]].forEach(pt=>{c.fillRect(pt[0]-hs,pt[1]-hs,hs*2,hs*2)})}
  c.restore();
}
/* Dibujo vectorial de mobiliario (cm reales) */
drawFurn(o){
  const c=this.x,x=o.x,y=o.y,w=o.w,h=o.h;
  c.lineWidth=Math.max(0.3,1.2/this.zoom);
  switch(o.kind){
    case'cama':case'cama2':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.strokeRect(x+3,y+3,w-6,h*0.14);const pw=(w-14)/(o.kind==='cama'?1:2);for(let i=0;i<(o.kind==='cama'?1:2);i++)c.strokeRect(x+5+i*(pw+4),y+6,pw,h*0.1);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h*0.3);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h*0.3);c.stroke();break}
    case'sofa':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.strokeRect(x+w*0.06,y+h*0.22,w*0.88,h*0.66);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w/3,y+h*0.22);c.lineTo(x+w/3,y+h*0.88);c.moveTo(x+2*w/3,y+h*0.22);c.lineTo(x+2*w/3,y+h*0.88);c.stroke();break}
    case'sillon':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.strokeRect(x+w*0.14,y+h*0.2,w*0.72,h*0.65);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*0.14,y+h*0.55);c.lineTo(x+w*0.86,y+h*0.55);c.stroke();break}
    case'mesa':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w/2,y);c.lineTo(x+w/2,y+h);c.setLineDash([3/this.zoom,3/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);break}
    case'mesac':c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.strokeRect(x+5,y+5,w-10,h-10);break;
    case'silla':{c.strokeRect(x+w*0.15,y,w*0.7,h*0.75);c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w/2,y+h*0.37,w*0.22,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'escr':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w*0.35,y+h+15,12,Math.PI,0);c.stroke();break}
    case'tv':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*0.1,y+h/2);c.lineTo(x+w*0.9,y+h/2);c.stroke();break}
    case'refri':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h*0.35);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h*0.35);c.stroke();break}
    case'estufa':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);const r=Math.min(w,h)*0.14;[[.28,.28],[.72,.28],[.28,.72],[.72,.72]].forEach(p=>{c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w*p[0],y+h*p[1],r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke()});break}
    case'lavadora':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w/2,y+h/2,Math.min(w,h)*0.3,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'freg':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.strokeRect(x+w*0.1,y+h*0.15,w*0.36,h*0.7);c.strokeRect(x+w*0.54,y+h*0.15,w*0.36,h*0.7);break}
    case'wc':{c.strokeRect(x,y+h*0.55,w,h*0.28);c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x+w/2,y+h*0.28,w*0.42,h*0.28,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'lavabo':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x+w/2,y+h/2,w*0.35,h*0.32,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'tina':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x+w/2,y+h/2,w*0.44,h*0.36,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'ducha':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h);c.lineTo(x+w,y);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w*0.25,y+h*0.25,w*0.08,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'closet':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w/2,y);c.lineTo(x+w/2,y+h);c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+w/2,y+h);c.moveTo(x+w/2,y);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h);c.stroke();break}
    case'estante':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);for(let i=1;i<3;i++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*i/3,y);c.lineTo(x+w*i/3,y+h);c.stroke()}break}
    case'mesan':c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);break;
    case'cuna':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);for(let i=1;i<5;i++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*i/5,y);c.lineTo(x+w*i/5,y+h);c.stroke()}break}
    case'banco':c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);break;
    case'planta':{c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w/2,y+h/2,Math.min(w,h)/2,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w/2,y+h/2);c.lineTo(x+w*0.8,y+h*0.2);c.moveTo(x+w/2,y+h/2);c.lineTo(x+w*0.2,y+h*0.25);c.moveTo(x+w/2,y+h/2);c.lineTo(x+w*0.5,y+h*0.85);c.stroke();break}
    case'arbol':{c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w/2,y+h/2,Math.min(w,h)/2,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.arc(x+w/2,y+h/2,Math.min(w,h)*0.3,0.5,5.5);c.stroke();break}
    default:c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);
  }
}
/* Dibujo vectorial de simbolos */
drawSym(o){
  const c=this.x,x=o.x,y=o.y,w=o.w,h=o.h,cx=x+w/2,cy=y+h/2,r=Math.min(w,h)/2;
  c.lineWidth=Math.max(0.3,1.2/this.zoom);
  switch(o.kind){
    case'toma':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx-r*0.4,cy-r*0.35);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.4,cy-r*0.35);c.moveTo(cx-r*0.4,cy+r*0.35);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.4,cy+r*0.35);c.stroke();break}
    case'interr':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx-r*0.6,cy+r*0.6);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.6,cy-r*0.6);c.stroke();break}
    case'foco':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx-r*0.6,cy-r*0.6);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.6,cy+r*0.6);c.moveTo(cx+r*0.6,cy-r*0.6);c.lineTo(cx-r*0.6,cy+r*0.6);c.stroke();break}
    case'apliq':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,Math.PI,0);c.closePath();c.stroke();break}
    case'tablero':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h);c.stroke();break}
    case'medidor':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r*0.5,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'agua':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,cy-r*0.5);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.45,cy+r*0.35);c.lineTo(cx-r*0.45,cy+r*0.35);c.closePath();c.stroke();break}
    case'dren':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,cy);c.lineTo(x+w,cy);c.stroke();break}
    case'reg':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r*0.3,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'llave':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r*0.6,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx-r,cy);c.lineTo(cx+r,cy);c.stroke();break}
    case'ac':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);for(let i=1;i<4;i++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*i/4,y+h*0.25);c.lineTo(x+w*i/4,y+h*0.75);c.stroke()}break}
    case'vent':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=i*2.09;c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,cy);c.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(a)*r*0.8,cy+Math.sin(a)*r*0.8);c.stroke()}break}
    case'ext':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,y+h*0.2);c.lineTo(cx,y+h*0.4);c.stroke();break}
    case'cam':{c.strokeRect(x,y+h*0.25,w*0.6,h*0.5);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*0.6,cy);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h*0.2);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h*0.8);c.closePath();c.stroke();break}
    case'norte':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,cy-r*0.8);c.lineTo(cx+r*0.3,cy+r*0.6);c.lineTo(cx,cy+r*0.25);c.lineTo(cx-r*0.3,cy+r*0.6);c.closePath();c.stroke();c.font=(r*0.6)+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='bottom';c.fillStyle=c.strokeStyle;c.fillText('N',cx,y-r-2/this.zoom);break}
    case'nivel':{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(cx,y+h*0.15);c.lineTo(x+w*0.85,cy+h*0.2);c.lineTo(x+w*0.15,cy+h*0.2);c.closePath();c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,cy+h*0.2);c.lineTo(x+w,cy+h*0.2);c.stroke();break}
    case'corte':{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,cy);c.lineTo(x+w,cy);c.setLineDash([6/this.zoom,3/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);[[x,1],[x+w,-1]].forEach(pt=>{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(pt[0],cy-6/this.zoom*2);c.lineTo(pt[0]+pt[1]*7,cy);c.lineTo(pt[0],cy+6/this.zoom*2);c.stroke()});break}
    case'eje':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.setLineDash([4/this.zoom,3/this.zoom]);c.stroke();c.setLineDash([]);break}
    case'det':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,cy);c.lineTo(x+w,cy);c.moveTo(cx,y);c.lineTo(cx,y+h);c.stroke();break}
    case'salida':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w*0.5,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+w*0.55,cy);c.lineTo(x+w,cy);c.moveTo(x+w*0.8,cy-4);c.lineTo(x+w,cy);c.lineTo(x+w*0.8,cy+4);c.stroke();break}
    case'asc':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h);c.moveTo(x+w,y);c.lineTo(x,y+h);c.stroke();break}
    case'rampa':{c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h);c.lineTo(x+w,y);c.closePath();c.stroke();break}
    case'escsym':{c.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);for(let i=1;i<6;i++){c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h*i/6);c.lineTo(x+w,y+h*i/6);c.stroke()}c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y+h);c.lineTo(x+w,y);c.stroke();break}
    case'gas':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.font=r+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillStyle=c.strokeStyle;c.fillText('G',cx,cy);break}
    case'tel':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.font=r+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillStyle=c.strokeStyle;c.fillText('T',cx,cy);break}
    case'dat':{c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();c.font=r+'px "Space Grotesk"';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillStyle=c.strokeStyle;c.fillText('D',cx,cy);break}
    default:c.beginPath();c.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();
  }
}
bbox(o){
  switch(o.type){
    case'line':case'wall':case'dimension':case'window':return{x:Math.min(o.x1,o.x2),y:Math.min(o.y1,o.y2),w:Math.abs(o.x2-o.x1)||1,h:Math.abs(o.y2-o.y1)||1};
    case'rect':case'furniture':case'symbol':return{x:o.x,y:o.y,w:o.w,h:o.h};
    case'circle':case'arc':return{x:o.cx-o.r,y:o.cy-o.r,w:o.r*2,h:o.r*2};
    case'text':{this.x.font=o.size+'px "Space Grotesk"';return{x:o.x,y:o.y-o.size,w:this.x.measureText(o.content).width||10,h:o.size}}
    case'door':return{x:o.x,y:o.y,w:o.width,h:o.width*1.1};
    case'freehand':case'polyline':{let mx=Infinity,my=Infinity,Mx=-Infinity,My=-Infinity;o.points.forEach(p=>{mx=Math.min(mx,p.x);my=Math.min(my,p.y);Mx=Math.max(Mx,p.x);My=Math.max(My,p.y)});return{x:mx,y:my,w:(Mx-mx)||1,h:(My-my)||1}}
    default:return{x:0,y:0,w:10,h:10};
  }
}
drawPrev(){
  const c=this.x,s=this.sp,p=this.cp;
  c.save();c.translate(this.px,this.py);c.scale(this.zoom,this.zoom);
  c.strokeStyle=this.sCol+'AA';c.lineWidth=this.lineWidth/this.zoom;c.setLineDash([6/this.zoom,4/this.zoom]);c.lineCap='round';
  switch(this.tool){
    case'line':case'wall':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(s.x,s.y);c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);c.stroke();break;
    case'rect':{const x=Math.min(s.x,p.x),y=Math.min(s.y,p.y);c.strokeRect(x,y,Math.abs(p.x-s.x),Math.abs(p.y-s.y));break}
    case'circle':{const r=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);c.beginPath();c.arc(s.x,s.y,Math.max(.1,r),0,Math.PI*2);c.stroke();break}
    case'arc':{const r=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);const a=Math.atan2(p.y-s.y,p.x-s.x)||Math.PI;c.beginPath();c.arc(s.x,s.y,Math.max(.1,r),0,a);c.stroke();break}
    case'dimension':c.beginPath();c.moveTo(s.x,s.y);c.lineTo(p.x,p.y);c.stroke();break;
    case'freehand':if(this.freePts.length>1){c.setLineDash([]);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(this.freePts[0].x,this.freePts[0].y);for(let i=1;i<this.freePts.length;i++)c.lineTo(this.freePts[i].x,this.freePts[i].y);c.stroke()}break;
  }
  c.setLineDash([]);const d=Math.hypot(p.x-s.x,p.y-s.y);
  if(d>5){const fs=11/this.zoom;c.font='500 '+fs+'px "JetBrains Mono"';c.fillStyle='#FF6B2B';c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='bottom';c.fillText(d.toFixed(1)+' cm',(s.x+p.x)/2,(s.y+p.y)/2-8/this.zoom)}
  c.restore();
}

/* Acciones */
saveU(){this.undoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));if(this.undoS.length>50)this.undoS.shift();this.redoS=[]}
undo(){if(!this.undoS.length)return;this.redoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));this.objs=JSON.parse(this.undoS.pop());this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Deshacer','info')}
redo(){if(!this.redoS.length)return;this.undoS.push(JSON.stringify(this.objs));this.objs=JSON.parse(this.redoS.pop());this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Rehacer','info')}
zoomIn(){const wb=this.s2w(this.W/2,this.H/2);this.zoom=Math.min(20,this.zoom*1.3);const wa=this.w2s(wb.x,wb.y);this.px+=this.W/2-wa.x;this.py+=this.H/2-wa.y;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
zoomOut(){const wb=this.s2w(this.W/2,this.H/2);this.zoom=Math.max(.1,this.zoom/1.3);const wa=this.w2s(wb.x,wb.y);this.px+=this.W/2-wa.x;this.py+=this.H/2-wa.y;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
resetView(){this.zoom=1;this.px=this.W/2;this.py=this.H/2;this.dirty=true;this.render();this.uZoom()}
clearAll(){if(!this.objs.length)return;this.saveU();this.objs=[];this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Lienzo limpio','success')}
deleteSelected(){if(this.selIdx<0)return;this.saveU();this.objs.splice(this.selIdx,1);this.selIdx=-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Eliminado','success')}
duplicateSelected(){if(this.selIdx<0)return;this.saveU();const cp=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.objs[this.selIdx]));this.mvObj(cp,20,20);this.objs.push(cp);this.selIdx=this.objs.length-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast('Duplicado','success')}
finishPolyline(){if(this.polyPts.length>1){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'polyline',layer:this.aLayer,points:[...this.polyPts],color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lineWidth,closed:false});toast('Polilinea ('+this.polyPts.length+' pts)','success')}this.polyPts=[];this.dirty=true;this.render()}
closePolyline(){if(this.polyPts.length>2){this.saveU();this.objs.push({type:'polyline',layer:this.aLayer,points:[...this.polyPts],color:this.sCol,lineWidth:this.lineWidth,closed:true});toast('Poligono cerrado','success')}this.polyPts=[];this.dirty=true;this.render()}
insertFurn(kind,name,w,h){this.saveU();const s=this.s2w(this.W/2,this.H/2);const snp=this.sn(s.x,s.y);this.objs.push({type:'furniture',kind,layer:this.aLayer,x:snp.x-w/2,y:snp.y-h/2,w,h,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:1});this.selIdx=this.objs.length-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast(name+' insertado','success');closeM('furn-modal')}
insertSym(kind,name,w,h){this.saveU();const s=this.s2w(this.W/2,this.H/2);const snp=this.sn(s.x,s.y);this.objs.push({type:'symbol',kind,layer:this.aLayer,x:snp.x-w/2,y:snp.y-h/2,w,h,color:this.sCol,lineWidth:1});this.selIdx=this.objs.length-1;this.dirty=true;this.render();toast(name+' insertado','success');closeM('sym-modal')}
uCoord(x,y){document.getElementById('coords').textContent='X: '+x.toFixed(1)+' Y: '+y.toFixed(1)}
uZoom(){document.getElementById('zoom-ind').textContent=Math.round(this.zoom*100)+'%'}
setTool(t){
  if(this.tool==='polyline'&&t!=='polyline')this.finishPolyline();
  this.tool=t;this.selIdx=-1;this.panning=false;this.dirty=true;this.render();
  document.querySelectorAll('#toolstrip button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.tool===t));
  const hints={select:'Toca para seleccionar / arrastra para mover',move:'Arrastra para mover vista',line:'Arrastra para trazar linea',rect:'Arrastra para rectangulo',circle:'Centro y arrastra radio',arc:'Centro y arrastra arco',polyline:'Toca puntos · doble tap = cerrar',freehand:'Dibuja libre con el dedo',wall:'Arrastra para trazar muro',door:'Toca para insertar puerta',window:'Toca para insertar ventana',dimension:'Arrastra para acotar',text:'Toca para colocar texto',furniture:'Selecciona del catalogo',symbol:'Selecciona del catalogo',erase:'Toca un objeto para borrarlo'};
  document.getElementById('tool-hint').textContent=hints[t]||t;
}
}

/* ===== VARIABLES GLOBALES ===== */
let cad=null;

/* ===== TOAST ===== */
function toast(msg,type){
  type=type||'info';
  const c=document.getElementById('toast-c'),t=document.createElement('div');
  t.className='toast '+type;
  t.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-'+(type==='success'?'check-circle':type==='error'?'times-circle':'info-circle')+'"></i>'+msg;
  c.appendChild(t);setTimeout(()=>{t.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>t.remove(),300)},2200);
}

/* ===== MODALES ===== */
function openM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open')}
function closeM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open')}

/* ===== PANELES ===== */
let activePanel=null;
function togglePanel(id){
  const p=document.getElementById('panel-'+id);
  if(activePanel===id){p.classList.remove('open');activePanel=null;document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));return}
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(pp=>pp.classList.remove('open'));
  p.classList.add('open');activePanel=id;
  document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.panel===id));
}

/* ===== CAPAS ===== */
function renderLayers(){
  const c=document.getElementById('layers-list');c.innerHTML='';
  cad.layers.forEach((l,i)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='li'+(i===cad.aLayer?' active':'');
    const eye=document.createElement('button');eye.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-'+(l.vis?'eye':'eye-slash')+'"></i>';
    eye.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();l.vis=!l.vis;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers()};
    const dot=document.createElement('span');dot.className='ldot';dot.style.background=l.col;
    const nm=document.createElement('span');nm.className='ln';nm.style.color=l.col;nm.textContent=l.name+(i===cad.aLayer?' · activa':'');
    const del=document.createElement('button');del.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-trash"></i>';
    del.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(cad.layers.length>1){cad.layers.splice(i,1);cad.objs.forEach(o=>{if(o.layer>=cad.layers.length)o.layer=cad.layers.length-1});if(cad.aLayer>=cad.layers.length)cad.aLayer=cad.layers.length-1;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers();toast('Capa eliminada','info')}};
    d.appendChild(eye);d.appendChild(dot);d.appendChild(nm);d.appendChild(del);
    d.onclick=()=>{cad.aLayer=i;renderLayers()};
    c.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function addLayer(){
  const n=cad.layers.length;
  const cols=['#EAEAEA','#22C55E','#3B82F6','#FBBF24','#EF4444','#A855F7','#06B6D4','#EC4899','#FF6B2B','#84CC16'];
  cad.layers.push({id:n,name:'Capa '+n,vis:true,col:cols[n%cols.length],lock:false});
  cad.aLayer=n;renderLayers();toast('Capa '+n+' creada','success');
}

/* ===== UTILIDADES UI ===== */
let activeCat='all';
function renderCatChips(){
  const c=document.getElementById('cat-chips');c.innerHTML='';
  const all=document.createElement('button');all.className='cchip'+(activeCat==='all'?' active':'');all.textContent='Todas ('+U.length+')';
  all.onclick=()=>{activeCat='all';filterUtils()};c.appendChild(all);
  CATS.forEach(cat=>{
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='cchip'+(activeCat===cat.id?' active':'');
    b.innerHTML='<i class="fas '+cat.icon+'"></i> '+cat.name;
    b.onclick=()=>{activeCat=cat.id;filterUtils()};c.appendChild(b);
  });
}
function filterUtils(){
  const q=document.getElementById('usearch').value.toLowerCase();
  const g=document.getElementById('ugrid');g.innerHTML='';
  renderCatChips();
  let count=0;
  U.forEach(u=>{
    const cat=CATS[u[2]];
    if(activeCat!=='all'&&cat.id!==activeCat)return;
    if(q&&u[1].toLowerCase().indexOf(q)<0&&cat.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(q)<0)return;
    count++;
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='ucard';
    d.innerHTML='<i class="fas '+u[3]+'" style="color:'+cat.color+'"></i><span>'+u[1]+'</span>';
    d.onclick=()=>openUtil(u);
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
  document.getElementById('ucount').textContent=count+' utilidades';
}
function openUtil(u){
  document.getElementById('um-title').textContent=u[1];
  let h='';
  if(u[4].length>0){
    u[4].forEach(inp=>{
      const txt=inp[3]?true:false;
      h+='<div class="ci"><label>'+inp[1]+'</label><input type="'+(txt?'text':'number')+'" id="ui_'+inp[0]+'" step="any" '+(txt?'placeholder="..."':'placeholder="0"')+'><span class="unit">'+(inp[2]||'')+'</span></div>';
    });
    h+='<button class="cbtn" onclick="calcUtil('+U.indexOf(u)+')">Calcular</button>';
    h+='<div class="rbox" id="ures" style="display:none"></div>';
  }else{
    h='<p style="color:var(--fg3);text-align:center;padding:20px">Sin entradas</p>';
  }
  document.getElementById('um-body').innerHTML=h;
  openM('util-modal');
}
function calcUtil(idx){
  const u=U[idx],vals={};
  u[4].forEach(inp=>{
    const el=document.getElementById('ui_'+inp[0]);
    const raw=el?el.value:'';
    vals[inp[0]]=inp[3]?raw:(parseFloat(raw)||0);
  });
  try{
    const res=u[5](vals);
    let h='';
    for(const k of Object.keys(res)){
      h+='<div class="rrow"><span class="rl">'+k+'</span><span class="rv">'+res[k]+'</span></div>';
    }
    const box=document.getElementById('ures');box.innerHTML=h;box.style.display='block';
  }catch(e){
    const box=document.getElementById('ures');
    box.innerHTML='<div class="rrow"><span class="rl">Error</span><span class="rv">'+(e.message||'datos invalidos')+'</span></div>';
    box.style.display='block';
  }
}

/* ===== PREVIEWS MOBILIARIO/SIMBOLOS ===== */
function previewSVG(kind,isSym){
  const entry=(isSym?SYMS:FURN).find(f=>f[0]===kind);
  const w=entry?entry[2]:40,h=entry?entry[3]:40;
  const fakeCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');fakeCanvas.width=30;fakeCanvas.height=30;
  return null;
}
function renderFurn(){
  const g=document.getElementById('furn-g');g.innerHTML='';
  FURN.forEach(f=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='fbtn';
    d.innerHTML='<canvas class="fpv" width="60" height="60" data-kind="'+f[0]+'"></canvas>'+f[1];
    d.onclick=()=>cad.insertFurn(f[0],f[1],f[2],f[3]);
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#furn-g canvas').forEach(cv=>drawPreview(cv,cv.dataset.kind,false))});
}
function renderSyms(){
  const g=document.getElementById('sym-g');g.innerHTML='';
  SYMS.forEach(s=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='fbtn';
    d.innerHTML='<canvas class="fpv" width="60" height="60" data-kind="'+s[0]+'"></canvas>'+s[1];
    d.onclick=()=>cad.insertSym(s[0],s[1],s[2],s[3]);
    g.appendChild(d);
  });
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#sym-g canvas').forEach(cv=>drawPreview(cv,cv.dataset.kind,true))});
}
function drawPreview(cv,kind,isSym){
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,60,60);
  const entry=(isSym?SYMS:FURN).find(f=>f[0]===kind);if(!entry)return;
  const w=entry[2],h=entry[3],sc=Math.min(44/w,44/h);
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(30-w*sc/2,30-h*sc/2);ctx.scale(sc,sc);
  ctx.strokeStyle='#EAEAEA';ctx.fillStyle='#EAEAEA';ctx.lineWidth=1.5/sc;ctx.lineCap='round';
  const fake={kind,x:0,y:0,w,h};
  if(cad){const old=cad.x;cad.x=ctx;(isSym?cad.drawSym(fake):cad.drawFurn(fake));cad.x=old}else{ctx.strokeRect(0,0,w,h)}
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ===== EXPORTAR ===== */
function exportPNG(){
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.png';
  a.href=cad.c.toDataURL('image/png');a.click();toast('PNG exportado','success');
}
function exportJPG(){
  const tmp=document.createElement('canvas');tmp.width=cad.c.width;tmp.height=cad.c.height;
  const tc=tmp.getContext('2d');tc.fillStyle='#0C0C0C';tc.fillRect(0,0,tmp.width,tmp.height);tc.drawImage(cad.c,0,0);
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.jpg';
  a.href=tmp.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.9);a.click();toast('JPG exportado','success');
}
function exportSVG(){
  let svg='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="'+cad.W+'" height="'+cad.H+'" viewBox="'+(-cad.px/cad.zoom)+' '+(-cad.py/cad.zoom)+' '+(cad.W/cad.zoom)+' '+(cad.H/cad.zoom)+'" style="background:#0C0C0C">';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    const c=o.color||'#FFF',lw=o.lineWidth||2;
    switch(o.type){
      case'line':svg+='<line x1="'+o.x1+'" y1="'+o.y1+'" x2="'+o.x2+'" y2="'+o.y2+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+lw+'"/>';break;
      case'wall':svg+='<line x1="'+o.x1+'" y1="'+o.y1+'" x2="'+o.x2+'" y2="'+o.y2+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+o.thickness+'" opacity="0.35"/>';break;
      case'rect':svg+='<rect x="'+o.x+'" y="'+o.y+'" width="'+o.w+'" height="'+o.h+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+lw+'" fill="none"/>';break;
      case'circle':svg+='<circle cx="'+o.cx+'" cy="'+o.cy+'" r="'+o.r+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+lw+'" fill="none"/>';break;
      case'text':svg+='<text x="'+o.x+'" y="'+o.y+'" fill="'+c+'" font-size="'+o.size+'">'+o.content+'</text>';break;
      case'polyline':case'freehand':{let pts=o.points.map(p=>p.x+','+p.y).join(' ');svg+='<polyline points="'+pts+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+lw+'" fill="none"'+(o.closed?' /':'/')+'>';break}
      case'dimension':svg+='<line x1="'+o.x1+'" y1="'+o.y1+'" x2="'+o.x2+'" y2="'+o.y2+'" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="'+lw+'" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>';break;
    }
  });
  svg+='</svg>';
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.svg';a.href='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);a.click();toast('SVG exportado','success');
}
function exportJSON(){
  const data=JSON.stringify({app:'ArquiCAD Pro',version:2,objects:cad.objs,layers:cad.layers,activeLayer:cad.aLayer},null,2);
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-proyecto.json';a.href='data:application/json;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(data);a.click();toast('Proyecto guardado','success');
}
function importJSON(){document.getElementById('json-in').click()}
function handleJSONImport(e){
  const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;
  const r=new FileReader();r.onload=ev=>{
    try{
      const d=JSON.parse(ev.target.result);cad.objs=d.objects||[];
      if(d.layers&&d.layers.length)cad.layers=d.layers;
      cad.aLayer=d.activeLayer||0;if(cad.aLayer>=cad.layers.length)cad.aLayer=0;
      cad.selIdx=-1;cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers();toast('Proyecto cargado','success');
    }catch(err){toast('Archivo invalido','error')}
  };r.readAsText(f);e.target.value='';
}
function exportDXF(){
  let dxf='0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n';
  const ln=o=>'0\nLINE\n8\n'+(cad.layers[o.layer]?cad.layers[o.layer].name:'0')+'\n10\n'+o.x1+'\n20\n'+o.y1+'\n30\n0\n11\n'+o.x2+'\n21\n'+o.y2+'\n31\n0\n';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    switch(o.type){
      case'line':case'wall':dxf+=ln(o);break;
      case'circle':dxf+='0\nCIRCLE\n8\n'+(cad.layers[o.layer]?cad.layers[o.layer].name:'0')+'\n10\n'+o.cx+'\n20\n'+o.cy+'\n30\n0\n40\n'+o.r+'\n';break;
      case'rect':{const x2=o.x+o.w,y2=o.y+o.h;dxf+=ln({x1:o.x,y1:o.y,x2:x2,y2:o.y,layer:o.layer})+ln({x1:x2,y1:o.y,x2:x2,y2:y2,layer:o.layer})+ln({x1:x2,y1:y2,x2:o.x,y2:y2,layer:o.layer})+ln({x1:o.x,y1:y2,x2:o.x,y2:o.y,layer:o.layer});break}
      case'polyline':case'freehand':{for(let i=1;i<o.points.length;i++)dxf+=ln({x1:o.points[i-1].x,y1:o.points[i-1].y,x2:o.points[i].x,y2:o.points[i].y,layer:o.layer});if(o.closed&&o.points.length>2)dxf+=ln({x1:o.points[o.points.length-1].x,y1:o.points[o.points.length-1].y,x2:o.points[0].x,y2:o.points[0].y,layer:o.layer});break}
      case'text':dxf+='0\nTEXT\n8\n'+(cad.layers[o.layer]?cad.layers[o.layer].name:'0')+'\n10\n'+o.x+'\n20\n'+o.y+'\n30\n0\n40\n'+o.size+'\n1\n'+o.content+'\n';break;
    }
  });
  dxf+='0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n';
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.dxf';a.href='data:text/plain;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(dxf);a.click();toast('DXF exportado','success');
}
function exportCSV(){
  let csv='Tipo,Detalle,X1,Y1,X2,Y2,Color,Capa\n';
  cad.objs.forEach(o=>{
    const det=o.kind||o.content||'';
    csv+=[o.type,'"'+String(det).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"',o.x1!==undefined?o.x1:(o.x!==undefined?o.x:o.cx),o.y1!==undefined?o.y1:(o.y!==undefined?o.y:o.cy),o.x2!==undefined?o.x2:'',o.y2!==undefined?o.y2:'',o.color||'',cad.layers[o.layer]?cad.layers[o.layer].name:'0'].join(',')+'\n';
  });
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.download='arquicad-plano.csv';a.href='data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(csv);a.click();toast('CSV exportado','success');
}

/* ===== ESCANEAR ===== */
let scanImg=null;
function openScanner(){document.getElementById('sc-file').click()}
function loadScanFile(e){
  const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;
  const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{
    scanImg=img;const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv');
    const maxW=900,sc=Math.min(1,maxW/img.width);
    cv.width=Math.round(img.width*sc);cv.height=Math.round(img.height*sc);cv.style.display='block';
    cv.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    toast('Imagen cargada','success');
  };img.src=URL.createObjectURL(f);e.target.value='';
}
function applyScan(){
  if(!scanImg)return;const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv'),cx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const br=+document.getElementById('sc-br').value,co=+document.getElementById('sc-co').value,th=+document.getElementById('sc-th').value;
  cx.drawImage(scanImg,0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
  const id=cx.getImageData(0,0,cv.width,cv.height),d=id.data;
  const cf=(co+100)/100;
  for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
    let g=(d[i]*0.299+d[i+1]*0.587+d[i+2]*0.114);
    g=Math.min(255,Math.max(0,(g+br)*cf));
    const v2=g>th?255:0;d[i]=v2;d[i+1]=v2;d[i+2]=v2;
  }
  cx.putImageData(id,0,0);
}
function vectorizeScan(){
  if(!scanImg){toast('Carga una imagen primero','error');return}
  applyScan();
  const cv=document.getElementById('scan-cv'),cx=cv.getContext('2d');
  const id=cx.getImageData(0,0,cv.width,cv.height),d=id.data;
  cad.saveU();
  const wc=cad.s2w(cad.W/2,cad.H/2);
  const scale=1/cad.zoom;
  const ox=wc.x-cv.width/2*scale,oy=wc.y-cv.height/2*scale;
  let n=0;const maxL=4000;
  const lines=[];
  for(let y=0;y<cv.height;y+=3){
    for(let x=0;x<cv.width-1;x+=1){
      const i=(y*cv.width+x)*4,i2=i+4;
      if(d[i]>128&&d[i2]<128){lines.push([ox+x*scale,oy+y*scale]);n++;if(n>maxL)break}
    }
    if(n>maxL)break;
  }
  for(let x=0;x<cv.width;x+=3){
    for(let y=0;y<cv.height-1;y+=1){
      const i=(y*cv.width+x)*4,i2=((y+1)*cv.width+x)*4;
      if(d[i]>128&&d[i2]<128){lines.push([ox+x*scale,oy+y*scale]);n++;if(n>maxL)break}
    }
    if(n>maxL)break;
  }
  lines.forEach(p=>{cad.objs.push({type:'line',layer:cad.aLayer,x1:p[0],y1:p[1],x2:p[0]+2*scale,y2:p[1],color:'#22C55E',lineWidth:1})});
  cad.dirty=true;cad.render();
  toast('Vectorizacion: '+lines.length+' puntos','success');
}

/* ===== NUEVO PROYECTO ===== */
function newProject(){
  if(cad&&cad.objs.length&&!confirm('Crear proyecto nuevo? Se perderan cambios no guardados.'))return;
  if(cad){cad.objs=[];cad.selIdx=-1;cad.undoS=[];cad.redoS=[];cad.dirty=true;cad.render();renderLayers()}
  toast('Proyecto nuevo','info');
}

/* ===== COLOR PICKER SYNC ===== */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  document.getElementById('p-col').addEventListener('input',function(){if(cad)cad.sCol=this.value;document.getElementById('p-col-t').value=this.value});
  document.getElementById('p-col-t').addEventListener('input',function(){if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(this.value)){if(cad)cad.sCol=this.value;document.getElementById('p-col').value=this.value}});

  /* Splash */
  setTimeout(()=>{document.getElementById('splash').classList.add('hide')},1700);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    document.getElementById('app').classList.add('show');
    cad=new CAD(document.getElementById('cad'));
    cad.px=cad.W/2;cad.py=cad.H/2;
    cad.dirty=true;cad.render();
    renderLayers();renderCatChips();filterUtils();renderFurn();renderSyms();

    document.querySelectorAll('#toolstrip button[data-tool]').forEach(b=>{
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const t=b.dataset.tool;
        if(t==='furniture'){renderFurn();openM('furn-modal');return}
        if(t==='symbol'){renderSyms();openM('sym-modal');return}
        cad.setTool(t);
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#bnav button[data-panel]').forEach(b=>{
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>togglePanel(b.dataset.panel));
    });
    document.getElementById('usearch').addEventListener('input',filterUtils);
    document.querySelectorAll('.mover').forEach(m=>{
      m.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===m)m.classList.remove('open')});
    });
    toast('ArquiCAD Pro listo · '+U.length+' utilidades','success');
  },2100);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
